## 1980103
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ほう。伝説のフライパンを<line-break>探しているのですか…。<line-break>それで私を訪ねてきたのですね。<end>"
EN: "Oh? You're looking for the Legendary Frying Pan. So that's why you've come to see me.<end>"
KO: "호오. 전설의 프라이팬을 찾고 계십니까…. 그래서 저를 찾아오셨군요.<end>"

## 1980104
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "き、聞いたことないですよね。<line-break>そんな魔道器の話なんて…。<end>"
EN: "Y-you've never heard of it, have you? A magical artifact like that...<end>"
KO: "드, 들어 본 적 없죠. 그런 마도기에 관한 이야기는….<end>"

## 1980105
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いえ。ありますよ。<end>"
EN: "Actually, I have.<end>"
KO: "아니요. 있어요.<end>"

## 1980106
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

EN: "What?!<end>"
KO: "네!?<end>"

## 1980107
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "正確な名前は知りませんが、<line-break>エルフに古くから伝わる<line-break>魔道器があると聞いています。<end>"
EN: "I don't know its exact name, but I've heard of a magical artifact passed down among the Elves since ancient times.<end>"
KO: "정확한 이름은 모르지만 엘프에게 오래전부터 전해지는 마도기가 있다고 들었습니다.<end>"

## 1980108
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そ、それはどこにあるんですか！？<end>"
EN: "W-where is it?!<end>"
KO: "그, 그건 어디에 있습니까!?<end>"

## 1980109
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "とは言え、いつだったか<line-break>人間たちに奪われてしまい<line-break>以来、行方知れずらしいですがね。<end>"
EN: "That said, the humans apparently stole it at some point, and no one has known where it is since.<end>"
KO: "그렇다 해도 언젠가 인간들에게 빼앗긴 뒤로는 행방을 알 수 없다고 합니다만.<end>"

## 1980110
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうなんですか…。<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>…よかった。<end>"
EN: "Is that so...? Thank goodness.<end>"
KO: "그런가요…. …다행이다.<end>"

## 1980111
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "何か言いましたか？<end>"
EN: "Did you say something?<end>"
KO: "뭐라고 하셨나요?<end>"

## 1980113
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "言い伝えによると、<line-break>その魔道器の使い方を誤ったせいで、<line-break>一国が滅びたとあります。<line-break>城塞都市跡あたりに行けば<line-break>何か手がかりぐらいは<line-break>残っているかもしれません。<end>"
EN: "According to the legend, an entire nation was destroyed because the artifact was used improperly. There may still be some clue left near the Fortress City Ruins.<end>"
KO: "전승에 따르면 그 마도기를 잘못 사용한 탓에 한 나라가 멸망했다고 합니다. 성채도시 터쯤에 가면 무언가 단서라도 남아 있을지 모릅니다.<end>"

## 1980114
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "え…ええーっ！？<line-break>そ、そんな、まさか…。<end>"
EN: "Wh-what?! No, that can't be...<end>"
KO: "에… 에엣!? 그, 그럴 리가….<end>"

## 1980115
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どうしました？<line-break>お顔の色がすぐれないようですが。<end>"
EN: "What's wrong? You look rather pale.<end>"
KO: "무슨 일이죠? 안색이 좋지 않은 것 같은데요.<end>"

## 1980116
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だ、大丈夫です！<line-break>ただの寝不足ですから！<line-break>ご心配には及びません！<end>"
EN: "I-I'm fine! I just haven't had enough sleep! There's no need to worry about me!<end>"
KO: "괘, 괜찮습니다! 그냥 잠이 부족한 것뿐이에요! 걱정하지 마세요!<end>"

## 1980117
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それにしても驚きましたね。<line-break>伝説のフライパンのことを<line-break>オルファウスさんがご存じだったなんて。<line-break>旅を続けていると、私には<line-break>びっくりするようなことばかりです。<end>"
EN: "Still, what a surprise that Orphaus knew about the Legendary Frying Pan. The more I travel, the more astonishing things happen to me.<end>"
KO: "그래도 놀랐어요. 전설의 프라이팬을 오르파우스 씨가 알고 계셨다니. 여행을 계속하다 보니 저한텐 놀랄 일뿐이에요.<end>"

## 1980118
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やっぱり、すごいですね。<line-break><value:$28>さんは…。<line-break>　<line-break>どんなことが起こっても動じないし。<line-break>とっても頼もしくて…。<line-break>優しくて…。<line-break>だけど、ホント言うと…。<line-break>少し不安になることもあります。<end>"
EN: "You really are amazing, <value:$28>. No matter what happens, nothing ever seems to faze you. You're so dependable... and so kind. But to tell you the truth... sometimes I feel a little uneasy.<end>"
KO: "역시 대단하세요. <value:$28> 씨는…. 무슨 일이 일어나도 흔들리지 않고, 정말 든든하고…. 상냥하고…. 하지만 사실은…. 조금 불안해질 때도 있어요.<end>"

## 1980119
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どうして不安になるのか、<line-break>自分にもわからないんです。<line-break>　<line-break><value:$28>さんが、<line-break>強くなればなるほど、どんどん、<line-break>遠くに行ってしまうみたいで…。<line-break>こんな気持ち、初めてです。<end>"
EN: "I don't even know why I feel uneasy. <value:$28>, the stronger you become, the farther away you seem to drift... I've never felt this way before.<end>"
KO: "왜 불안해지는지 저도 모르겠어요. <value:$28> 씨가 강해지면 강해질수록 점점 더 멀리 가 버리는 것 같아서…. 이런 기분은 처음이에요.<end>"

## 1980120
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "変なことを言うんだな<end>"
EN: "That's a strange thing to say.<end>"
KO: "이상한 소리를 하는군.<end>"

## 1980121
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺は俺だよ。　変わったりしない<end>"
EN: "I'm still me. That's not going to change.<end>"
KO: "난 나야. 변하지 않아.<end>"

## 1980122
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そ、そうですよね…。<line-break>ごめんなさい。<line-break>変なこと言ってしまって…。<line-break>きっと私、長旅で疲れてるんです。<line-break>忘れてください…。<line-break>おやすみなさい。<end>"
EN: "Y-you're right, of course... I'm sorry. I shouldn't have said anything so strange. I must be tired from the long journey. Please, forget I said it... Good night.<end>"
KO: "그, 그렇죠… 미안해요. 이상한 말을 해 버렸네요… 아마 긴 여행에 지친 모양이에요. 잊어 주세요… 안녕히 주무세요.<end>"

## 1980123
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よかった…。<line-break>変わらないでくださいね。<line-break>このまま、ずっと。<line-break>私の胸の内にある、<line-break><value:$28>さんのまま。<end>"
EN: "I'm glad... Please don't ever change. Stay just as you are, always―the <value:$28> I hold in my heart.<end>"
KO: "다행이에요…. 변하지 말아 주세요. 이대로 계속, 제 마음속의 <value:$28>님 그대로.<end>"

## 1980124
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あっ、<value:$28>さん。<line-break>どうしたんですか、こんな夜に…。<end>"
EN: "Oh, <value:$28>. What brings you here this late at night...?<end>"
KO: "아, <value:$28>님. 이런 밤중에 무슨 일이세요…?<end>"

## 1980125
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "えっ？　伝説のフライパンの話ですか？<line-break>えーと、あの、その…。<end>"
EN: "What? You mean the Legendary Frying Pan? Um, well, you see...<end>"
KO: "네? 전설의 프라이팬 이야기인가요? 음, 그게, 저…<end>"

## 1980126
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お、思い出しました！<line-break>おばあちゃんに聞いた話だと…<line-break>　<line-break>使い方を誤って、<line-break>国を滅ぼしちゃった魔道器は<line-break>すんごく厳重に封印されたんだとか。<end>"
EN: "I-I remember now! My grandmother once told me about a magical artifact that destroyed a country when it was misused. Supposedly, it was sealed away very, very securely.<end>"
KO: "아, 생각났어요! 할머니께 들은 이야기로는… 사용법을 잘못해 나라를 멸망시킨 마도기는 엄청나게 엄중히 봉인됐다더군요.<end>"

## 1980127
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そこまで厳重に封印されたものを<line-break>探すのは難しいような気が…。<line-break>　<line-break>残念ですけど、ここはきっぱりと<line-break>あきらめて他の魔道器を…。<end>"
EN: "I can't imagine it would be easy to find something sealed away that securely... It's a shame, but perhaps you should give up on it once and for all and look for another magical artifact...<end>"
KO: "그렇게 엄중히 봉인된 걸 찾는 건 어려울 것 같은데…. 아쉽지만 여기선 깔끔하게 포기하고 다른 마도기를….<end>"

## 1980128
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…えっ？<line-break>その封印を解く方法ですか？<line-break>　<line-break>えーと、そ、そうですね。<line-break>確か、あったことはあったと<line-break>思うんですけど…その…。<end>"
EN: "...What? How to break the seal? Um, y-yes. I think there was a way, if I remember correctly, but... well...<end>"
KO: "…네? 그 봉인을 푸는 방법인가요? 음, 그, 글쎄요. 분명 있긴 했던 것 같은데… 그게….<end>"

## 1980129
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そ、そうです。呪文です！<line-break>何か特別な呪文を唱えないと<line-break>封印は破れないって言ってました。<end>"
EN: "Y-yes, that's it. An incantation! She said the seal couldn't be broken unless someone recited a special incantation.<end>"
KO: "마, 맞아요. 주문이에요! 특별한 주문을 외우지 않으면 봉인을 풀 수 없다고 했어요.<end>"

## 1980130
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "厨房からなんとも言えない香りと<line-break>不穏な空気がただよってきた。<end>"
EN: "An indescribable smell drifted from the kitchen, along with an ominous air.<end>"
KO: "부엌에서 뭐라 형용하기 힘든 냄새와 불길한 기운이 흘러나왔다.<end>"

## 1980131
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うまいかまずいか以前に<line-break>食えるブツかどうかが疑問。<line-break>生命の危機を感じる…。<end>"
EN: "Before even asking whether it tastes good or bad, the question is whether this stuff is edible at all. A sense of mortal danger sets in…<end>"
KO: "맛있고 맛없고 이전에 먹을 수 있는 물건인지부터 의문이야. 생명의 위기를 느껴….<end>"

## 1980132
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どうしても？<end>"
EN: "Do you really have to?<end>"
KO: "꼭 그래야 해?<end>"

## 1980133
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そ、そうですか…。<line-break>やっぱり、ここまで来たら<line-break>見つけたいですよね。<line-break>でも、そんな古い呪文なんて、<line-break>そうそう、わかるはずが…。<end>"
EN: "I-I see... Now that you've come this far, of course you want to find it. But surely no one would just happen to know an incantation that old...<end>"
KO: "그, 그런가요…. 역시 여기까지 왔으면 찾고 싶죠. 하지만 그렇게 오래된 주문 같은 건 쉽게 알 리가….<end>"

## 1980134
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "呪文に詳しい方を<line-break>ご存じなんですか？<line-break>　<line-break>古代遺跡を調べている<line-break>考古学者のエストさん…。<end>"
EN: "You know someone well versed in incantations? Est, the archaeologist studying ancient ruins...<end>"
KO: "주문에 밝은 분을 아세요? 고대 유적을 조사하는 고고학자 에스트 씨….<end>"

## 1980135
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "すみません。旅の疲れが<line-break>出たみたいで、少しめまいが…。<line-break>先に休ませてください。<end>"
EN: "I'm sorry. I think the journey's catching up with me. I'm feeling a little dizzy... Please let me turn in early.<end>"
KO: "죄송해요. 여행 피로가 몰려온 것 같아서 조금 어지럽네요…. 먼저 쉬게 해 주세요.<end>"

## 1980136
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これはこれは<value:$28>様。<line-break>今日はどういったご用件でしょうか？<end>"
EN: "Ah, <value:$28>. What brings you here today?<end>"
KO: "이런, <value:$28> 님. 오늘은 무슨 용건이신가요?<end>"

## 1980137
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "はい。エスト様でございますか？<line-break>確か今はアルノートゥンで、<line-break>研究をなさっておられるはずですが。<end>"
EN: "Certainly. Est, you say? I believe Est is currently in Alnortun, conducting research.<end>"
KO: "예. 에스트 님 말씀이십니까? 지금은 분명 알노툰에서 연구를 하고 계실 텐데요.<end>"

## 1980139
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "えっ？　僕に聞きたいことがあるの？<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>…伝説のフライパンだって？<line-break>驚いたな…。<line-break>ちょっと、この古文書を見てよ。<end>"
EN: "What? You have something you want to ask me? The Legendary Frying Pan? That's a surprise... Here, take a look at this ancient manuscript.<end>"
KO: "응? 나한테 물어볼 게 있어? …전설의 프라이팬이라고? 놀랍네…. 잠깐, 이 고문서를 봐.<end>"

## 1980140
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "伝説のフライパンだって？<line-break>…驚いたな。<line-break>ちょっと、この古文書を見てよ。<end>"
EN: "The Legendary Frying Pan? That's a surprise. Here, take a look at this ancient manuscript.<end>"
KO: "전설의 프라이팬이라고? …놀랍군. 잠깐 이 고문서를 봐.<end>"

## 1980141
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ほら、ここ。<line-break>このフライパンみたいな絵と、<line-break>この呪文…。<line-break>まさしく、その魔道器のことが<line-break>記されているんだよ。<end>"
EN: "See, right here. This picture that looks like a frying pan, and this incantation... They're describing that very magical artifact.<end>"
KO: "봐, 여기. 이 프라이팬 같은 그림과 이 주문…. 틀림없이 그 마도기에 대해 적혀 있어.<end>"

## 1980142
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "信じられない。<line-break>なんて僕は幸運なんだ。<line-break>　<line-break>古来より幾多の先人たちが<line-break>探していた伝説の魔道器…。<line-break>それを見つける手がかりがそろったんだ。<end>"
EN: "I can't believe it. What incredible luck! Countless generations have sought this legendary magical artifact since ancient times... and now we have all the clues we need to find it.<end>"
KO: "믿을 수 없어. 난 정말 운이 좋군. 예로부터 수많은 선인들이 찾던 전설의 마도기… 그것을 찾을 단서가 모두 모였어.<end>"

## 1980143
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ロストールに伝わっていた伝承。<line-break>古文書に記された呪文。<line-break>そして、城塞都市跡…。<end>"
EN: "The legend handed down in Rostorl. The incantation recorded in the ancient manuscript. And the Fortress City Ruins...<end>"
KO: "로스톨에 전해지던 전승. 고문서에 적힌 주문. 그리고 성채도시 터…<end>"

## 1980144
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "素晴らしいよ、<value:$28>！<line-break>僕も一緒に連れてってよ。<line-break>一生のお願い！<line-break>伝説の魔道器がよみがえる瞬間を、<line-break>僕はこの目に焼きつけたいんだ。<line-break>　<line-break>もし、イヤだって言われても、<line-break>僕は絶対についていくよ。いいね<end>"
EN: "This is wonderful, <value:$28>! Take me with you. I'm begging you! I want to see the moment that legendary magical artifact is restored with my own eyes. Even if you say no, I'm coming with you no matter what. Understood?<end>"
KO: "멋져, <value:$28>! 나도 같이 데려가 줘. 평생의 부탁이야! 전설의 마도기가 되살아나는 순간을 이 눈에 새기고 싶어. 싫다고 해도 난 반드시 따라갈 거야. 알겠지?<end>"

## 1980146
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よし、古文書に記されている場所は<line-break>間違いなくここだ。<end>"
EN: "All right. This is definitely the place described in the ancient text.<end>"
KO: "좋아, 고문서에 적힌 장소는 틀림없이 여기다.<end>"

## 1980147
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "じゃ、あとは僕に任せて。<line-break>みんな下がってて…。<end>"
EN: "Leave the rest to me. Everyone, stand back...<end>"
KO: "그럼 나머지는 내게 맡겨. 다들 물러나 있어….<end>"

## 1980149
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…変だな。<line-break>この呪文でいいはずなんだけど。<line-break>発音が微妙に違ったのかな…？<end>"
EN: "That's strange. This should be the right spell. Was my pronunciation slightly off...?<end>"
KO: "…이상하네. 이 주문이 맞을 텐데. 발음이 미묘하게 달랐나…?<end>"

## 1980150
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "きゃあーっ！<line-break><value:$28>さん！<line-break>あ、あれ…！<end>"
EN: "Aah! <value:$28>! Th-that...!<end>"
KO: "꺄악! <value:$28> 씨! 저, 저건…!<end>"

## 1980151
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ご…ごめんなさい、<line-break><value:$28>さん。<line-break>実は伝説のフライパンって…。<end>"
EN: "I-I'm sorry, <value:$28>. The truth is, the Legendary Frying Pan...<end>"
KO: "미… 미안해요, <value:$28> 씨. 사실 전설의 프라이팬은….<end>"

## 1980152
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "え！？<end>"
EN: "What?!<end>"
KO: "어!?<end>"

## 1980153
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "こ、これって…<line-break>もしかして…！<end>"
EN: "C-could this really be...?<end>"
KO: "이, 이건… 혹시…!<end>"

## 1980154
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "伝説の魔道器だ！<end>"
EN: "It's the legendary magical artifact!<end>"
KO: "전설의 마도기다!<end>"

## 1980155
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうか！　わかったぞ。<line-break>あの怪物は<line-break>魔道器の守護者だったんだ。<line-break>万が一、封印が解かれた時は、<line-break>侵入者を滅ぼすよう、<line-break>仕掛けられていたんだ。<end>"
EN: "Of course! Now I understand. That monster was the magical artifact's guardian. It had been set to destroy any intruders if the seal was ever broken.<end>"
KO: "그래! 알겠어. 그 괴물은 마도기의 수호자였던 거야. 만일 봉인이 풀렸을 때 침입자를 없애도록 만들어져 있었던 거야.<end>"

## 1980156
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "見てください、<value:$28>さん。<line-break>本当に…<line-break>本当にあったんです…。<end>"
EN: "Look, <value:$28>. It really... it really exists...<end>"
KO: "보세요, <value:$28> 씨. 정말… 정말 있었어요….<end>"

## 1980157
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わ、私…。私は…。<line-break>ひっく…。えっく…。<end>"
EN: "I-I... I... Hic... h-hic...<end>"
KO: "저, 저는…. 저는…. 흐윽…. 흑….<end>"

## 1980158
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あっ、<value:$28>さん…。<end>"
EN: "Oh, <value:$28>...<end>"
KO: "앗, <value:$28> 씨….<end>"

## 1980159
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ええ。そうですね。<line-break>私もエストさんから聞きました。<line-break>　<line-break>形こそ似ているけど、<line-break>あれはフライパンじゃないって。<end>"
EN: "Yes, that's right. Est told me too. It may look like one, but it isn't actually a frying pan.<end>"
KO: "네, 그렇네요. 저도 에스트 씨에게 들었습니다. 모양은 비슷하지만 저건 프라이팬이 아니라고요.<end>"

## 1980160
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "でも、発見できてよかった。<line-break>今、私…。<line-break>とっても解放された気分なんです。<end>"
EN: "Still, I'm glad we found it. I feel... so free now.<end>"
KO: "그래도 발견해서 다행이에요. 지금 저… 정말 해방된 기분이에요.<end>"

## 1980161
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "えっ？　どうしてって…それは…。<end>"
EN: "What? Why...? Well...<end>"
KO: "에? 왜냐니… 그건….<end>"

## 1980162
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ごめんなさい！<line-break>伝説のフライパンって、<line-break>全部、ウソだったんです。<end>"
EN: "I'm sorry! The whole thing about the Legendary Frying Pan was a lie.<end>"
KO: "미안해요! 전설의 프라이팬 이야기는 전부 거짓말이었어요.<end>"

## 1980163
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おばあちゃんから聞いたというのは<line-break>本当ですけど…。<line-break>　<line-break>でも、おばあちゃんの話は<line-break>幼かった私を楽しませるための<line-break>おとぎ話だって知ってたから…。<end>"
EN: "It was true that I heard the story from my grandmother. But I knew it was only a fairy tale she'd made up to entertain me when I was little...<end>"
KO: "할머니에게 들었다는 건사실이지만…. 하지만 할머니 이야기는어렸던 나를 즐겁게 해 주기 위한동화라는 걸 알고 있었으니까….<end>"

## 1980164
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私は自分のウソで自分を縛り、<line-break>そのあげくひとりで苦しんでいたんです。<line-break>ほんと、バカみたいですよね。<end>"
EN: "I trapped myself in my own lie, and ended up suffering all alone because of it. I must seem like such a fool.<end>"
KO: "저는 제 거짓말로 스스로를 옭아매고, 결국 혼자 괴로워하고 있었어요. 정말 바보 같죠.<end>"

## 1980165
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "でも、不思議と今は自分のついたウソに<line-break>後悔がないんです。<end>"
EN: "But strangely enough, I don't regret the lie now.<end>"
KO: "하지만 이상하게도 지금은 제가 한 거짓말을 후회하지 않아요.<end>"

## 1980166
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だって、そのお陰で<line-break><value:$28>さんと、<line-break>一緒に旅ができたんですから。<line-break>苦しんだけど、嬉しかったんです。<line-break>この思い出は、私の一生の宝物です。<line-break>ありがとうございましたっ！<end>"
EN: "Because thanks to that lie, I got to travel with you, <value:$28>. It was painful, but it made me happy too. I'll treasure these memories for the rest of my life. Thank you so much!<end>"
KO: "그 덕분에 <value:$28> 씨와 함께 여행할 수 있었으니까요. 힘들었지만 행복했어요. 이 추억은 제 평생의 보물이에요. 정말 고마웠어요!<end>"

## 1980167
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "でも、その旅ももう終わりですね…。<line-break>伝説のフライパンは見つかったし、<line-break>私が一緒にいる理由もありません。<end>"
EN: "But I suppose our journey is over now... We've found the Legendary Frying Pan, and I have no reason to stay with you anymore.<end>"
KO: "하지만 그 여행도 이제 끝이네요…. 전설의 프라이팬도 찾았고, 제가 함께 있을 이유도 없습니다.<end>"

## 1980168
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "もう少しだけご一緒したら、<line-break>私はロストールに帰ります。<line-break>それで…いいですよね？<end>"
EN: "I'll travel with you a little longer, then return to Rostorl. That's... all right with you, isn't it?<end>"
KO: "조금만 더 함께한 뒤 저는 로스톨로 돌아갈게요. 그래도… 괜찮죠?<end>"

## 1980169
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これからも一緒にいてほしい<end>"
EN: "I want you to stay with me.<end>"
KO: "앞으로도 함께 있어 줬으면 해<end>"

## 1980170
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そんなこと…<end>"
EN: "That's not...<end>"
KO: "그런 일은…<end>"

## 1980171
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いいんですか、そんなこと言って！？<line-break>信じちゃいますよ…。<line-break>私、バカだから…。<end>"
EN: "Are you sure you mean that?! Because I'll believe you... I'm foolish enough to...<end>"
KO: "그런 말을 해도 되는 거예요!? 믿어 버린다고요…. 저, 바보니까….<end>"

## 1980172
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、いいんです。<line-break>気を遣わないでください。<line-break>　<line-break><value:$28>さんと一緒に<line-break>ここまで来られただけでも<line-break>私は幸せ者ですから。<end>"
EN: "Oh, it's all right. You don't have to spare my feelings. I'm lucky just to have made it this far with you, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "아, 괜찮아요. 신경 쓰지 마세요. <value:$28>님과 함께 여기까지 올 수 있었던 것만으로도 저는 행복한 사람이니까요.<end>"

## 1980174
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それにしても、お前さんは<line-break>人間ができているねえ。<line-break>よく怒らないもんだ。<end>"
EN: "Still, you're remarkably understanding. I don't know how you keep from getting angry.<end>"
KO: "그나저나 자네는 참 인품이 좋군. 용케 화를 안 내는구먼.<end>"

## 1980175
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "怒るって、誰にです？<end>"
EN: "Angry? With whom?<end>"
KO: "화낸다니, 누구한테요?<end>"

## 1980176
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "決まってる。あいつに、だよ。<line-break>こんな可愛い娘さんを置いて、<line-break>大陸中をほっつき歩いてる、あいつ。<end>"
EN: "You know who. The one who left a lovely girl like you behind to go traipsing all over the continent.<end>"
KO: "뻔하지. 그 녀석한테 말이야. 이렇게 귀여운 딸을 두고 대륙을 싸돌아다니는 그 녀석.<end>"

## 1980177
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "しょうがないですよ。<line-break>あの人は自由気ままな風と同じです。<line-break>つなぎとめておくことはできません。<end>"
EN: "It can't be helped. That one's as free as the wind. There's no tying someone like that down.<end>"
KO: "어쩔 수 없어요. 그 사람은 자유로운 바람과 같으니까요. 붙잡아 둘 수는 없어요.<end>"

## 1980178
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ハッハッハッ。そりゃそうだ。<line-break>家の中におさまってるあいつの姿なんて<line-break>想像もつかねえからな。<end>"
EN: "Ha ha ha! True enough. I can't even picture that one staying put at home.<end>"
KO: "하하하, 그건 그렇지. 집 안에 얌전히 들어앉아 있는 그 녀석 모습은 상상도 안 되니까.<end>"

## 1980179
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私はただの港なだけで充分なんです。<line-break>冒険という荒々しい未知の航海から、<line-break>あの人が帰ってくるための港…。<end>"
EN: "I'm content just to be a harbor―a harbor to return to from the rough, uncharted voyage of adventure...<end>"
KO: "저는 그저 항구이면 충분해요. 모험이라는 거칠고 미지의 항해에서 그 사람이 돌아올 수 있는 항구….<end>"

## 1980180
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あーあ、ごちそうさん。<line-break>今度、あいつに会ったら、<line-break>俺が言っといてやるよ。<line-break>さっさと帰港しやがれ<line-break>このろくでなしってな。<end>"
EN: "Oh, spare me. Next time we meet, I'll pass on a message: “Get back to port already, you good-for-nothing.”<end>"
KO: "아아, 잘 먹었다. 다음에 그 녀석 만나면 내가 말해 둘게. 얼른 귀항하라고, 이 못난 놈아 하고.<end>"

## 1980181
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そろそろ戻ってくるんじゃないかって<line-break>思ってました。<line-break>なんとなくそんな気がして…。<end>"
EN: "I thought you might be coming back soon. I don't know why. I just had a feeling...<end>"
KO: "슬슬 돌아오실 때가 됐다고 생각했어요. 왠지 그런 느낌이 들어서….<end>"

## 1980182
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お帰りなさい、<value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "Welcome home, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "어서 와, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 1980183
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あっ、<value:$28>さん！　<line-break>　<line-break>あ、あの…。<line-break>先ほどはありがとうございました。<end>"
EN: "Oh, <value:$28>! Um... thank you for what you did earlier.<end>"
KO: "아, <value:$28>님! 저, 저기…. 아까는 고마웠어요.<end>"

## 1980184
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どうぞ、ゆっくりしていってください。<end>"
EN: "Please, stay and make yourself comfortable.<end>"
KO: "부디 느긋하게 쉬다 가세요.<end>"

## 1980185
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いらっしゃいませ、<line-break><value:$28>さん。<line-break>どうぞ、ゆっくりしていってください。<end>"
EN: "Welcome, <value:$28>. Please, stay and make yourself comfortable.<end>"
KO: "어서 오세요, <value:$28>님. 편히 쉬다 가세요.<end>"

## 1980186
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いらっしゃいませ！<line-break><value:$28>さん！<line-break>　<line-break>あ、もしかして私の料理を<line-break>食べに来てくれたんですか？<end>"
EN: "Welcome, <value:$28>! Oh, did you come to have some of my cooking?<end>"
KO: "어서 오세요! <value:$28>님! 아, 혹시 제 요리를 먹으러 와 주신 건가요?<end>"

## 1980187
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "へえ。いいですね。<line-break>世界のあちこちを旅しているなんて。<line-break>　<line-break>できることなら私も一緒に…。<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>あ、いえ。なんでもないです。<line-break>気になさらないでください。<end>"
EN: "Oh, that sounds wonderful. Traveling all over the world like that... If I could, I'd go with you... Oh! No, it's nothing. Please don't mind me.<end>"
KO: "헤에. 좋네요. 세계 곳곳을 여행하고 있다니. 가능하다면 저도 함께…. 아, 아니에요. 아무것도 아니에요. 신경 쓰지 마세요.<end>"

## 1980188
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "えっ？　今は闇の神器を<line-break>探している…？<line-break>それってスゴいものなんですか？<end>"
EN: "What? You're looking for a Dark Artifact now...? Is it something really amazing?<end>"
KO: "네? 지금은 어둠의 신기를 찾고 있다고요…? 그게 그렇게 대단한 물건인가요?<end>"

## 1980189
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、えーと…。<line-break>………………。<end>"
EN: "Oh, um...<end>"
KO: "아, 음…. ……………….<end>"

## 1980190
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どうしよう…。<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>この機会を逃したら<line-break>もう<value:$28>さんとは<line-break>会えないかも…。<end>"
EN: "What should I do...? If I let this chance slip away, I may never see <value:$28> again...<end>"
KO: "어쩌지…. 이 기회를 놓치면 이제 <value:$28>님과 못 만날지도 몰라….<end>"

## 1980191
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>さん…。<line-break>もしかして、私を冒険の旅に<line-break>加えていただけるんですか？<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>... Does this mean you'll let me join you on your adventures?<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>님…. 혹시 저도 모험 여행에 데려가 주시는 건가요?<end>"

## 1980192
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ど、どうしよう…？<line-break>なんとかつじつまを合わせなきゃ…。<line-break>　<line-break>………………！<end>"
EN: "Wh-what am I going to do...? I have to make the story fit somehow... Wait!<end>"
KO: "어, 어떡하지…? 어떻게든 말이 되게 맞춰야 해…. ………………!<end>"

## 1980193
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "エルフとドワーフが協力して作った<line-break>魔道器とな？<end>"
EN: "A magical artifact made by Elves and Dwarves working together, you say?<end>"
KO: "엘프와 드워프가 협력해 만든 마도기라고?<end>"

## 1980194
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ど、どうしよう…？<line-break>私のついたウソがバレちゃう…。<line-break>　<line-break>せめて、自分から先に<line-break>ごめんなさいって謝ったほうが…。<end>"
EN: "Wh-what should I do...? My lie's going to be exposed... Maybe I should apologize first...<end>"
KO: "어, 어떡하지…? 내가 한 거짓말이 들통나겠어…. 차라리 먼저 내가 미안하다고 사과하는 게….<end>"

## 1980195
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "むっ。待てよ…。<line-break>もしかすると、あれか？<end>"
EN: "Hm. Wait... Could it be that?<end>"
KO: "음. 잠깐…. 혹시 그건가?<end>"

## 1980196
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ええっ！<end>"
EN: "What?!<end>"
KO: "에엣!<end>"

## 1980197
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そ、それって本当なんですか！？<end>"
EN: "I-is that really true?!<end>"
KO: "그, 그게 정말인가요!?<end>"

## 1980198
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そ、そうですか…。<end>"
EN: "I-I see...<end>"
KO: "그, 그런가요….<end>"

## 1980199
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "（どうしよう…。まさか<line-break>　こんなことになっちゃうなんて…。）<line-break>　<line-break>（このまま<value:$28>さんが、<line-break>　伝説のフライパンを探し続けたら、<line-break>　私のウソがバレて嫌われちゃう…。）<line-break>（ああん！　どうしたらいいの…！？）<end>"
EN: "(What am I going to do...? I never thought it would turn into this.) (If <value:$28> keeps searching for the Legendary Frying Pan, my lie will come out... and I'll be hated.) (Oh, what am I supposed to do?!)<end>"
KO: "(어쩌지…. 설마 이렇게 될 줄이야….) (이대로 <value:$28> 씨가 전설의 프라이팬을 계속 찾으면 내 거짓말이 들켜서 미움받을 거야….) (아앙! 어떡하면 좋아…!?)<end>"

## 1980200
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ど、どうしよう…。<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>なんとかして、それとなく<line-break><value:$28>さんに<line-break>あきらめてもらわないと…。<end>"
EN: "W-what am I going to do...? I have to find some way to get <value:$28> to give up without making it obvious...<end>"
KO: "어, 어떡하지…. 어떻게든 눈치채지 못하게 <value:$28>님이 포기하도록 해야 해….<end>"

## 1980201
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "でも、とても古い話なので、<line-break>もうその賢者様もとっくに<line-break>亡くなっているでしょうし…。<line-break>残念ですけど、ちょっと<line-break>探しようがないですよね？<line-break>ですよねっ！？<end>"
EN: "But that was such a long time ago. The sage must be long dead by now... It's a shame, but there's no way we could find the sage now, is there? There isn't, right!?<end>"
KO: "하지만 아주 오래된 이야기라 그 현자님도 이미 돌아가셨을 테고…. 아쉽지만 찾을 방법이 없겠죠? 그렇죠!?<end>"

## 1980202
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そ、そんなはずは…。<line-break>だってあれは…おばあちゃんから<line-break>聞いた根も葉もないおとぎ話…。<line-break>それこそ、探したって絶対に<line-break>見つかるはずが…。<end>"
EN: "Th-that can't be... It was just some ridiculous fairy tale my grandmother told me. No matter how hard anyone looked, it could never be found...<end>"
KO: "그, 그럴 리가…. 그건… 할머니께 들은 근거 없는 동화일 뿐인데…. 찾아도 절대 발견될 리가….<end>"

## 1980203
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いえ、そうとも限りませんよ。<line-break>わずかですが手がかりはあります。<end>"
EN: "Not necessarily. There is one small lead.<end>"
KO: "아니요, 꼭 그렇지만은 않습니다. 아주 조금이지만 단서는 있습니다.<end>"

## 1980204
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "えっ？　それって<line-break>どういう意味ですか？<end>"
EN: "What? What do you mean?<end>"
KO: "네? 그게 무슨 뜻인가요?<end>"

## 1980205
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "その魔道器…<line-break>見つかるといいですね、<line-break><value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "I hope you find that artifact, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "그 마도기… 찾을 수 있으면 좋겠네요, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 1980206
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いらっしゃい、<value:$28>。<line-break>今日はどうしたんですか？<end>"
EN: "Welcome, <value:$28>. What brings you here today?<end>"
KO: "어서 와요, <value:$28>. 오늘은 무슨 일이에요?<end>"

## 1980207
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ほう。伝説のフライパンを<line-break>探しているのですか…。<line-break>それで私を訪ねてきたのですね。<end>"
EN: "Oh? You're looking for the Legendary Frying Pan. So that's why you've come to see me.<end>"
KO: "호오. 전설의 프라이팬을 찾고 계십니까…. 그래서 저를 찾아오셨군요.<end>"

## 1980208
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

KO: "드, 들어 본 적 없죠. 그런 마도기에 관한 이야기는….<end>"

## 1980209

## 1980211

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1980212

## 1980213
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1980214

## 1980219
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "言い伝えによると、<line-break>その魔道器の使い方を誤ったせいで、<line-break>一国が滅びたとあります。<line-break>城塞都市跡あたりに行けば<line-break>何か手がかりぐらいは<line-break>残っているかもしれません。<end>"
EN: "According to the legend, an entire nation was destroyed because the artifact was used improperly. There may still be some clue left near the Fortress City Ruins.<end>"
KO: "전승에 따르면 그 마도기를 잘못 사용한 탓에 한 나라가 멸망했다고 합니다. 성채도시 터쯤에 가면 무언가 단서라도 남아 있을지 모릅니다.<end>"

## 1980220
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1980221
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1980222
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だ、大丈夫です。<line-break>ただの寝不足ですから。<line-break>ご心配には及びません。<end>"
EN: "I-I'm fine. I just haven't had enough sleep. There's no need to worry about me.<end>"
KO: "괘, 괜찮아요. 그냥 잠이 부족한 것뿐이에요. 걱정하지 마세요.<end>"

## 1980223

## 1980224
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どうしよう…。<line-break>何とかして<value:$28>さんに<line-break>あきらめてもらわないと…。<end>"

KO: "어쩌지…. 어떻게든 <value:$28> 씨가 포기하게 해야 해….<end>"

## 1980225
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "すごく古くて特別な呪文ですから、<line-break>知っている人だって<line-break>今はいるかどうか…。<end>"
EN: "It's a very old and very special incantation. I doubt anyone who knows it is still around...<end>"
KO: "아주 오래되고 특별한 주문이라서 지금 아는 사람이 있을지도 모르겠어요….<end>"

## 1980226
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そんな面倒な魔道器のことは<line-break>もう忘れちゃって、<line-break>何か他の物を探してみては…。<end>"
EN: "Why not forget all about that troublesome magical artifact and look for something else...?<end>"
KO: "그렇게 번거로운 마도기 이야기는 이제 잊고 다른 걸 찾아보는 게 어때요….<end>"

## 1980227
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "（終わった…。ううう…。<line-break>　そんな専門家に尋ねたら<line-break>　私のウソなんて見抜かれちゃう…。）<line-break>（そうなったら、もう<line-break>　<value:$28>さんの<line-break>　おそばにいられない…。）<end>"
EN: "(I'm finished... Oh no... If you ask an expert like that, my lie will be exposed...) (And then I won't be able to stay by <value:$28>'s side anymore...)<end>"
KO: "(끝났다…. 으으…. 그런 전문가에게 물어보면 내 거짓말 같은 건 들켜 버릴 거야….) (그러면 이제 <value:$28> 씨 곁에 있을 수 없어….)<end>"

## 1980228
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うまいかまずいか以前に<line-break>食えるブツかどうかが疑問。<line-break>生命の危機を感じる…。<end>"
EN: "Before even asking whether it tastes good or bad, the question is whether this stuff is edible at all. A sense of mortal danger sets in…<end>"
KO: "맛있고 맛없고 이전에 먹을 수 있는 물건인지부터 의문이야. 생명의 위기를 느껴….<end>"

## 1980229
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "で、ですから、伝説のフライパンは<line-break>ドワーフの技術とエルフの魔力によって<line-break>強力無比な破壊力を秘めているとか。<end>"
EN: "S-so, they say the Legendary Frying Pan combines Dwarven craftsmanship with Elven magic, giving it unrivaled destructive power.<end>"
KO: "그, 그러니까 전설의 프라이팬은 드워프의 기술과 엘프의 마력으로 비할 데 없이 강력한 파괴력을 지니고 있다던가요.<end>"

## 1980230
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "で、でもよく考えてみると<line-break>変な話ですよね？<line-break>　<line-break>昔から仲の悪いエルフとドワーフが<line-break>協力して作った魔道器なんて…。<end>"
EN: "B-but when you really think about it, it does sound strange, doesn't it? A magical artifact made by Elves and Dwarves working together, when they've always been at odds...<end>"
KO: "그, 그런데 잘 생각해 보면 이상한 이야기죠? 예전부터 사이가 나쁜 엘프와 드워프가 협력해서 만든 마도기라니….<end>"

## 1980231
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そ、そうですよね…。<line-break>おかしな話だって<line-break><value:$28>さんも思いますよね。<line-break>（よかった…。<line-break>　これでもうウソをつかなくてすむ…。）<line-break>　<line-break>（このまま<value:$28>さんが<line-break>　この話を忘れてくれたら、<line-break>　ずっと一緒に旅をしていられる…。）<end>"
EN: "Y-yes, exactly... You think it sounds strange too, don't you, <value:$28>? (Thank goodness... Now I won't have to keep lying...) (If <value:$28> will just forget all about it, we can keep traveling together...)<end>"
KO: "그, 그렇죠…. 이상한 이야기라고 <value:$28>님도 생각하시죠. (다행이다…. 이제 더는 거짓말하지 않아도 돼….) (이대로 <value:$28>님이 이 이야기를 잊어 주신다면 계속 함께 여행할 수 있어….)<end>"

## 1980232
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そ、そうですか…。<end>"
EN: "I-is that so...?<end>"
KO: "그, 그런가요….<end>"

## 1980233
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "すみません…。<line-break>一晩中、星空をながめていたから<line-break>ちょっと寝不足ぎみで…。<end>"
EN: "I'm sorry... I stayed up all night watching the stars, so I'm just a little short on sleep...<end>"
KO: "죄송해요…. 밤새 별하늘을 보고 있었더니 조금 잠이 부족해서….<end>"

## 1980234
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だ、大丈夫です！<line-break>皆さんの足手まといにならないよう<line-break>なんとかがんばりますから…。<line-break>さ、行きましょう、<line-break><value:$28>さん。<end>"
EN: "I-I'm all right! I'll do my best not to slow anyone down... Come on, let's go, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "괜, 괜찮아요! 여러분께 짐이 되지 않도록 어떻게든 힘낼게요…. 자, 가요, <value:$28>님.<end>"

## 1980235
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "変なことを言うんだな<end>"
EN: "That's a strange thing to say.<end>"
KO: "이상한 소리를 하는군.<end>"

## 1980236
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私は私よ。　変わったりしない<end>"
EN: "I'm still me. That's not going to change.<end>"
KO: "나는 나야. 변하지 않아.<end>"

## 1980237
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そ、そうですよね…。<line-break>ごめんなさい。<line-break>変なこと言ってしまって…。<line-break>きっと私、長旅で疲れてるんです。<line-break>忘れてください…。<line-break>おやすみなさい。<end>"
EN: "Y-you're right, of course... I'm sorry. I shouldn't have said anything so strange. I must be tired from the long journey. Please, forget I said it... Good night.<end>"
KO: "그, 그렇죠… 미안해요. 이상한 말을 해 버렸네요… 아마 긴 여행에 지친 모양이에요. 잊어 주세요… 안녕히 주무세요.<end>"

## 1980238
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よかった…。<line-break>変わらないでくださいね。<line-break>このまま、ずっと。<line-break>私の胸の内にある、<line-break><value:$28>さんのまま。<end>"
EN: "I'm glad... Please don't ever change. Stay just as you are, always―the <value:$28> I hold in my heart.<end>"
KO: "다행이에요…. 변하지 말아 주세요. 이대로 계속, 제 마음속의 <value:$28>님 그대로.<end>"

## 1980239
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "すみません、<value:$28>さん。<line-break>私、あの人ちょっと苦手なんで<line-break>隠れてますね。<end>"
EN: "Sorry, <value:$28>. I'm not very comfortable around that man, so I'm going to hide.<end>"
KO: "미안해요, <value:$28>님. 저, 그 사람은 좀 어려워서 숨어 있을게요.<end>"

## 1980240
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今はそれどころじゃないって…。<line-break><value:$28>さんは<line-break>私のことをどう思って…。<end>"
EN: "I said we don't have time for this... What does <value:$28> really think of me...?<end>"
KO: "지금은 그럴 때가 아니라니까…. <value:$28>님은 나를 어떻게 생각하고….<end>"

## 1980241
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、いえ。気にしていません…。<line-break>大丈夫です…。<line-break>　<line-break><value:$28>さんは考えがあって<line-break>あの場はそう言ったのだと<line-break>信じていますから…。<end>"
EN: "Oh, no. It doesn't bother me... I'm all right. I trust that <value:$28> had a reason for saying that back there.<end>"
KO: "아, 아니요. 신경 쓰지 않아요… 괜찮아요… <value:$28> 씨에게는 생각이 있어서 그 자리에서 그렇게 말한 거라고 믿으니까요…<end>"

## 1980242
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>さん…。<line-break>あの人は？<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>... What about that man?<end>"
KO: "<value:$28> 씨…. 그 사람은?<end>"

## 1980243
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "じゃあ、<line-break>あきらめてくれたんですね。<line-break>よかった…。<line-break><value:$28>さんのお陰です。<line-break>ありがとうございます。<end>"
EN: "Then he finally gave up. Thank goodness... It's all thanks to you, <value:$28>. Thank you.<end>"
KO: "그럼 포기해 주신 거네요. 다행이다…. <value:$28> 씨 덕분이에요. 감사합니다.<end>"

## 1980244
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>さん！<line-break>大丈夫ですか！？<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>! Are you all right?!<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>님! 괜찮으세요!?<end>"

## 1980245
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>さんは<line-break>やっばり優しいんですね、<line-break>わざと負けてあげるなんて。<line-break>私、うれしいです、<line-break>そんな<value:$28>さんと<line-break>一緒に旅ができたことが…。<end>"
EN: "You really are kind, <value:$28>. You lost on purpose for him. I'm so glad... that I got to travel with someone like you, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28> 씨는 역시 상냥하시네요, 일부러 져 주시다니. 저, 기뻐요. 그런 <value:$28> 씨와 함께 여행할 수 있어서….<end>"

## 1980246
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "伝説のフライパンを手に入れた。<end>"
EN: "Obtained the Legendary Frying Pan.<end>"
KO: "전설의 프라이팬을 손에 넣었다.<end>"

## 1980247
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フェルムメッセージ終了<end>"
EN: ""
KO: "페름 메시지 종료<end>"

## 1990000

## 1990001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec199-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>か…。<line-break>俺もヒマだし、せっかくだから<line-break>たまには身の上話でもしてやろう。<line-break>俺はこれでも昔、先生をやっていてな。<line-break>結構、優秀な生徒たちを育てたもんさ。<end>"
EN: "Well, <value:$28>... I've got time to kill, so I may as well tell you a little about myself for once. Believe it or not, I used to be a teacher. Trained some remarkably talented students, too.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>인가…. 나도 한가하니 모처럼 신세 이야기나 해 주지. 이래 봬도 옛날엔 선생을 했어. 꽤 우수한 제자들을 길렀지.<end>"

## 1990002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec199-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "えっ？　いつの話かだって？<line-break>そんなの俺が天界にいた頃に<line-break>決まってるじゃないか。<end>"
EN: "What? When was this? Why, back when I lived in the heavenly realm, of course.<end>"
KO: "뭐? 언제 이야기냐고? 당연히 내가 천계에 있었을 때지.<end>"

## 1990003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec199-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いいんだぜ…。<line-break>べつに無理に信じなくても。<line-break>　<line-break>まあ、続きが聞きたくなったら<line-break>また来な。<line-break>気が向いたら話してやるよ。<end>"
EN: "That's fine. You don't have to believe me. Well, come back if you ever want to hear the rest. I might tell you, if the mood takes me.<end>"
KO: "괜찮아…. 굳이 믿지 않아도 돼. 뭐, 뒷이야기가 듣고 싶어지면 또 와. 내키면 이야기해 줄게.<end>"

## 1990004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec199-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "PSP用不良少年オルファウスイベント01用<end>"
EN: ""
KO: "PSP용 불량소년 오르파우스 이벤트01용<end>"

## 2000000
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec200-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "PSP用不良少年オルファウスイベント02用<end>"
EN: ""
KO: "PSP용 불량소년 오르파우스 이벤트02용<end>"

## 2000001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec200-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>か…。<line-break>俺もヒマだし、せっかくだから<line-break>少し話につきあえよ。<line-break>その昔、俺が天界で<line-break>先生をやっていたことは<line-break>以前、話したよな？<end>"
EN: "Ah, <value:$28>. I've got time to kill, so why don't you keep me company for a while? I've told you before that I used to teach in the heavens, haven't I?<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>인가… 나도 한가하니 잠깐 이야기나 하자. 옛날 내가 천계에서 선생을 했다는 건 전에 말했지?<end>"

## 2000002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec200-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺の博識ぶりは、つとに有名でな。<line-break>神々から尊敬されたもんだった。<line-break>むろん生徒たちからも、な。<line-break>だけど、どういう巡り合わせか<line-break>生徒の中にひとり、<line-break>とんでもねえワルがいたんだよ。<line-break>そいつには俺もずいぶんと<line-break>手を焼いたものさ。<end>"
EN: "I was famous for my vast learning, you know. The gods respected me. My students did too, of course. But by some twist of fate, one of them was an unbelievable rogue. Gave me no end of trouble, that one.<end>"
KO: "내 박식함은 아주 유명했지. 신들에게도 존경받았고, 물론 학생들에게도 말이야. 그런데 무슨 운명의 장난인지 학생 중에 하나, 정말 터무니없는 악동이 있었어. 그 녀석 때문에 나도 꽤 골머리를 앓았지.<end>"

## 2000003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec200-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おっ、噂をすれば影だ。<line-break>なあ、オルファウス。<line-break>　<line-break>俺が天界で先生をやってた頃、<line-break>とんでもねえワルがいたんだが<line-break>覚えてるか？<end>"
EN: "Well, look who just showed up. Tell me, Orphaus. Do you remember that unbelievable rogue I had for a student when I taught in the heavens?<end>"
KO: "오, 호랑이도 제 말 하면 온다더니. 이봐, 올파우스. 내가 천계에서 선생 노릇하던 시절에 엄청난 악당 하나 있었는데 기억나?<end>"

## 2000004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec200-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さあ、いったいどなたの話ですか、<line-break>先生？<end>"
EN: "I can't imagine whom you mean, Teacher.<end>"
KO: "자, 대체 누구 이야기인가요, 선생님?<end>"

## 2000005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec200-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今は大賢者様と呼ばれている<line-break>誰かさんのことだよ。<end>"
EN: "A certain someone now known as the Great Sage.<end>"
KO: "지금은 대현자님이라고 불리는 어느 분 얘기야.<end>"

## 2000006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec200-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ああ、そうでしたか。<line-break>私はてっきり別の人の話かと。<line-break>　<line-break>ふふっ。言われてみると、<line-break>そんな時代もありましたね。<end>"
EN: "Ah, I see. I was certain you meant someone else. Heh. Now that you mention it, there was a time like that.<end>"
KO: "아, 그랬군요. 저는 완전히 다른 사람 이야기인 줄 알았습니다. 후후, 듣고 보니 그런 시절도 있었네요.<end>"

## 2000007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec200-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ったく、しれっとした顔で<line-break>すっトボケようとしやがって。<line-break>　<line-break>いずれ、お前の悪行を<line-break>全部<value:$28>に<line-break>バラしてやるからな。<end>"
EN: "Damn it, you're trying to play dumb with a straight face. One of these days, I'm going to tell <value:$28> about every rotten thing you ever did.<end>"
KO: "정말, 아무렇지도 않은 얼굴로 시치미 떼려고 하네. 언젠가 네 악행을 전부 <value:$28>에게 폭로해 줄 거야.<end>"

## 2000008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec200-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "はいはい。どうぞご自由に。<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>さて、ネモがもっと悪い記憶を<line-break>掘り起こさないうちに<line-break>退散するとしましょうか。<end>"
EN: "Yes, yes. Be my guest. Now then, I think I'll make my exit before Nemo dredges up any worse memories.<end>"
KO: "네네. 마음대로 하세요. 자, 네모가 더 나쁜 기억을 끄집어내기 전에 저는 물러나 볼까요.<end>"

## 2000009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec200-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "PSP用不良少年オルファウスイベント02用<end>"
EN: ""
KO: "PSP용 불량소년 오르파우스 이벤트02용<end>"

## 2010000
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec201-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "PSP用不良少年オルファウスイベント03_1用<end>"
EN: ""
KO: "PSP용 불량소년 오르파우스 이벤트03_1용<end>"

## 2010001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec201-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>か…。<line-break>俺もヒマだし、せっかくだから<line-break>少し話につきあえよ。<line-break>俺が昔、天界で先生をやっていた頃、<line-break>オルファウスが問題児だったってことは<line-break>以前、話したよな？<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, huh? Well, I've got time to kill, so you might as well keep me company for a while. I told you before that Orphaus was a problem student back when I taught in the heavens, right?<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>인가…. 나도 한가하니 모처럼 조금 이야기나 해 줘. 내가 옛날 천계에서 선생을 하던 시절 오르파우스가 문제아였다는 건 전에 말했지?<end>"

## 2010002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec201-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "くしゃみが出たんで、<line-break>もしやと思ったんですが、<line-break>また私の話ですか、ネモ？<end>"
EN: "I sneezed, so I had a feeling... Are you talking about me again, Nemo?<end>"
KO: "재채기가 나와서 혹시나 했는데, 또 제 이야기입니까, 네모?<end>"

## 2010003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec201-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おう。お前がどれだけ手のかかる<line-break>問題児だったか、<value:$28>に<line-break>聞かせていたんだ。<end>"
EN: "Yeah. I was telling <value:$28> just how much trouble you were as a student.<end>"
KO: "그래. 네가 얼마나 손이 많이 가는 문제아였는지 <value:$28>에게 들려주고 있었지.<end>"

## 2010004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec201-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それでよ。オルファウスの奴は、<line-break>俺の授業で、とんでもない悪さを<line-break>しやがってな。<line-break>こともあろうに俺の後頭部に<line-break>ファイアの呪文をぶつけやがったんだ。<line-break>なあ、オルファウス？<end>"
EN: "Anyway, Orphaus pulled one hell of a stunt in my class. He actually hit me in the back of the head with a fire spell! Isn't that right, Orphaus?<end>"
KO: "그래서 말이지. 오르파우스 녀석이 내 수업에서 엄청난 장난을 쳤어. 하필이면 내 뒤통수에 파이어 주문을 날렸다고. 그렇지, 오르파우스?<end>"

## 2010005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec201-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "少々、誤解が生じているようですね、<line-break>先生。<line-break>　<line-break>あれはネモを狙ったわけではなく、<line-break>私の前に座っていた生徒に<line-break>向けたものだったのですよ。<line-break>そこにひょっこりとネモが<line-break>現れたものですから<line-break>私としては不可抗力でしょう？<end>"
EN: "There seems to be a slight misunderstanding, Professor. I wasn't aiming at Nemo. The spell was meant for the student sitting in front of me. Nemo simply popped up at the wrong moment, so it was hardly my fault, was it?<end>"
KO: "조금 오해가 있으신 듯하군요, 선생님. 그건 네모를 노린 게 아니라 제 앞에 앉아 있던 학생을 향한 것이었습니다. 그런데 네모가 갑자기 나타나는 바람에 저로서는 불가항력이었지요?<end>"

## 2010006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec201-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "じゃあ、俺はとばっちりで、<line-break>火傷したってのか！？<line-break>ひでえ！　なんて生徒だ…。<end>"
EN: "So I got burned just because I happened to be in the way?! That's awful! What kind of student does that...?<end>"
KO: "그럼 난 애꿎게 휘말려서 화상 입은 거야!? 너무하잖아! 대체 무슨 학생이야….<end>"

## 2010007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec201-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "きっと、その日のネモの運勢が<line-break>あまりよくなかったんですよ。<line-break>　<line-break>そういう運の悪い日は<line-break>誰にでもありますから。<end>"
EN: "Nemo's luck must have been rather poor that day. Everyone has unlucky days now and then.<end>"
KO: "분명 그날 네모의 운세가 별로 좋지 않았던 거예요. 누구에게나 그런 운 나쁜 날은 있으니까요.<end>"

## 2010008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec201-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ちぇっ。こんな奴が大賢者様と<line-break>呼ばれているんだから世も末だぜ。<line-break>やってらんねーや。<end>"
EN: "Tch. The world's gone to hell if someone like this gets called the Great Sage. I've had enough.<end>"
KO: "쳇. 이런 녀석이 대현자님이라고 불리니 세상도 끝이군. 못 해먹겠어.<end>"

## 2010009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec201-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さて、ネモがもっと悪い記憶を<line-break>掘り起こさないうちに<line-break>退散するとしましょうか。<end>"
EN: "Well, I think I'll take my leave before Nemo dredges up any worse memories.<end>"
KO: "자, 네모가 더 나쁜 기억을 파헤치기 전에 이만 물러나도록 할까.<end>"

## 2010010
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec201-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふうっ。<line-break>なんだか話す気が失せちまった。<line-break>　<line-break>続きはまた今度、<line-break>俺様の機嫌がいい時にしてやるよ。<end>"
EN: "Whew. I don't feel like talking anymore. I'll let you hear the rest another time, when I'm in a better mood.<end>"
KO: "후우. 왠지 이야기할 마음이 사라졌군. 나머지는 다음에, 내 기분이 좋을 때 해 주지.<end>"

## 2010011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec201-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "PSP用不良少年オルファウスイベント03_1用<end>"
EN: ""
KO: "PSP용 불량소년 오르파우스 이벤트03_1용<end>"

## 2020000
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec202-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "PSP用不良少年オルファウスイベント03_2用<end>"
EN: ""
KO: "PSP용 불량소년 오르파우스 이벤트03_2용<end>"

## 2020001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec202-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ええ。私がネモに火傷をさせたのは<line-break>事実ですよ。<line-break>ただ……。<line-break>真相は少しだけ異なります。<end>"
EN: "Yes. It's true that I burned Nemo. But... what actually happened was a little different.<end>"
KO: "네. 제가 네모에게 화상을 입힌 건 사실입니다. 다만…. 진상은 조금 다릅니다.<end>"

## 2020002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec202-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "じつはあの時、<line-break>ネモにイタズラしようとしていたのは、<line-break>私の前に座っていた生徒だったのです。<line-break>私はそれを止めようとしたのですが、<line-break>とっさのことだったもので…<line-break>　<line-break>つい無意識にライトアローの呪文を<line-break>使ってしまったんです。<end>"
EN: "In fact, the student sitting in front of me was trying to play a prank on Nemo. I tried to stop it, but it all happened so suddenly... I ended up casting Light Arrow without even thinking.<end>"
KO: "사실 그때 네모에게 장난을 치려던 건 제 앞에 앉아 있던 학생이었습니다. 저는 그걸 막으려 했지만 워낙 갑작스러운 일이어서… 무의식중에 라이트 애로 주문을 써 버렸습니다.<end>"

## 2020003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec202-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "少々やり過ぎたかもしれませんね。<line-break>お蔭でその飛び火が<line-break>ネモの頭に当たってしまいましたから。<line-break>このことはネモには内緒ですよ。<end>"
EN: "I may have overdone it a little. The stray flame ended up hitting Nemo on the head. Don't tell him about this.<end>"
KO: "조금 지나쳤을지도 모르겠네요. 덕분에 불똥이 네모의 머리에 맞았으니까요. 이건 네모에게 비밀이에요.<end>"

## 2020004

## 2030002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec203-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "くしゃみが出たんで、<line-break>もしやと思ったんですが、<line-break>また私の話ですか、ネモ？<end>"
EN: "I sneezed, so I had a feeling... Are you talking about me again, Nemo?<end>"
KO: "재채기가 나와서 혹시나 했는데, 또 제 이야기입니까, 네모?<end>"

## 2030003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec203-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "コイツは昔っから野放図でな。<line-break>まあ、その点は今とたいして<line-break>変わっちゃいないが…。<line-break>昔はもっとヒドかったんだ。<line-break>なにしろ、よく俺様の授業中に<line-break>グースカ寝てやがったからな。<line-break>たまに、姿が見えないと思ったら<line-break>そん時は森の中でのんきに<line-break>お昼寝ときたもんだ。<end>"
EN: "This guy's always done whatever he pleases. Not that he's changed much in that respect... but he was even worse back then. He used to snore his way through my classes. And whenever he was nowhere to be seen, sure enough, he'd be off in the woods napping without a care in the world.<end>"
KO: "이 녀석은 옛날부터 제멋대로였어. 뭐, 그 점은 지금도 별로 달라지지 않았지만…. 예전엔 더 심했지. 내 수업 시간에 툭하면 퍼질러 자질 않나, 가끔 모습이 안 보인다 싶으면 숲속에서 태평하게 낮잠이나 자고 있었으니까.<end>"

## 2030004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec203-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "しかも怒鳴ろうが殴ろうが<line-break>まったく起きやしねえ。<line-break>　<line-break>あれだけ根性入れて眠ってたのは<line-break>天界でもお前だけだろうよ。<end>"
EN: "No amount of shouting or hitting would wake him, either. You must've been the only one in the whole celestial realm who could sleep with that kind of determination.<end>"
KO: "게다가 소리쳐도 때려도 전혀 일어나질 않더군. 그렇게 근성 있게 자는 놈은 천계에서도 너뿐일 거다.<end>"

## 2030005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec203-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "根性じゃありません、呪文ですよ。<line-break>自分にスリープの呪文を<line-break>かけていたんです。<end>"
EN: "It wasn't determination. It was a spell. I cast Sleep on myself.<end>"
KO: "근성이 아니라 주문이에요. 자기 자신에게 슬립 주문을 걸어 둔 겁니다.<end>"

## 2030006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec203-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "………………。<line-break>お前なぁ…。<line-break>　<line-break>そうまでして、俺の授業が<line-break>聞きたくなかったのかよ！<line-break>　<line-break>ははぁ、わかったぞ。<line-break>さては俺様の授業が高度すぎて、<line-break>ついてこられなかったんだな。<end>"
EN: "You... You went that far to avoid listening to my classes?! Ah, now I get it. My lessons were too advanced for you to keep up, weren't they?<end>"
KO: "………………. 너 말이야…. 그렇게까지 내 수업을 듣기 싫었던 거냐! 하하, 알겠다. 이 몸의 수업이 너무 수준 높아서 못 따라왔던 거구나.<end>"

## 2030007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec203-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "むしろ逆でしたかね。<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>すでに自分の知っている知識を<line-break>授業で繰り返されても<line-break>つまらないだけですよ。<end>"
EN: "Quite the opposite, really. There's nothing interesting about sitting through lessons on things I already know.<end>"
KO: "오히려 반대였나 보군요. 이미 아는 내용을 수업에서 반복해 봐야 지루할 뿐이죠.<end>"

## 2030008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec203-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ねえ、<value:$28>？<end>"
EN: "Isn't that right, <value:$28>?<end>"
KO: "있잖아, <value:$28>?<end>"

## 2030009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec203-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "な、なんだとぉ～！<end>"
EN: "W-What did you say?!<end>"
KO: "뭐, 뭐라고오~!<end>"

## 2030010
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec203-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "でも、ネモの授業はなかなか<line-break>興味深かったですよ。<line-break>だから、たまに起きていたんです。<line-break>他の先生方の授業は退屈すぎて<line-break>ほとんど眠ってましたから。<line-break>　<line-break>それだけでもネモの授業は<line-break>優秀だったと思いますよ。<end>"
EN: "Still, Nemo's classes were fairly interesting. That's why I was awake for them now and then. The other teachers' lessons were so dull that I slept through nearly all of them. By that measure alone, I'd say Nemo's classes were excellent.<end>"
KO: "그래도 네모의 수업은 꽤 흥미로웠어요. 그래서 가끔은 깨어 있었죠. 다른 선생님들 수업은 너무 지루해서 거의 잤거든요. 그것만으로도 네모의 수업은 훌륭했다고 생각해요.<end>"

## 2030011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec203-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…なんか、素直に喜べねえな。<line-break>学問よりも先に、もう少し礼節を<line-break>学ばせるべきだったか…。<end>"
EN: "Somehow, I can't quite bring myself to be pleased. Maybe I should've taught you a few more manners before I taught you anything scholarly...<end>"
KO: "…왠지 마냥 기뻐할 수가 없네. 학문보다 먼저 예절을 좀 더 가르쳤어야 했나….<end>"

## 2030012
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec203-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今頃反省しても遅いと思いますけど。<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>さて、ネモがもっと悪い記憶を<line-break>掘り起こさないうちに<line-break>退散するとしましょうか。<end>"
EN: "It's a little late for regrets now. Well, I think I'll make my exit before Nemo dredges up any more unpleasant memories.<end>"
KO: "이제 와 반성해도 늦었다고 생각합니다만. 자, 네모가 더 나쁜 기억을 끄집어내기 전에 물러나도록 하죠.<end>"

## 2030013
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec203-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふうっ。<line-break>なんだか話す気が失せちまった。<line-break>　<line-break>続きはまた今度、<line-break>俺様の機嫌がいい時にしてやるよ。<end>"
EN: "Whew. I've lost the urge to talk. I'll tell you the rest some other time, when I'm in a better mood.<end>"
KO: "후우. 왠지 이야기할 마음이 사라졌군. 나머지는 다음에, 내 기분이 좋을 때 해 주지.<end>"

EN: ""
KO: "PSP용 불량소년 오르파우스 이벤트04용<end>"

source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec204-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "PSP用不良少年オルファウスイベント05用<end>"

## 2040001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec204-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>か…。<line-break>俺もヒマだし、せっかくだから<line-break>少し話につきあえよ。<line-break>そろそろネタも尽きてきたから、<line-break>オルファウスのとっておきの話を<line-break>聞かせてやろう。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, huh? Well, I've got time to kill, so stay and chat for a while. I'm running out of material, so I'll tell you one of my best stories about Orphaus.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>인가…. 나도 한가하니 잠깐 이야기 상대나 해 줘. 슬슬 소재도 떨어져 가니 오르파우스의 비장의 이야기를 들려주지.<end>"

## 2040002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec204-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "くしゃみが出たんで、<line-break>もしやと思ったんですが、<line-break>また私の話ですか、ネモ？<end>"
EN: "I sneezed, so I had a feeling... Are you talking about me again, Nemo?<end>"
KO: "재채기가 나와서 혹시나 했는데, 또 제 이야기입니까, 네모?<end>"

## 2040003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec204-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ああ、覚えてるだろ？<line-break>俺とお前が戦った時のことだ。<end>"
EN: "Yeah. You remember when you and I fought, don't you?<end>"
KO: "그래, 기억하지? 나와 네가 싸웠던 때 말이야.<end>"

## 2040004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec204-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そんなこともありましたね。<line-break>もうだいぶ昔のことなんで<line-break>あまり記憶にありませんけど。<end>"
EN: "Oh, yes. That did happen, didn't it? It was so long ago that I hardly remember it, though.<end>"
KO: "그런 일도 있었죠. 이미 꽤 옛날 일이라 별로 기억은 안 납니다만.<end>"

## 2040005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec204-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あの問題児が、<line-break>クィーダロアに選ばれたと思ったら、<line-break>たちまち立派な指導者だぜ。<end>"
EN: "That notorious troublemaker got chosen as Quidaloa, and in no time at all he'd become a fine leader.<end>"
KO: "그 문제아가 퀴다로아에 뽑혔다 싶더니 순식간에 훌륭한 지도자가 됐어.<end>"

## 2040006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec204-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さらにはエルフの軍勢を率いて、<line-break>魔人の俺様に徹底抗戦だ。<line-break>思わず別人かと疑っちまったよ。<line-break>それから何度も矛を交えたが、<line-break>最後に戦ったのは<line-break>バロルの時だったな…。<line-break>俺様は魔獣軍を指揮して、こいつは<line-break>ネメアとレーグ、ケリュネイアを<line-break>引き連れていた。<end>"
EN: "Then he led an army of Elves and put up a relentless fight against me, a demon. I almost thought he was a different person. We clashed many times after that, but our last battle came during the events surrounding Balor... I commanded the army of magical beasts, while he brought Nemea, Reig, and Ceryneia with him.<end>"
KO: "게다가 엘프 군세를 이끌고 마인인 나한테 철저히 맞섰지. 순간 다른 사람인가 싶었다니까. 그 뒤로 몇 번이나 맞붙었지만 마지막으로 싸운 건 발로르 때였어…. 나는 마수군을 지휘했고, 이 녀석은 네메아와 레이그, 케류네이아를 데리고 있었지.<end>"

## 2040007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec204-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "しかも、相変わらず俺様を<line-break>手こずらせたあげく、<line-break>しまいには敗北させやがって…。<line-break>敬愛する恩師相手に少しは<line-break>手加減してくれてもよかったんだぜ？<end>"
EN: "And after giving me as much trouble as ever, he even went and defeated me... You could have gone a little easier on your beloved mentor, you know?<end>"
KO: "게다가 여전히 이 몸을 애먹인 끝에 결국 패배시키기까지 하고…. 존경하는 스승을 상대로 조금은 봐줘도 좋았잖아?<end>"

## 2040008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec204-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "敬愛もなにも<line-break>私は昔から<line-break>札付きの問題児でしたからね。<line-break>手加減をするつもりなんて<line-break>これっぽっちもありませんでしたよ。<line-break>　<line-break>むしろネモこそ、可愛い生徒を相手に<line-break>後腐れないよう徹底的に<line-break>叩き潰すつもりだったのでは？<end>"
EN: "Beloved or not, I was always a notorious troublemaker. I had no intention of going easy on you. If anything, weren't you the one planning to crush your dear pupil so thoroughly that there would be no trouble afterward, Nemo?<end>"
KO: "존경이고 뭐고 저는 예전부터 악명 높은 문제아였으니까요. 봐줄 생각은 조금도 없었습니다. 오히려 네모야말로 귀여운 제자를 상대로 뒤탈이 없게 철저히 짓밟을 생각이었던 것 아닌가요?<end>"

## 2040009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec204-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "バレてたか…。<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>そう言うお前だって、隙あらば<line-break>俺を亡き者にしようとしてたろ？<end>"
EN: "So you saw through me... But you were ready to do away with me too, the moment you got the chance, weren't you?<end>"
KO: "들켰나…. 너도 틈만 나면 나를 없애려 했잖아?<end>"

## 2040010
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec204-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まあ、最悪の場合はそれもありかな、<line-break>…と思っていたことは認めますよ。<line-break>　<line-break>でも、意外としぶとかったですね、<line-break>先生。<end>"
EN: "Well, I admit I did think it might come to that if worst came to worst. But you proved surprisingly hard to kill, Master.<end>"
KO: "뭐, 최악의 경우엔 그것도 괜찮다고 생각했던 건 인정합니다. 그래도 의외로 끈질기셨네요, 선생님.<end>"

## 2040011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec204-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ちぇっ、よく言うぜ。<line-break>お前が慢心して手を抜いていたら、<line-break>今頃、世界は変わっていたのによ。<end>"
EN: "Tch. Listen to you. If you'd grown overconfident and held back, the world would be a very different place by now.<end>"
KO: "쳇, 말은 잘하지. 네가 자만해서 힘을 뺐다면 지금쯤 세계는 달라져 있었을 텐데.<end>"

## 2040013
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec204-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やれやれ、口が達者なのは昔っから<line-break>変わんねえな。<line-break>　<line-break>まったく、恩師思いの生徒を持って<line-break>俺は幸せ者だよ。<end>"
EN: "Good grief. You've always had a silver tongue. I'm truly blessed to have such a devoted pupil.<end>"
KO: "이런, 말 잘하는 건 옛날부터 변함없군. 참, 스승을 생각해 주는 제자를 둬서 나는 행복한 사람이야.<end>"

## 2040014
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec204-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どういたしまして、先生。<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>さて、ネモがもっと悪い記憶を<line-break>掘り起こさないうちに<line-break>私は退散するとしましょうか。<end>"
EN: "You're very welcome, Master. Now, before Nemo dredges up any more unpleasant memories, I think I'll take my leave.<end>"
KO: "천만에요, 선생님. 자, 네모가 더 나쁜 기억을 끄집어내기 전에 저는 물러나죠.<end>"

## 2040015

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "PSP用不良少年オルファウスイベント05用<end>"

KO: "PSP용 불량소년 오르파우스 이벤트05용<end>"

JP: "初めての海用開始<end>"
EN: ""
KO: "첫 바다용 시작<end>"

## 2050001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec205-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これが海…なのですか…。<line-break>見渡すかぎり碧い水だけ…。<end>"
EN: "So this is... the sea? Nothing but blue water, as far as I can see...<end>"
KO: "이게 바다…인가요…. 바라보는 곳마다 푸른 물뿐….<end>"

## 2050002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec205-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "こんなにも広く、どこまでも<line-break>水が続いている…。<line-break>私には不思議な感じがします。<end>"
EN: "It's so vast, with water stretching on and on without end... It feels strange to me.<end>"
KO: "이렇게나 넓고 어디까지나 물이 이어져 있어…. 제게는 신기한 느낌입니다.<end>"

## 2050003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec205-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "海よりも、もっと深い驚きが<line-break>他にもあるのですか…？<end>"
EN: "Could there be other wonders deeper even than the sea...?<end>"
KO: "바다보다 더 깊은 놀라움이 또 있다는 건가요…?<end>"

## 2050004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec205-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あのまま神殿で朽ち果てても<line-break>悔いはないつもりでしたが、<line-break>　<line-break>今こうして<value:$28>様と<line-break>いろいろなものを見ていると<line-break>これでよかったと思います…。<end>"
EN: "I thought I would have no regrets even if I withered away in the temple. But now, seeing so many things with you, <value:$28>, I'm glad things turned out this way...<end>"
KO: "그대로 신전에서 썩어 사라져도 후회는 없을 생각이었습니다만, 지금 이렇게 <value:$28> 님과 여러 가지를 보니 이게 잘된 일이라 생각합니다….<end>"

## 2050005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec205-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ありがとう…。<end>"
EN: "Thank you...<end>"
KO: "고마워….<end>"

## 2060001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec206-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おい、見ろ！<line-break>ダークエルフだぞ！<end>"
EN: "Hey, look! A Dark Elf!<end>"
KO: "야, 봐! 다크 엘프다!<end>"

## 2060002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec206-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どうやら面倒なことになりそうね。<end>"
EN: "Looks like this is going to be trouble.<end>"
KO: "아무래도 귀찮은 일이 될 것 같네.<end>"

## 2060003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec206-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "町から出ていけ！<line-break>汚らわしいダークエルフめ！<end>"
EN: "Get out of town, you filthy Dark Elf!<end>"
KO: "마을에서 나가! 더러운 다크엘프 놈!<end>"

## 2060004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec206-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺の息子はダークエルフに<line-break>殺されたんだ！<line-break>誰か仇をとってくれ！<end>"
EN: "A Dark Elf killed my son! Someone avenge him!<end>"
KO: "내 아들은 다크엘프에게 살해당했어! 누가 복수해 줘!<end>"

## 2060005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec206-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "構うことはねえ。<line-break>今ここでやっちまえ！<line-break>　<line-break>放っておくと、<line-break>こいつは悪事を働くぞ！<end>"
EN: "Who cares? Let's kill the Dark Elf right now! If we do nothing, the Dark Elf will only go on to commit some crime!<end>"
KO: "상관없어. 지금 여기서 해치워! 내버려 두면 이놈은 나쁜 짓을 할 거야!<end>"

## 2060006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec206-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふざけんな。<line-break>ダークエルフをかばう気か、てめえ！<end>"
EN: "Don't give me that. You mean to defend a Dark Elf, you bastard?<end>"
KO: "웃기지 마. 다크엘프를 감싸겠다는 거냐, 이 자식아!<end>"

## 2060007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec206-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "説得しようとしたってムダよ、<line-break><value:$28>。<line-break>　<line-break>こいつらは矢で口をふさがないかぎり<line-break>引き下がらないわ。<end>"
EN: "There's no point trying to reason with them, <value:$28>. They won't back down unless we silence them with arrows.<end>"
KO: "설득해 봐야 소용없어, <value:$28>. 이 녀석들은 화살로 입을 막지 않는 한 물러서지 않을 거야.<end>"

## 2060008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec206-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なんなの、これ？<line-break>バカの集会？<line-break>それとも大陸一のバカを決めるお祭り？<line-break>どっちでもいいけど、<line-break>さっさと道を開けなさいよぉ！<line-break>むさ苦しくしてガマンできなくてよ！<end>"
EN: "What is this? A gathering of fools? Or a festival to decide the greatest fool on the continent? Either way, clear the road already! I can't stand this sweaty mob!<end>"
KO: "뭐야, 이건? 바보들 모임? 아니면 대륙 최고의 바보를 뽑는 축제? 어느 쪽이든 좋으니까 얼른 길을 비켜! 칙칙해서 못 견디겠어!<end>"

## 2060009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec206-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なんだと、貴様。ふざけるな！<end>"
EN: "What did you say, you bastard? Don't screw with me!<end>"
KO: "뭐라고, 네놈. 장난치지 마!<end>"

## 2060010
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec206-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふざけているのはそっちよ！<line-break>どこの世界にダークエルフだからって<line-break>迫害してもいいなんて理屈が通るのよ！<line-break>他のダークエルフがやったことなんて<line-break>こっちが知るわけないでしょぉ！<end>"
EN: "You're the ones being ridiculous! What kind of logic says it's all right to persecute someone just for being a Dark Elf? How are we supposed to know what other Dark Elves have done?<end>"
KO: "장난하는 건 그쪽이잖아! 다크엘프라고 해서 박해해도 된다는 논리가 어디 있어! 다른 다크엘프가 한 일을 우리가 어떻게 아냐고!<end>"

## 2060011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec206-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だったら、<line-break>あんたたち下等生物がひとり罪を犯せば<line-break>残りもみーんな同罪なわけね？<line-break>それで罰せられても文句ないんでしょ！<end>"
EN: "So if one of you inferior creatures commits a crime, that makes every last one of you guilty too, does it? Then you won't object when you're all punished for it!<end>"
KO: "그렇다면 너희 하등생물 중 한 명이 죄를 지으면 나머지도 전부 같은 죄인인 거네? 그렇게 벌받아도 불만 없겠지!<end>"

## 2060012
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec206-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うっ！　い、いや、それは…。<end>"
EN: "Urk! N-no, that's...<end>"
KO: "윽! 아, 아니, 그건….<end>"

## 2060013
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec206-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どきなさいよぉ！　高貴なアタクシは<line-break>さっさとおフロに入りたいの！<line-break>くだらないことで邪魔しないで！<end>"
EN: "Out of my way! My noble self wishes to bathe at once! Don't hold me up with this nonsense!<end>"
KO: "비켜어! 고귀한 이 몸은 얼른 목욕하고 싶어! 시시한 일로 방해하지 마!<end>"

## 2060014
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec206-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ほら、行くわよ<value:$28>！<end>"
EN: "Come along, <value:$28>!<end>"
KO: "자, 가자 <value:$28>!<end>"

## 2060015
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec206-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ありがとう。<line-break>おかげで無用の争いを<line-break>しないですんだわ。<end>"
EN: "Thank you. That spared us a pointless fight.<end>"
KO: "고마워. 덕분에 쓸데없는 싸움을 하지 않아도 됐어.<end>"

## 2060016
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec206-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "べ、別にあんたたちを<line-break>助けたわけじゃないわよ。<line-break>　<line-break>アタクシは早くおフロで<line-break>ゆっくりしたかっただけなの。<line-break>誤解しないでちょうだい。<end>"
EN: "I-it's not like I was trying to help you. I only wanted to get to the bath quickly so I could relax. Don't get the wrong idea.<end>"
KO: "벼, 별로 너희를 도운 게 아니야. 난 빨리 목욕하면서 쉬고 싶었을 뿐이라고. 오해하지 마.<end>"

## 2060017
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec206-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "迫害のダークエルフ用終端<end>"
EN: ""
KO: "박해받는 다크엘프용 끝<end>"

## 2070000
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec207-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "異種族間の１用開始<end>"
EN: ""
KO: "이종족 간 1용 시작<end>"

## 2070001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec207-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "きゃーっ！！　誰かーっ！！<end>"
EN: "Aah! Somebody!<end>"
KO: "꺄악!! 누구 없어요!!<end>"

## 2070002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec207-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "誰かッ！　助けてーッ！！<end>"
EN: "Somebody! Help me!<end>"
KO: "누구 없어요! 살려 줘요!!<end>"

## 2070003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec207-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "待ってろ。今、助けてやる！！<end>"
EN: "Hold on! I'm coming!<end>"
KO: "기다려. 지금 구해 줄게!!<end>"

## 2070004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec207-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺の後ろから絶対に離れるな！<end>"
EN: "Stay behind me, no matter what!<end>"
KO: "내 뒤에서 절대 떨어지지 마!<end>"

## 2070006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec207-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おう、手を貸してくれるのか？<line-break>助かるね。<end>"
EN: "Oh, you're lending a hand? I could use the help.<end>"
KO: "오, 도와줄 거야? 살았네.<end>"

## 2070007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec207-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "もう大丈夫だ。ケガはないか？<end>"
EN: "You're safe now. Are you hurt?<end>"
KO: "이제 괜찮아. 다친 데는 없어?<end>"

## 2070009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec207-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ちぇっ、無愛想な娘だな…。<line-break>礼も言わずに逃げちまった。<line-break>俺ってそんなに恐い顔してたかな。<line-break>んっ…！？　これは…。<end>"
EN: "Sheesh, what an unfriendly girl. Ran off without even saying thanks. Do I really look that scary? Hm? What's this...?<end>"
KO: "쳇, 무뚝뚝한 애네…. 고맙단 말도 없이 도망가 버렸어. 내가 그렇게 무섭게 생겼나. 응…!? 이건….<end>"

## 2070010
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec207-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ペンダント…？　あの娘の物か？<end>"
EN: "A pendant...? Is it hers?<end>"
KO: "펜던트…? 그 여자아이 물건인가?<end>"

## 2070011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec207-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それはそうと、ありがとよ。<line-break>おかげで俺も助かったぜ。<end>"
EN: "Anyway, thanks. You got me out of a tight spot too.<end>"
KO: "그건 그렇고 고맙다. 덕분에 나도 살았어.<end>"

## 2070012
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec207-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "悲鳴を聞いて思わず飛び出しちまったが<line-break>魔物に勝てるか<line-break>ちょっと自信がなかったんだ。<line-break>ハハッ。俺はアミラルのヒューズ。<line-break>見ての通り冒険者だ。<end>"
EN: "I heard the scream and rushed in without thinking, but I wasn't too sure I was a match for monsters. Ha! I'm Hughes, from Amiral. As you can see, I'm an adventurer.<end>"
KO: "비명을 듣고 나도 모르게 뛰쳐나왔지만 마물에게 이길 자신은 좀 없었어. 하하. 난 아미랄의 휴즈. 보다시피 모험자야.<end>"

## 2070013
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec207-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>っていうのか。<line-break>身のこなしでわかっていたが<line-break>やっぱりあんたもご同業か。<line-break>よろしくな、<value:$28>。<line-break>普段はアミラルの酒場にいるから、<line-break>近くに来たら、寄ってくれ。<line-break>お礼に一杯、ごちそうするよ。<line-break>じゃあな！<end>"
EN: "So you're <value:$28>. I could tell by the way you move―you're an adventurer too, then. Good to meet you, <value:$28>. I'm usually at the tavern in Amiral, so stop by if you're ever nearby. I'll buy you a drink to thank you. See you around!<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>이라고 하는군. 몸놀림을 보고 알았지만 역시 당신도 동업자였나. 잘 부탁해, <value:$28>. 평소엔 아미랄 술집에 있으니 근처에 오면 들러 줘. 답례로 한잔 살게. 그럼 또 보자!<end>"

## 2070014
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec207-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "異種族間の１用終端<end>"
EN: ""
KO: "이종족 간 1용 끝<end>"

## 2080000
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec208-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "異種族間の２用開始<end>"
EN: ""
KO: "이종족 간 2용 시작<end>"

## 2080001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec208-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よぉ！　来たか。<line-break><value:$28>！<end>"
EN: "Hey! You made it, <value:$28>!<end>"
KO: "여어! 왔구나, <value:$28>!<end>"

## 2080002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec208-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "シェーヌの森で助けてもらったお礼に<line-break>一杯おごるって約束してたからな。<line-break>いつ来るかと待ちわびてたぜ。<line-break>えっ！　酒は飲めないのか？<line-break>そいつは残念だな…。それなら、<line-break>せめてお茶でもおごらせてくれよ。<end>"
EN: "I promised to buy you a drink for helping me in Chene Forest. I've been waiting for you to show up. What? You can't drink? That's a shame… Well, at least let me buy you some tea.<end>"
KO: "셰누의 숲에서 도와준 사례로 한잔 사겠다고 약속했잖아. 언제 오나 기다렸어. 뭐! 술은 못 마셔? 아쉽네…. 그럼 적어도 차라도 한잔 사게 해 줘.<end>"

## 2080003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec208-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ヒューズからルーマティーを　<line-break>おごってもらった。<end>"
EN: "Hughes treated you to some Rumor Tea.<end>"
KO: "휴즈에게 루마티를 얻어마셨다.<end>"

## 2080004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec208-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…そうか。あんたもけっこう、<line-break>いろんな所を旅しているんだな…。<line-break>　<line-break>どうりで魔物と戦った時も<line-break>落ち着いていたわけだ…。<end>"
EN: "You've traveled around quite a bit too, haven't you? No wonder you were so calm when you fought those monsters.<end>"
KO: "…그렇군. 당신도 꽤 여러 곳을 여행했구나…. 어쩐지 마물과 싸울 때도 침착하더라니….<end>"

## 2080005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec208-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうだ！　すまないが<line-break>ひとつ頼まれてくれないか？<end>"
EN: "Oh, that reminds me! Sorry to ask, but could you do me a favor?<end>"
KO: "그래! 미안하지만 하나 부탁해도 될까?<end>"

## 2080006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec208-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これは、シェーヌの森で<line-break>あの娘を助けたときに<line-break>拾ったペンダントだ。<line-break>返しに行くつもりだったが、<line-break>急な仕事が入っちまったんでな。<line-break>　<line-break>できれば、あんたに代わりを<line-break>頼みたいんだ。<line-break>　<line-break>あんたは信用できる上に<line-break>かなり腕も立つ。<line-break>引き受けてくれないか？<end>"
EN: "I found this pendant when I helped that girl in Chene Forest. I meant to return it, but an urgent job came up. If you can, I'd like you to go in my place. I trust you, and you're more than capable. Will you do it?<end>"
KO: "이건 셴느의 숲에서 그 아이를 도왔을 때 주운 펜던트다. 돌려주러 갈 생각이었는데 갑자기 일이 생겨서 말이야. 가능하다면 네가 대신해 줬으면 해. 넌 믿을 만하고 실력도 상당하니까. 맡아 주지 않겠나?<end>"

## 2080007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec208-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "仕方ないな<end>"
EN: "All right, I'll do it.<end>"
KO: "어쩔 수 없군<end>"

## 2080008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec208-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "忙しいからダメ<end>"
EN: "I'm too busy.<end>"
KO: "바쁘니까 안 돼<end>"

## 2080009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec208-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ありがとう！　シェーヌの森に<line-break>届けてくれるんだな。<line-break>これで俺も安心して旅立てる。<end>"
EN: "Thanks! You'll take it to Chene Forest, then. Now I can leave with one less thing to worry about.<end>"
KO: "고마워! 셰느의 숲에 전해 주는 거구나. 이걸로 나도 안심하고 떠날 수 있겠어.<end>"

## 2080010
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec208-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "恩に着るぜ。<line-break>今日のところはここでお別れだ。<line-break>またな、<value:$28>！<end>"
EN: "I owe you one. This is goodbye for now. See you around, <value:$28>!<end>"
KO: "신세 졌다. 오늘은 여기서 헤어지자. 또 보자, <value:$28>!<end>"

## 2080011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec208-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…そうか。まあ仕方ないよな。<line-break>あんたにはあんたの仕事が<line-break>あるんだろうし。<line-break>無理な頼み事をして悪かった。<line-break>他を当たるよ。すまんな。<end>"
EN: "Oh. Well, I can't blame you. You've got your own work to do. Sorry for asking so much of you. I'll find someone else. Sorry about that.<end>"
KO: "…그런가. 뭐, 어쩔 수 없지. 당신에겐 당신 일이 있을 테니까. 무리한 부탁을 해서 미안했다. 다른 사람을 알아볼게. 미안하군.<end>"

## 2080012
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec208-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "異種族間の２用終端<end>"
EN: ""
KO: "이종족 간 2용 끝<end>"
