## 2090001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec209-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あなたは…。<line-break>もしかして、あの時<line-break>助けてくださった方ですね？<end>"
EN: "You're... Could you be the person who helped me that time?<end>"
KO: "당신은… 혹시 그때 저를 구해주셨던 분인가요?<end>"

## 2090002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec209-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "本当にありがとうございました。<line-break>私はフィアと申します。<end>"
EN: "Thank you so much. My name is Fia.<end>"
KO: "정말 감사합니다. 저는 피아라고 합니다.<end>"

## 2090003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec209-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…えっ？　私に渡す物？<end>"
EN: "What? You have something for me?<end>"
KO: "…어? 나한테 줄 물건?<end>"

## 2090004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec209-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これは…！確かに私のペンダントです！<line-break>ずっと探していたんです。あなたが見つけて、<line-break>拾っておいてくださったのですね。<line-break>ありがとうございます。<end>"
EN: "Oh! It really is my pendant! I've been looking for it all this time. You found it and held on to it for me, didn't you? Thank you so much.<end>"
KO: "이건…! 분명 제 펜던트예요! 계속 찾고 있었어요. 당신이 찾아서 주워 두셨군요. 감사합니다.<end>"

## 2090005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec209-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あなたが拾ってくださったのでは<line-break>ないのですか？<end>"
EN: "Weren't you the one who picked it up?<end>"
KO: "당신이 주워 주신 게 아니었습니까?<end>"

## 2090006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec209-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ああ、あのもうひとりの方が…。<line-break>そうだったのですか。<end>"
EN: "Oh, it was the other person... So that's what happened.<end>"
KO: "아, 그 다른 분이…. 그랬군요.<end>"

## 2090007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec209-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "大変、申しわけないのですが、<line-break>ひとつお願いを聞いて<line-break>いただけませんか？<line-break>あの方をこちらへ<line-break>お連れいただきたいのです。<line-break>　<line-break>あの時はすっかり動揺してしまって<line-break>お礼も言えませんでした。<line-break>　<line-break>ですから、せめてひと言<line-break>あの方にも、お礼が言いたくて…。<end>"
EN: "I'm terribly sorry to ask, but would you do me a favor? I'd like you to bring the other person here. I was so shaken at the time that I never even said thank you. I'd at least like the chance to thank that person as well...<end>"
KO: "정말 죄송하지만 부탁 하나만 들어주시겠습니까? 그분을 이곳으로 데려와 주셨으면 합니다. 그때는 너무 당황해서 감사 인사도 하지 못했습니다. 그러니 적어도 한마디라도 그분께 감사하다고 말하고 싶어서….<end>"

## 2090008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec209-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いいよ<end>"
EN: "Sure.<end>"
KO: "좋아<end>"

## 2090009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec209-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今は忙しい<end>"
EN: "I'm busy right now.<end>"
KO: "지금은 바빠<end>"

## 2090010
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec209-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ありがとうございます！<line-break>私、ここで待っておりますから<line-break>どうぞよろしくお願いいたします。<end>"
EN: "Thank you! I'll be waiting here. I truly appreciate this.<end>"
KO: "감사합니다! 저는 여기서 기다리고 있을 테니 잘 부탁드리겠습니다.<end>"

## 2090011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec209-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…そうですか。<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>すみませんでした。<line-break>変なお願いをしてしまって。<line-break>気を悪くなさらないでください。<end>"
EN: "Oh... I understand. I'm sorry for making such a strange request. Please don't take offense.<end>"
KO: "…그렇군요. 죄송했습니다. 이상한 부탁을 해 버렸네요. 기분 나쁘게 생각하지 말아 주세요.<end>"

## 2090012
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec209-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ペンダント、届けてくださって、<line-break>本当にうれしかったです。<end>"
EN: "I was so glad you brought me my pendant.<end>"
KO: "펜던트를 전해 주셔서 정말 기뻤습니다.<end>"

## 2090013
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec209-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あの方にもよろしくお伝えください。<line-break>それでは、失礼いたします。<end>"
EN: "Please give the other person my regards as well. Now, if you'll excuse me.<end>"
KO: "그분께도 안부 전해 주세요. 그럼 실례하겠습니다.<end>"

## 2100001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec210-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "恩に着るぜ、<value:$28>。<line-break>あんたのことだ。<line-break>ペンダントは渡してきてくれたんだろ？<line-break>さあ、一杯おごらせてくれ。<end>"
EN: "I owe you one, <value:$28>. Knowing you, you've already delivered the pendant, right? Come on, let me buy you a drink.<end>"
KO: "신세 졌다, <value:$28>. 너라면 펜던트는 전해 줬겠지? 자, 한잔 사게 해 줘.<end>"

## 2100002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec210-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "えっ、あのエルフが俺を呼んでいる？<end>"
EN: "What? That Elf wants to see me?<end>"
KO: "뭐, 그 엘프가 나를 부른다고?<end>"

## 2100003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec210-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふーん、あの娘、<line-break>フィアっていうのか…。<end>"
EN: "Huh. So her name's Fia...<end>"
KO: "흐음, 그 애 이름이 피아인가….<end>"

## 2100004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec210-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わ、わかった、行くよ。<line-break>ちょうど仕事が片づいたとこだし。<line-break>でも、なあ…。<end>"
EN: "A-all right, I'll go. I just finished up my work anyway. It's just...<end>"
KO: "아, 알았어. 갈게. 마침 일도 끝났고. 하지만 말이야….<end>"

## 2100005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec210-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いやいや、違うよ。<line-break>会いたくないわけじゃないんだ。<end>"
EN: "No, no, that's not it. It's not that I don't want to see her.<end>"
KO: "아니야, 아니야. 만나기 싫다는 뜻은 아니야.<end>"

## 2100007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec210-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なあ、頼む。<line-break>できれば<value:$28>も<line-break>一緒に来てくれないか？<end>"
EN: "Come on, do me a favor. Could you come with me, <value:$28>?<end>"
KO: "부탁이야. 가능하면 <value:$28>도 같이 와 주지 않을래?<end>"

## 2100008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec210-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "一緒に行く<end>"
EN: "I'll come with you.<end>"
KO: "같이 간다<end>"

## 2100009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec210-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "悪いけど…<end>"
EN: "Sorry, but…<end>"
KO: "미안하지만…<end>"

## 2100010
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec210-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ありがとよ。助かるぜ。<line-break>じゃあ、さっそく行こうか。<end>"
EN: "Thanks. You're a lifesaver. Well, let's get going.<end>"
KO: "고맙다. 살았어. 그럼 바로 가 볼까.<end>"

## 2100011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec210-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よかった…。<line-break>来てくださったんですね。<line-break><line-break>ありがとうございます。<line-break>ペンダントも拾っていただいて。<end>"
EN: "I'm so glad... You came. Thank you―and thank you for finding my pendant, too.<end>"
KO: "다행이에요…. 와 주셨군요. 감사합니다. 펜던트도 주워 주시고.<end>"

## 2100012
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec210-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "礼を言われるほどのことじゃない。<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>しかし、ちょっと意外だな…。<end>"
EN: "It was nothing. Still, I have to say, I'm a little surprised...<end>"
KO: "감사받을 일은 아니야. 하지만 조금 의외군….<end>"

## 2100013
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec210-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "えっ？<end>"
EN: "What?<end>"
KO: "어?<end>"

## 2100014
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec210-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "長いこと冒険者稼業をしているが<line-break>エルフにお礼を言われたのは<line-break>これが初めてだよ。<end>"
EN: "I've been an adventurer a long time, but this is the first time an Elf has ever thanked me.<end>"
KO: "오랫동안 모험자 일을 했지만 엘프에게 고맙다는 말을 들은 건 처음이야.<end>"

## 2100015
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec210-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…助けていただいて<line-break>お礼を申し上げるのは当然です。<line-break>それに私のほうこそ驚いています。<end>"
EN: "It's only natural that I should thank you for helping me. Besides, I'm surprised myself.<end>"
KO: "…도움을 받았으니 감사드리는 건 당연합니다. 그리고 오히려 놀란 건 저예요.<end>"

## 2100016
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec210-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "小さい頃からずっと、森の外には<line-break>礼儀知らずで野蛮な人間たちが<line-break>住んでいると教えられてきましたから。<end>"
EN: "Ever since I was little, I was taught that the humans beyond the forest were rude and barbaric.<end>"
KO: "어릴 때부터 숲 밖에는 예의 없고 야만적인 인간들이 산다고 배워 왔으니까요.<end>"

## 2100017
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec210-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それじゃあ、お互いさまだな。<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>俺はエルフなんていつも<line-break>お高くとまった、いけ好かない<line-break>連中だと思っていたよ。<line-break>でも、今日のあんたを見て、<line-break>認識を改めたけど…。<end>"
EN: "Then I guess we're even. I always thought Elves were a stuck-up, unpleasant lot. But after meeting you today, I've changed my mind...<end>"
KO: "그럼 피차 마찬가지군. 나도 엘프란 늘 잘난 척하는 재수 없는 족속이라고 생각했거든. 하지만 오늘 당신을 보고 생각을 고쳤지만….<end>"

## 2100018
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec210-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どういうふうにですか？<end>"
EN: "How so?<end>"
KO: "어떤 식으로요?<end>"

## 2100019
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec210-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なんて言うか、あんたみたいに<line-break>気さくで、優しいエルフも<line-break>中には、いるんだなって…。<line-break>おまけに、エラい美人だし…。<end>"
EN: "Well, I guess there are Elves who are friendly and kind, like you... And very beautiful, too.<end>"
KO: "뭐랄까, 당신처럼 붙임성 좋고 상냥한 엘프도 있구나 싶어서…. 게다가 엄청난 미인이기도 하고….<end>"

## 2100020
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec210-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まあ…。<end>"
EN: "Oh my...<end>"
KO: "어머….<end>"

## 2100021
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec210-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうか、残念だな。<line-break>別の人に頼んでみるよ。<line-break>　<line-break>じゃあな。<end>"
EN: "That's a shame. I'll ask someone else, then. See you around.<end>"
KO: "그런가, 아쉽군. 다른 사람에게 부탁해 볼게. 그럼.<end>"

## 2110001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec211-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よぉ！　あんたか。<line-break>ちょうどよかった…。<end>"
EN: "Hey! There you are. Good timing...<end>"
KO: "여어! 당신이군. 마침 잘됐어….<end>"

## 2110002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec211-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…実は折り入って、<line-break>あんたに話があるんだ。<end>"
EN: "Actually... there's something important I need to talk to you about.<end>"
KO: "…사실은 따로 할 이야기가 있어.<end>"

## 2110003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec211-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺、あれからちょくちょく<line-break>あの森でフィアと会っててさ。<line-break>　<line-break>それで…その…<line-break>なんて言うか、あれだよ…。<line-break>その…つまり、わかるだろ？<line-break>いいかげん察してくれよ。<end>"
EN: "I've been meeting up with Fia in that forest every so often since then. And, well... how do I put it? It's just... You know what I mean, right? Come on, take the hint already.<end>"
KO: "나, 그 뒤로 종종 그 숲에서 피아를 만나고 있어. 그래서… 그게…. 뭐라고 해야 하나, 그거 있잖아…. 그러니까… 알겠지? 이제 좀 눈치채 줘.<end>"

## 2110004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec211-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "惚れちまったんだ…。<line-break>人間のこの俺が、エルフのあの娘に<line-break>恋しちまったんだよ！<end>"
EN: "I've fallen for her... Me, a human, in love with an Elf!<end>"
KO: "반해 버렸어…. 인간인 내가 엘프인 그 여자에게 사랑에 빠졌다고!<end>"

## 2110005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec211-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "若いの、盗み聞きする気はなかったが<line-break>つい耳に入ってしまったのでな。<line-break>ひと言、言わせてもらえんかの？<end>"
EN: "Youngster, I did not mean to eavesdrop, but I could not help overhearing. May I say something?<end>"
KO: "젊은이, 엿들을 생각은 없었는데 우연히 귀에 들어왔구나. 한마디 해도 되겠나?<end>"

## 2110006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec211-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…あ、ああ。<end>"
EN: "Uh... sure.<end>"
KO: "…아, 그래.<end>"

## 2110007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec211-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ずばり言う。<line-break>おぬし気は確かか？<end>"
EN: "I'll be blunt. Are you in your right mind?<end>"
KO: "단도직입적으로 묻지. 자네 제정신인가?<end>"

## 2110008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec211-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "えっ…！？<end>"
EN: "What...?!<end>"
KO: "어…!?<end>"

## 2110009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec211-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "エルフなんぞ高慢で、<line-break>他種族を見下すしか能のない<line-break>鼻持ちならん連中じゃぞ？<line-break>わしも長いこと大陸を回っておるが<line-break>人間とエルフが恋に落ちて幸せになった<line-break>などという話はついぞ聞かぬわい。<end>"
EN: "Elves are arrogant, insufferable creatures who do nothing but look down on other races. I have wandered this continent for many a year, yet never once have I heard of a human and an Elf falling in love and living happily together.<end>"
KO: "엘프 따위는 오만하고 다른 종족을 깔보는 것밖에 모르는 못마땅한 족속이잖아? 나도 오랫동안 대륙을 돌아다녔지만 인간과 엘프가 사랑에 빠져 행복해졌다는 이야기는 한 번도 못 들었네.<end>"

## 2110010
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec211-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だろうね…。<line-break>…確かに俺もずっとそう思ってた。<end>"
EN: "I suppose not... To be honest, that's what I always thought too.<end>"
KO: "그렇겠지…. …확실히 나도 계속 그렇게 생각했어.<end>"

## 2110011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec211-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…けどさ、話してるうちに<line-break>エルフもそんな奴らだけじゃない、<line-break>ってことに気づいたんだ。<line-break>数は少ないけど、人間と対等に<line-break>付き合ってくれる変わり者も<line-break>いるんだよ。<line-break>お互いに偏見を捨てさえすれば、<line-break>きっと、もっといい関係を<line-break>築けるはずなんだ。<line-break>俺とフィアだって必ず…。<end>"
EN: "But after talking to her, I realized not all Elves are like that. There may not be many, but some of them are odd enough to treat humans as equals. If both sides could just let go of their prejudices, I'm sure we could build a better relationship. And Fia and I can too. I know we can...<end>"
KO: "…그런데 말이지, 이야기를 나누다 보니 엘프가 전부 그런 녀석들만은 아니라는 걸 알게 됐어. 수는 적지만 인간과 대등하게 지내 주는 별난 녀석들도 있더라고. 서로 편견만 버린다면 분명 더 좋은 관계를 만들 수 있을 거야. 나와 피아도 반드시….<end>"

## 2110012
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec211-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふん！　バカバカしい…。<line-break>世間知らずのひよっこの言葉じゃな。<line-break>　<line-break>わしに言わせれば、<line-break>恋とやらで頭に血がのぼって<line-break>現実が見えておらんだけじゃ。<end>"
EN: "Hmph! What nonsense... Spoken like a greenhorn who knows nothing of the world. As I see it, you've let this thing called love go to your head. You simply can't see reality anymore.<end>"
KO: "흥! 어리석기는…. 세상 물정 모르는 풋내기의 말이군. 내가 보기엔, 사랑이니 뭐니 해서 머리에 피가 올라현실이 안 보이는 것뿐이야.<end>"

## 2110013
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec211-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…勝手にせい！<line-break>どうせうまくいくはずないわい！<line-break>　<line-break>痛い思いをすれば、<line-break>そのうち幻想から覚めるじゃろう。<end>"
EN: "Do as you please! It will never work! Once you get hurt, you'll wake up from that fantasy soon enough.<end>"
KO: "…마음대로 해! 어차피 잘될 리가 없어! 아픈 꼴을 당하다 보면 언젠가 환상에서 깨어나겠지.<end>"

## 2110014
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec211-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "大丈夫。わかってるよ。<line-break>あのドワーフだって、別に俺とフィアの<line-break>仲に水を差そうと言ったわけじゃない。<line-break>普通に考えれば、あっちのほうが<line-break>正しいってことなんだろうな。<line-break>でも、俺は…。<end>"
EN: "It's okay. I understand. That Dwarf wasn't actually trying to come between Fia and me. If you look at it sensibly, the Dwarf is probably right. But I...<end>"
KO: "괜찮아. 알고 있어. 그 드워프도 딱히 나와 피아 사이를 갈라놓으려고 한 말은 아니었어. 보통 생각하면 그쪽 말이 맞는 거겠지. 하지만 나는….<end>"

## 2110015
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec211-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "………………。<line-break>悪い、<value:$28>。<line-break>しばらくひとりにしておいてくれ。<end>"
EN: "…Sorry, <value:$28>. I need to be alone for a while.<end>"
KO: "………………. 미안, <value:$28>. 잠시 혼자 있게 해줘.<end>"

## 2120000
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec212-part99.toml

## 2120001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec212-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "下等生物が、<line-break>神聖なエルフの森に<line-break>何の用なの？<end>"
EN: "What business does a lower life-form have in the sacred forest of the Elves?<end>"
KO: "하등생물이 신성한 엘프의 숲에 무슨 볼일이야?<end>"

## 2120002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec212-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "驚きに満ちた世界を<line-break>見せてくれるのかと思ったら、<line-break>エルフの森じゃないのぉ！<line-break>それともまさか、<line-break>このアタクシにエルフを見て驚け<line-break>なんて言うんじゃないでしょうねぇ！？<end>"
EN: "I thought you were going to show me a world full of wonders, and you bring me to the Elven forest! Surely you don't expect me to marvel at the sight of Elves?!<end>"
KO: "놀라움으로 가득한 세계를 보여 준다더니 엘프의 숲이잖아! 설마 이 나에게 엘프를 보고 놀라라고 하는 건 아니겠지!?<end>"

## 2120003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec212-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ホント、まっったくどうしようもない！<line-break>好奇心しか取り柄のない<line-break>下等生物ときたら…。<end>"
EN: "Honestly, lower life-forms are utterly hopeless! Curiosity is all they have going for them...<end>"
KO: "정말이지, 도저히 답이 없어! 호기심밖에 장점이 없는 하등생물이란….<end>"

## 2120004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec212-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フェティさん、人間を下等生物などと<line-break>おっしゃらないでください。<end>"
EN: "Fety, please don't call humans lower life-forms.<end>"
KO: "페티 씨, 인간을 하등생물이라고 부르지 말아 주세요.<end>"

## 2120005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec212-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…なんですってぇ！？<end>"
EN: "What did you just say?!<end>"
KO: "…뭐라고요!?<end>"

## 2120006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec212-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…じつはあれから何度かヒューズが<line-break>会いに来てくれたんです。<line-break>　<line-break>それ以来、私は少しずつ<line-break>彼に惹かれていく自分に<line-break>気づいてしまったのです…。<end>"
EN: "The truth is, Hughes has come to see me several times since then. And little by little, I've found myself falling for him...<end>"
KO: "…사실 그 뒤로 휴즈가 몇 번이나 만나러 와줬어요. 그때부터 조금씩 그에게 끌리는 저 자신을 깨닫고 말았어요…<end>"

## 2120007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec212-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今では一日千秋の想いで<line-break>あの人を待っています。<end>"
EN: "Now I count the hours until I can see him again.<end>"
KO: "지금은 하루가 천년 같은 마음으로 그 사람을 기다리고 있습니다.<end>"

## 2120008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec212-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なにが惹かれてく自分よ！<line-break>バカバカしすぎて、めまいがするわ！<line-break>　<line-break>人間は野蛮で下等で劣悪で<line-break>どうしようもない連中なのよぉ！<end>"
EN: "Don't give me that “falling for him” nonsense! It's so absurd it makes my head spin! Humans are savage, inferior, vile, hopeless creatures!<end>"
KO: "뭐가 마음이 끌린다는 거야! 너무 바보 같아서 어지러울 지경이야! 인간은 야만적이고 저급하고 열등한, 정말 답 없는 족속이라고!<end>"

## 2120009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec212-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あなたもクィーダ・シェーヌ様と<line-break>同じ考えなのですね…。<line-break>　<line-break>シェーヌ様は、<line-break>エルフ以外は皆、下等で愚かだと<line-break>おっしゃっていました。<end>"
EN: "So you share Quida Chene's view... Quida Chene said that everyone but the Elves was inferior and foolish.<end>"
KO: "당신도 퀴다 셰누 님과 같은 생각이군요…. 셰누 님은 엘프 이외에는 모두 열등하고 어리석다고 말씀하셨습니다.<end>"

## 2120010
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec212-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "でも、私はヒューズと出会って、<line-break>人間はそのような方ばかりでは<line-break>ないと知ったのです。<end>"
EN: "But meeting Hughes taught me that not all humans are like that.<end>"
KO: "하지만 저는 휴즈를 만나고, 인간이 그런 분들만 있는 것은 아니라는 걸 알게 되었습니다.<end>"

## 2120011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec212-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なによ、それ！？<line-break>信じらんないわ！<end>"
EN: "What is that supposed to mean?! I don't believe this!<end>"
KO: "뭐야, 그게!? 믿을 수 없어!<end>"

## 2120012
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec212-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ではお聞きしますが、フェティさん。<line-break>あなたはなぜ下等呼ばわりしている<line-break>人間と一緒に旅をしているのですか？<end>"
EN: "Then let me ask you this, Fety. Why are you traveling with a human when you call humans inferior?<end>"
KO: "그럼 묻겠습니다, 페티 씨. 당신은 왜 하등하다고 부르는 인간과 함께 여행하고 있습니까?<end>"

## 2120013
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec212-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ウッ、…そ、それは…。<end>"
EN: "Ugh... Th-that's because...<end>"
KO: "윽, …그, 그건….<end>"

## 2120014
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec212-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…この<value:$28>って人間が<line-break>驚きに満ちた世界を見せるって言うから<line-break>嫌々つき合ってあげてるだけよ！<end>"
EN: "It's only because this human, <value:$28>, promised to show me a world full of wonders! I'm merely putting up with the journey!<end>"
KO: "…이 <value:$28>이라는 인간이 놀라움으로 가득한 세계를 보여 준다길래 마지못해 같이 다니는 것뿐이야!<end>"

## 2120015
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec212-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "高貴なエルフと下等生物の人間が<line-break>恋ですって？<line-break>バカじゃない！？　アホじゃない！？<line-break>とにかく！<line-break>高貴なアタクシはそんなんじゃないの！<line-break>自分と一緒にしないでほしいわ！<end>"
EN: "A noble Elf in love with a lowly human? Are you stupid?! An idiot?! In any case, a noble Elf such as myself has nothing of the sort going on! Don't compare me to you!<end>"
KO: "고귀한 엘프와 하등생물인 인간이 사랑이라고? 바보 아니야!? 멍청이 아니야!? 아무튼! 고귀한 나는 그런 게 아니야! 나랑 엮지 마!<end>"

## 2120016
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec212-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "すみません。言い争いをするつもりでは<line-break>なかったのですが…。<line-break>　<line-break>ヒューズや、あなたのことを<line-break>悪しざまに言われたので、つい…。<line-break>気を悪くされたのなら謝ります。<end>"
EN: "I'm sorry. I didn't mean to start an argument. But after hearing such awful things said about Hughes and you, I couldn't stay silent... If I've offended you, I apologize.<end>"
KO: "죄송합니다. 말다툼할 생각은 없었습니다만…. 휴즈나 당신을 험하게 말해서 저도 모르게…. 기분이 상하셨다면 사과드리겠습니다.<end>"

## 2120017
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec212-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "でも、彼とのことは本気なのです。<line-break>それだけは信じてくださいね。<end>"
EN: "But I am serious about him. Please believe that, if nothing else.<end>"
KO: "하지만 그 사람과의 일은 진심이에요. 그것만은 믿어주세요.<end>"

## 2120018
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec212-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ありがとうございます。<line-break>それでは、失礼いたします。<end>"
EN: "Thank you. Now, if you'll excuse me.<end>"
KO: "감사합니다. 그럼 실례하겠습니다.<end>"

## 2120019
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec212-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "すみません。言い争いをするつもりでは<line-break>なかったのですが…。<line-break>　<line-break>気を悪くされたのなら謝ります。<end>"
EN: "I'm sorry. I didn't mean to start an argument... If I've offended you, I apologize.<end>"
KO: "죄송합니다. 말다툼할 생각은 아니었는데…. 기분이 상하셨다면 사과드리겠습니다.<end>"

## 2120020
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec212-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "でも、彼とのことは本気なのです。<line-break>それだけは信じてくださいね。<end>"
EN: "But I am serious about him. Please believe that, if nothing else.<end>"
KO: "하지만 그 사람과의 일은 진심이에요. 그것만은 믿어주세요.<end>"

## 2120021
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec212-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 2120022
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec212-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ちょっとぉ！　待ちなさいよ！<line-break>まだ、言いたいことの<line-break>半分も言ってないわよ！<end>"
EN: "Hey! Wait! I haven't said half of what I wanted to say yet!<end>"
KO: "잠깐! 기다려! 아직 하고 싶은 말의 절반도 못 했다고!<end>"

JP: "異種族間の６用開始<end>"
EN: ""
KO: "이종족 간 6용 시작<end>"

## 2130001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec213-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よぉ、あんたか…！<end>"
EN: "Hey, it's you...!<end>"
KO: "여어, 당신인가…!<end>"

## 2130002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec213-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺は信心深くないが、今のあんたは<line-break>海王様の使いに見えるぜ。<line-break>頼む。相談に乗ってくれ。<end>"
EN: "I'm not a religious man, but right now you look like a messenger from the Sea King himself. Please, hear me out.<end>"
KO: "난 신앙심이 깊진 않지만 지금의 당신은 해왕님의 사자처럼 보여. 부탁한다. 상담 좀 받아 줘.<end>"

## 2130003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec213-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あれから何度も森へ出かけてるのに、<line-break>ここんとこ、なぜかフィアに<line-break>会えないんだ。<line-break>思えば最後に会った時、<line-break>どことなく態度がよそよそしくて<line-break>何かおかしい感じはしたんだが…。<end>"
EN: "I've gone out to the forest again and again since then, but for some reason I haven't been able to see Fia lately. Thinking back, she did seem a little distant the last time I saw her. I had a feeling something was wrong...<end>"
KO: "그 뒤로 몇 번이나 숲에 갔는데 요즘은 왜인지 피아를 만날 수가 없어. 생각해 보면 마지막으로 만났을 때 어딘가 태도가 서먹했고 뭔가 이상하긴 했는데….<end>"

## 2130004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec213-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…いや、彼女に何もなければ<line-break>俺は別に構わないんだ…。<line-break>　<line-break>でもひょっとして、あんたなら何か<line-break>知ってるかもと思ってな。<end>"
EN: "No... as long as nothing's happened to her, I don't mind. I just thought you might know something.<end>"
KO: "…아니, 그녀에게 아무 일도 없다면 난 상관없어…. 다만 혹시 당신이라면 뭔가 알지도 모른다고 생각해서.<end>"

## 2130005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec213-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "エルフが急に姿を見せなくなるとは<line-break>穏やかでないのう…。<end>"
EN: "It is troubling that an Elf would suddenly stop showing herself...<end>"
KO: "엘프가 갑자기 모습을 감추다니 심상치 않구먼….<end>"

## 2130006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec213-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…あんたは？<end>"
EN: "And you are...?<end>"
KO: "…당신은?<end>"

## 2130007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec213-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わしはイオンズ。<line-break>縁あって<value:$28>と<line-break>知り合った神官じゃ。<end>"
EN: "I'm Ions, a priest. Circumstances brought <value:$28> and me together.<end>"
KO: "나는 이온즈. 인연이 닿아 <value:$28>와 알게 된 신관일세.<end>"

## 2130008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec213-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どうじゃ、<value:$28>。<line-break>シェーヌの森へ行ってみんか？<end>"
EN: "What do you say, <value:$28>? Shall we pay Chene Forest a visit?<end>"
KO: "어때, <value:$28>. 셰느의 숲에 가 보지 않겠나?<end>"

## 2130009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec213-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なあに、構わんじゃろう。<line-break>それほど神経質になることもあるまい。<line-break>　<line-break>むしろ無関係のわしらにならば、<line-break>向こうも警戒せずに<line-break>会ってくれるかもしれん。<line-break>単なる男女の行き違いであれば<line-break>それを確認するだけでもよかろう。<end>"
EN: "Oh, I'm sure it will be fine. There's no need to fret so. Since we have nothing to do with the matter, she may be less guarded and agree to see us. If this is simply a misunderstanding between a man and a woman, there is no harm in finding that out.<end>"
KO: "뭐, 괜찮지 않겠나. 그렇게 신경을 곤두세울 필요도 없을 게야. 오히려 아무 관련 없는 우리라면 상대도 경계하지 않고 만나 줄지 모르지. 단순히 남녀 사이의 엇갈림이라면 그것만 확인해도 충분할 테고.<end>"

## 2130010
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec213-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "むしろ、そうでない場合が<line-break>心配なんじゃ…。<end>"
EN: "What concerns me is the possibility that it isn't...<end>"
KO: "오히려 그렇지 않은 경우가 걱정인 게야….<end>"

## 2130011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec213-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "この人の言うとおりだ。<line-break>頼む！<end>"
EN: "The priest is right. Please!<end>"
KO: "이 사람이 말한 대로야. 부탁해!<end>"

## 2130012
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec213-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "仕方ないな<end>"
EN: "All right, I suppose.<end>"
KO: "어쩔 수 없군<end>"

## 2130013
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec213-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ちょっと今は…<end>"
EN: "Not right now…<end>"
KO: "지금은 좀….<end>"

## 2130014
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec213-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ありがとう、恩に着るぜ。<line-break>またあんたには借りが<line-break>できそうだな…。<line-break>俺はしばらく仕事でここを離れる。<line-break>結果はあとで聞かせてくれ。<line-break>頼んだぜ！<end>"
EN: "Thanks. I won't forget this. Looks like I'll owe you yet another favor... I'll be away from here on business for a while. You can tell me how it went later. I'm counting on you!<end>"
KO: "고마워, 신세 졌다. 또 네게 빚을 지게 될 것 같군… 난 잠시 일 때문에 이곳을 떠나. 결과는 나중에 들려줘. 부탁한다!<end>"

## 2130015
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec213-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "では行くかの、<value:$28>。<line-break>シェーヌの森とやらへ。<end>"
EN: "Then let us be off, <value:$28>―to this Chene Forest.<end>"
KO: "그럼 가 볼까, <value:$28>. 셰느의 숲이라는 곳으로.<end>"

## 2130016
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec213-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうか…。<end>"
EN: "I see...<end>"
KO: "그런가….<end>"

## 2130017
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec213-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いや、いいんだ。<line-break>くだらない話をして悪かった。<line-break>　<line-break>やっぱりこれは俺自身で<line-break>解決しないといけない問題だよな。<line-break>他人に頼るのが間違っていたんだ。<end>"
EN: "No, it's fine. Sorry to bother you with something so trivial. I suppose this is something I have to settle myself. I was wrong to ask anyone else for help.<end>"
KO: "아니, 됐어. 쓸데없는 얘기를 해서 미안해. 역시 이건 내 스스로해결해야 할 문제겠지. 남에게 의지한 게 잘못이었어.<end>"

## 2130018
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec213-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これまでありがとよ、<line-break><value:$28>。<line-break>じゃあな…。<end>"
EN: "Thanks for everything, <value:$28>. See you...<end>"
KO: "그동안 고마웠다, <value:$28>. 잘 있어….<end>"

## 2130019
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec213-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>よ。<line-break>本当によかったのか？　これで…。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>... Are you certain that was the right decision?<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>. 정말 이걸로 괜찮은 거냐…?<end>"

## 2130020

## 2140002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec214-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…えっ、ヒューズが？<end>"
EN: "…What? Hughes did?<end>"
KO: "…어, 휴즈가?<end>"

## 2140003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec214-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうですか。そんなことが…。<end>"
EN: "I see. So that's what happened…<end>"
KO: "그렇습니까. 그런 일이….<end>"

## 2140004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec214-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "違います！<line-break>私は今もずっと<line-break>彼のことを想い続けています。<line-break>それどころか、この想いは<line-break>日増しに募るばかりです。<line-break>でも、だからこそ…。<end>"
EN: "No! I still love him as much as ever. If anything, my feelings grow stronger with each passing day. But that is precisely why…<end>"
KO: "아닙니다! 저는 지금도 계속 그를 생각하고 있습니다. 오히려 이 마음은 날이 갈수록 더 커지고 있어요. 하지만 그래서 더….<end>"

## 2140005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec214-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…彼に会うのが<line-break>怖くなってしまったのです。<end>"
EN: "…I became afraid to see him.<end>"
KO: "…그를 만나는 게 무서워졌습니다.<end>"

## 2140006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec214-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…そう。怖いのです。<line-break>私たちエルフは人間と<line-break>比較にならないほど長命です。<line-break>いつか彼は、私を残して<line-break>先に死んでしまう…。<line-break>そして、私だけが生き続けていく。<line-break>ある日突然、彼がいなくなり、<line-break>幸せだった昨日までが嘘のように消え<line-break>悲しみの日々が始まるのです。<line-break>心に穴が空いたまま何千年と続く歳月を<line-break>苦しみと哀しみを背負いながら、無為に<line-break>生き続けなければならないのです…。<end>"
EN: "…Yes. I am afraid. We Elves live far longer than humans. Someday he will die before me, leaving me behind… and I alone will go on living. Then one day, he will suddenly be gone. All the happiness I had known until the day before will vanish as though it had all been a lie, and my days of sorrow will begin. For thousands of years, I will have to go on living a pointless existence, with a hole in my heart, bearing all that pain and grief…<end>"
KO: "…그래요. 무서운 겁니다. 우리 엘프는 인간과 비교할 수 없을 만큼 오래 삽니다. 언젠가 그는 나를 남겨 두고 먼저 죽겠지요…. 그러고도 나만 계속 살아가야 합니다. 어느 날 갑자기 그가 사라지고 행복했던 어제까지가 거짓말처럼 사라져 슬픔의 나날이 시작됩니다. 가슴에 구멍이 난 채 수천 년의 세월을 고통과 슬픔을 짊어지고 의미 없이 계속 살아가야 하는 겁니다….<end>"

## 2140007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec214-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…そんなこと、<line-break>私には…とても耐えられません。<line-break>　<line-break>それで、彼に会えなくなって<line-break>しまったのです。<line-break>　<line-break>愛の先に待っている絶望が、<line-break>私は恐ろしいのです。<end>"
EN: "…I could never bear that. That is why I could no longer bring myself to see him. I am terrified of the despair waiting at the end of our love.<end>"
KO: "…그런 건 저는… 도저히 견딜 수 없어요. 그래서 그를 만나지 못하게 됐습니다. 사랑 끝에 기다리는 절망이 저는 두렵습니다.<end>"

## 2140008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec214-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "異種族間で恋を成就させることは<line-break>相互理解や共存にも増して難しい…。<line-break>　<line-break>その点、お主は立派じゃ…。<end>"
EN: "For two people of different races to find fulfillment in love is harder even than achieving mutual understanding or coexistence… In that respect, you have done admirably.<end>"
KO: "이종족 사이의 사랑을 이루는 건 상호 이해나 공존보다도 어렵지…. 그 점에서 넌 훌륭하다….<end>"

## 2140009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec214-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "恋心で理性を失うこともなく<line-break>ちゃんと踏みとどまっておる。<line-break>　<line-break>激情にかられ、ダークエルフに<line-break>身を落とすこともなく、な。<end>"
EN: "You have not let love rob you of reason. You have held yourself back, rather than surrendering to passion and falling so far as to become a Dark Elf.<end>"
KO: "연심에 이성을 잃지도 않고 제대로 멈춰 섰군. 격정에 휩쓸려 다크엘프로 타락하지도 않았고 말이지.<end>"

## 2140011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec214-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "しかし明日も、明後日も<line-break>踏みとどまっておられる保証はない。<line-break>　<line-break>まずは彼に、<line-break>お主のすべての想いを<line-break>包み隠さずぶつけてみぬか？<end>"
EN: "But there is no guarantee you will be able to hold back tomorrow, or the day after. Why not begin by telling him everything you feel, without concealing a thing?<end>"
KO: "하지만 내일도 모레도 버텨 줄 거라는 보장은 없다. 우선 그에게 네 모든 마음을 숨김없이 부딪쳐 보는 게 어떻겠느냐?<end>"

## 2140012
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec214-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…そうですね。おっしゃるとおりです。<end>"
EN: "…Yes. You are right.<end>"
KO: "…그렇군요. 말씀하신 대로입니다.<end>"

## 2140013
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec214-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "彼に会ったら伝えていただけますか？<line-break>私はここで待っています、と。<end>"
EN: "If you see him, would you tell him that I am waiting here?<end>"
KO: "그를 만나면 전해 주시겠어요? 저는 여기서 기다리고 있다고.<end>"

## 2140014
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec214-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "種族の違いを越え、つながりを<line-break>求めている者たちを見ておると<line-break>ひと肌脱いでやりたくなってな。<line-break>まったく年甲斐もない。<line-break>お節介焼きの神官じゃわい。<end>"
EN: "When I see people reaching across the divide between races in search of a bond, I cannot help wanting to lend a hand. At my age, I should know better. What a meddlesome priest I am.<end>"
KO: "종족의 차이를 넘어 인연을 바라는 자들을 보고 있으면 한몫 거들고 싶어져서 말이지. 나이값도 못하는 참견쟁이 신관이군.<end>"

## 2140015
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec214-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "異種族間の７用終端<end>"
EN: ""
KO: "이종족 간 7용 끝<end>"

EN: ""

## 2150001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec215-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よぉ！　待ってたぜ。<end>"
EN: "Hey! I've been waiting for you.<end>"
KO: "여어! 기다렸다고.<end>"

## 2150003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec215-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうか、とにかく無事なんだな？<line-break>よかった…。<line-break>　<line-break>…でも、じゃあなぜ俺は<line-break>彼女と会えなかったんだ？<end>"
EN: "So she's safe, at least? Thank goodness... But then why couldn't I see her?<end>"
KO: "그래, 어쨌든 무사한 거지? 다행이다…. …그런데 그렇다면 왜 나는 그녀를 만나지 못했던 거지?<end>"

## 2150004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec215-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なんてこった。まさか…。<line-break>彼女がそこまで苦しんでいたなんて。<end>"
EN: "Damn... I can't believe she was suffering that much.<end>"
KO: "이럴 수가. 설마… 그녀가 그렇게까지 괴로워하고 있었다니.<end>"

## 2150005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec215-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まいったな…。<line-break>俺、頭悪いから、そんなこと<line-break>考えもしなかった…。<end>"
EN: "Damn... I'm not too bright, so it never even crossed my mind.<end>"
KO: "큰일이군… 난 머리가 나빠서 그런 건 생각도 못 했어…<end>"

## 2150006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec215-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…でも、彼女が言っていることも<line-break>当然だよな。<line-break>　<line-break>確かに彼女と俺とじゃ、<line-break>住む世界だけじゃなく<line-break>寿命も違う…。<line-break>まったく別の種族なんだもんな…。<end>"
EN: "Still, it's only natural she'd say that. She and I don't just live in different worlds; our lifespans are different, too... We're completely different races...<end>"
KO: "…하지만 그녀가 하는 말도 당연하긴 하지. 확실히 그녀와 나는 사는 세계뿐 아니라 수명도 달라…. 완전히 다른 종족이니까….<end>"

## 2150007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec215-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やれやれ。わしとしたことが<line-break>とんだ見こみ違いじゃったかな。<line-break>お主、もうあきらめるのか？<end>"
EN: "Good grief. Perhaps I misjudged you after all. Are you giving up already?<end>"
KO: "허허. 내가 크게 잘못 봤나 보군. 자네, 벌써 포기하는 건가?<end>"

## 2150008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec215-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "バカを言うな。俺は冒険者だぜ。<line-break>冒険者ってのは困難に立ち向かってこそ<line-break>真価を発揮できるってもんだろ？<line-break>フィアは俺にとって<line-break>この世で最も大切で<line-break>かけがえのない宝物だ。<line-break>どんな逆境や困難があろうと<line-break>絶対に手放したりはしない。<end>"
EN: "Don't be ridiculous. I'm an adventurer. It's when we face hardships head-on that we prove our worth, right? Fia is the most precious, irreplaceable treasure in the world to me. No matter what hardship or adversity stands in my way, I'll never let her go.<end>"
KO: "바보 같은 소리 마. 난 모험자야. 모험자는 어려움에 맞서야 진가를 발휘하는 법 아니겠어? 피아는 내게 이 세상에서 가장 소중하고 무엇과도 바꿀 수 없는 보물이야. 어떤 역경이나 어려움이 있어도 절대 놓지 않아.<end>"

## 2150009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec215-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ありがとうよ、<value:$28>。<line-break>それにイオンズさん。<line-break>おかげで踏ん切りがついたぜ。<line-break>…シェーヌの森へ行ってくる。<end>"
EN: "Thanks, <value:$28>. And you too, Mr. Ions. You've helped me make up my mind. I'm going to Chene Forest.<end>"
KO: "고맙다, <value:$28>. 그리고 이온즈 씨도. 덕분에 결심이 섰어. …셰느 숲으로 가볼게.<end>"

## 2150010
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec215-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "手を貸してはならんぞ。<line-break>今があのふたりにとって肝心な時じゃ。<line-break>　<line-break>魔物でさえ人間と共存できるんじゃ。<line-break>人とエルフにできぬ道理がなかろう。<end>"
EN: "You must not lend a hand. This is a crucial moment for those two. If even monsters can coexist with humans, there is no reason humans and Elves cannot do the same.<end>"
KO: "손을 빌려주면 안 된다. 지금이 저 둘에게 가장 중요한 때야. 마물조차 인간과 공존할 수 있는데 사람과 엘프가 못할 이유가 있겠나.<end>"

## 2150011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec215-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よぉ！　待ってたぜ。<end>"
EN: "Hey! I've been waiting for you.<end>"
KO: "여어! 기다렸다고.<end>"

## 2150017
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec215-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "もう彼女のことはあきらめるのか、<line-break>だって？<line-break>見損なってもらっちゃ困るぜ。<line-break>種族が違おうと、寿命に差があろうと<line-break>それが何だってんだ。<line-break>彼女を想う気持ちに変わりはねえ。<end>"
EN: "You think I'm giving up on her? You should know me better than that. So what if we're different races, or our lifespans aren't the same? It doesn't change how I feel about her.<end>"
KO: "이제 그 여자를 포기하는 거냐고? 날 너무 얕보면 곤란하지. 종족이 다르든 수명이 다르든 그게 뭐 어때. 그녀를 생각하는 마음은 변하지 않아.<end>"

## 2150019
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec215-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…ちょっくら森に行ってくる。<line-break>どうなるか自分でもわかんねえが、<line-break>帰ったら一杯やろうぜ。<end>"
EN: "I'm off to the forest for a bit. I don't know how this'll turn out, but when I get back, let's have a drink.<end>"
KO: "…잠깐 숲에 다녀올게. 어떻게 될지는 나도 모르겠지만 돌아오면 한잔하자고.<end>"

## 2160001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec216-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…驚いたな。こりゃ、<line-break>本当に神様ってもんが<line-break>いるのかもしれねえな…。<line-break>この旅立ちの時に<line-break><value:$28>と会えるなんてよ。<end>"
EN: "Well, I'll be damned. Maybe there really are gods after all. Running into you just as we're setting off, <value:$28>...<end>"
KO: "…놀랍군. 정말 신이라는 게 있을지도 모르겠어…. 여행을 떠나는 이때 <value:$28>과 만나다니 말이야.<end>"

## 2160002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec216-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "すまなかった。本当はあんたに<line-break>すぐ報告したかったんだが<line-break>出航が急だったんでな。<line-break>つい連絡しそびれちまった。<end>"
EN: "Sorry. I meant to tell you right away, but the ship was leaving on such short notice that I never got the chance to send word.<end>"
KO: "미안했다. 사실 바로 보고하고 싶었는데 출항이 급해서 연락을 못 했어.<end>"

## 2160003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec216-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "実は、あの後シェーヌの森で<line-break>ふたりで話し合ったんだ…。<line-break>　<line-break>たとえ俺が死んでも、その時は多分、<line-break>そばには俺たちの子どもがいるはずだ。<end>"
EN: "The truth is, we talked it all through afterward in Chene Forest. Even if I die, our children will probably be there by her side when it happens.<end>"
KO: "사실 그 뒤 셰느의 숲에서 둘이 이야기를 나눴어… 설령 내가 죽어도 그때는 아마 곁에 우리 아이가 있을 거야.<end>"

## 2160004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec216-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だったら、自分の分まで<line-break>その子どもたちを<line-break>愛してくれればいいって<line-break>彼が、そう言ってくれたんです。<end>"
EN: "He said that if that happened, I should love our children enough for both of us.<end>"
KO: "그렇다면 자기 몫까지 그 아이들을 사랑해 주면 된다고 그 사람이 말해 줬어요.<end>"

## 2160005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec216-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ガラにもないことを言っちまったが、<line-break>ま、そういうわけだ。<line-break>　<line-break>結局、ふたりでとことん<line-break>話し合ってさ、<line-break>一緒になるって決めたよ。<line-break>しがらみのない別の大陸に渡って、<line-break>そこで一から築くんだ。<line-break>俺たちの新しい世界を…。<end>"
EN: "Not the sort of thing you'd expect to hear from me, I know, but there it is. We talked until there was nothing left unsaid, and in the end, we decided to be together. We're crossing to another continent, somewhere free of all the ties that bind us here. We'll start from nothing and build a new world of our own.<end>"
KO: "나답지 않은 말을 해 버렸지만, 뭐 그런 거야. 결국 둘이서 끝까지 이야기를 나누고 함께하기로 했어. 얽매일 게 없는 다른 대륙으로 건너가 거기서 처음부터 만들어 갈 거야. 우리들의 새로운 세상을….<end>"

## 2160006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec216-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ありがとう、<value:$28>。<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>あんたには本当に世話になった。<line-break>ちゃんと別れの挨拶ができて<line-break>うれしいぜ…。<end>"
EN: "Thank you, <value:$28>. You've done more for me than I can say. I'm glad I got the chance to say a proper goodbye.<end>"
KO: "고마워, <value:$28>. 네게 정말 신세 많이 졌어. 제대로 작별 인사를 할 수 있어서 기쁘다….<end>"

## 2160007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec216-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "また世界のどこかで会おうぜ、<line-break><value:$28>！<end>"
EN: "We'll meet again somewhere in this world, <value:$28>!<end>"
KO: "또 세상 어딘가에서 만나자, <value:$28>!<end>"

## 2160008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec216-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なあに、心配には及ばん。<line-break>きっとうまくいくじゃろう。<line-break>　<line-break>あのふたり…。<line-break>実にいい顔をしておったからのう。<line-break>　<line-break>彼らの旅先に幸多からんことを<line-break>わしらも祈ろうではないか。<end>"
EN: "Come now, there is no need to worry. I am sure all will be well. Those two looked truly happy. Let us pray that good fortune attends them on their journey.<end>"
KO: "걱정할 것 없네. 분명 잘될 게야. 그 둘… 정말 좋은 얼굴을 하고 있었거든. 그들의 여행길에 많은 행복이 있기를 우리도 빌어 주자고.<end>"

## 2160009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec216-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "大丈夫かしら、あのふたり…。<line-break>エルフと人間がうまくいったなんて話、<line-break>聞いたことがないけど。<end>"
EN: "I wonder if those two will be all right. I've never heard of things working out between an Elf and a human.<end>"
KO: "괜찮으려나, 저 두 사람…. 엘프와 인간이 잘된다는 이야기는 들어 본 적이 없는데.<end>"

## 2160010
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec216-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…でも、幸せになってほしいわね。<end>"
EN: "Still, I hope they'll be happy.<end>"
KO: "…그래도 행복해졌으면 좋겠어.<end>"

## 2160011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec216-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ルルアンタ、あのふたりは<line-break>きっとうまくいくと思うな。<line-break>だって、とっても幸せそうだったもん。<line-break>ルルアンタ、昔フリントさんから<line-break>教わったことがあるの。<line-break>　<line-break>どんなに困難な旅路だって、<line-break>信頼し合っている者同士なら<line-break>ちっとも恐くないって。<line-break>うん。あのふたりなら<line-break>きっと大丈夫だよ！<end>"
EN: "Lulluantha thinks those two will be just fine. They looked so happy together! Flint taught Lulluantha something a long time ago: no journey is frightening, no matter how hard it is, as long as you trust each other. Mm-hm. Those two will be just fine!<end>"
KO: "루루안타는 저 두 사람이 분명 잘될 거라고 생각해. 정말 행복해 보였거든. 루루안타가 예전에 플린트 씨한테 들은 게 있어. 아무리 힘든 여행길이라도 서로 믿는 사람끼리라면 전혀 무섭지 않다고. 응, 저 두 사람이라면 분명 괜찮을 거야!<end>"

EN: ""
KO: "이종족 간 9용 끝<end>"

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

KO: "으랴!<end>"

## 2170003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec217-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なかなかいい動きをしていますね。<line-break>どなたかに師事されたのですか？<end>"
EN: "You move quite well. Did someone train you?<end>"
KO: "움직임이 꽤 좋군요. 누구에게 사사받으셨습니까?<end>"

## 2170004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec217-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "誰にも教わってねえよ。<line-break>我流だ！<line-break>　<line-break>裏山で魔物ぶちのめすとか<line-break>村でもケンカばっかしてたから<line-break>自然と身についたんだろうな。<end>"
EN: "Nobody taught me. I'm self-taught! I used to beat up monsters in the hills behind the village, and I was always getting into fights back home. Guess I just picked it up along the way.<end>"
KO: "아무에게도 배운 적 없어. 아류다! 뒷산에서 마물 때려잡고 마을에서도 맨날 싸움질했더니 자연스럽게 익힌 거겠지.<end>"

## 2170005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec217-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それにしちゃ筋がいいわね。<line-break>素質があるのかも。<line-break>どう、ひとつ手合わせしてみない？<end>"
EN: "You're awfully good for someone with no training. Maybe you have a gift for it. How about a sparring match?<end>"
KO: "그런 것치고는 제법 잘하는데. 소질이 있을지도 몰라. 어때, 한번 겨뤄 볼래?<end>"

## 2170006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec217-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "構わねえけどよ。<line-break>熱くなっちまったら、俺<line-break>女相手でも手加減できないぜ？<end>"
EN: "Fine by me. But once I get fired up, I can't hold back, even against a woman.<end>"
KO: "상관없긴 한데. 열받기 시작하면 난 여자 상대라도 봐주지 못한다고?<end>"

## 2170007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec217-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "言うじゃない。楽しみだわ。<end>"
EN: "Big talk. This should be fun.<end>"
KO: "말은 잘하네. 기대되는데.<end>"

## 2170008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec217-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よっしゃ！<line-break>んじゃ、いくぜ！<end>"
EN: "All right! Here I come!<end>"
KO: "좋아! 그럼 간다!<end>"

## 2170009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec217-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ぜえっ、ぜえっ。<line-break>はあっ、はあっ…。<end>"
EN: "Huff... huff... Hah... hah...<end>"
KO: "헉, 헉. 하아, 하아….<end>"

## 2170010
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec217-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "っかーっ！　まいったぜ！<line-break>まるで通じやしねえ！<end>"
EN: "Whew! I give up! Nothing I did even touched you!<end>"
KO: "크아! 참 안 되네! 전혀 통하지 않아!<end>"

## 2170011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec217-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "くすっ。<line-break>ヴィア相手にあそこまで<line-break>善戦できればたいしたものです。<end>"
EN: "Hee hee. Holding your own that well against Via is quite impressive.<end>"
KO: "후후. 비아를 상대로 그 정도 선전했다면 대단한 겁니다.<end>"

## 2170012
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec217-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "拳闘だけに、健闘したってか？<line-break>あはははは。<end>"
EN: "So I put up a good fight in a fistfight? Hahahaha!<end>"
KO: "권투라서 건투했다는 거야? 아하하하하.<end>"

## 2170015
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec217-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "えっと、あ…あとは経験でしょう。<line-break>場数を踏み、死線を越えたぶんだけ<line-break>人は強くなりますから。<end>このまま鍛え上げていけば、<line-break>いつの日か語り継がれる冒険者に<line-break>なれるかもしれません。<end>"
EN: "Um, well... What you need now is experience. The more battles you fight and the more brushes with death you survive, the stronger you become.<end>If you keep training like this, you may someday become an adventurer whose name is remembered for generations.<end>"
KO: "음, 아… 나머지는 경험이겠죠. 실전을 겪고 사선을 넘은 만큼 사람은 강해지니까요.<end>이대로 단련해 나가면 언젠가 길이 전해지는 모험가가 될지도 모릅니다.<end>"

## 2170016
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec217-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おおっ！　なんかそう聞いたら<line-break>がぜん、やる気が出てきたぜ。<line-break>もう一本、頼む。<end>"
EN: "Oh! Hearing that really fires me up. Come on, one more round!<end>"
KO: "오오! 그렇게 듣고 나니 갑자기 의욕이 솟는데. 한 잔 더 부탁해.<end>"

## 2170017
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec217-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いいわよ。あなたが音を上げるまで<line-break>つきあってあげるわ。<end>"
EN: "Gladly. I'll keep going until you give up.<end>"
KO: "좋아. 네가 두 손 들 때까지 상대해 줄게.<end>"

## 2190002

## 2190018
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec219-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "マゴスの影に怯えていた頃は<line-break>ずっと気持ちが張り詰めていて<line-break>　<line-break>見知らぬ誰かのために<line-break>自分から何かをしてあげるなんて<line-break>余裕はなかったかも…。<end>"
EN: "Back when I lived in fear of Magos's shadow, I was always so tense. Maybe I simply had no room to go out of my way for a complete stranger...<end>"
KO: "마고스의 그림자를 두려워하던 때는 늘 긴장해서 모르는 누군가를 위해 내가 먼저 무언가를 해줄 여유가 없었을지도 몰라…<end>"

## 2190019
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec219-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ほんと、不思議ね。<line-break>私にそんな一面があったなんて<line-break>自分でも驚いているわ。<end>"
EN: "It really is strange. I never knew I had this side to me. Even I'm surprised.<end>"
KO: "정말 신기하네. 내게 그런 면이 있었다니 나도 놀랐어.<end>"

## 2190020
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec219-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "この気持ちをもう少し<line-break>味わっていたいし<line-break>少しそのあたりを散策してくるわね。<end>"
EN: "I'd like to savor this feeling a little longer, so I think I'll take a walk around here.<end>"
KO: "이 기분을 조금 더 느끼고 싶고, 근처를 좀 산책하고 올게.<end>"

EN: ""
KO: "미아 이벤트 2용 종료<end>"

## 2200001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec220-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うぇーん、うぇーん！<end>"
EN: "Waaah! Waaah!<end>"
KO: "으아앙, 으아앙!<end>"

## 2200002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec220-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どうして、泣いてるの？<line-break>ルルアンタに話してみて。<end>"
EN: "Why are you crying? Tell Lulluantha what happened.<end>"
KO: "왜 울고 있어? 루루안타에게 말해 봐.<end>"

## 2200003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec220-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あのね、あのね、お母さんがいないの。<line-break>ねこちゃん、追っかけてたら、<line-break>お母さんがいなくなったの。<end>"
EN: "Well, um... Mama's gone. I was chasing a kitty, and then Mama was gone!<end>"
KO: "저기, 저기, 엄마가 없어. 고양이를 쫓아가다 보니까 엄마가 없어졌어.<end>"

## 2200004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec220-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうなんだ。<line-break>まいごになっちゃったんだね。<end>"
EN: "Oh, I see. You got lost, didn't you?<end>"
KO: "그랬구나. 길을 잃어버렸구나.<end>"

## 2200005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec220-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "涙はキレイだけど、<line-break>笑った顔はもっともっと<line-break>キレイだと思うよ。<end>"
EN: "Your tears are pretty, but I think your smile would be much, much prettier.<end>"
KO: "눈물도 예쁘지만 웃는 얼굴은 훨씬 더 예쁘다고 생각해.<end>"

## 2200006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec220-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうだよ。<line-break>おひさまみたいに笑ったら<line-break>きっと、げんきがでてくるよ。<end>"
EN: "That's right. Smile bright as the sun, and I'm sure you'll start feeling better.<end>"
KO: "그래. 해님처럼 웃으면 분명 힘이 날 거야.<end>"

## 2200007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec220-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うわー、すごーい！<end>"
EN: "Wow! That's amazing!<end>"
KO: "우와, 대단해ー!<end>"

## 2200008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec220-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いいぞ。いいぞ！<end>"
EN: "That's it! Keep it up!<end>"
KO: "좋아. 좋아!<end>"

## 2200009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec220-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なかなか面白いじゃないか。<end>"
EN: "Well, this is quite entertaining.<end>"
KO: "꽤 재밌잖아.<end>"

## 2200010
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec220-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ああ、よかった。ここにいたのね。<end>"
EN: "Oh, thank goodness. There you are!<end>"
KO: "아, 다행이다. 여기 있었구나.<end>"

## 2200011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec220-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お母さん！<end>"
EN: "Mama!<end>"
KO: "엄마!<end>"

## 2200012
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec220-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ありがとうございました。<line-break>この子の姿が見えなくなって<line-break>ずっと捜していたんです。<end>"
EN: "Thank you so much. I lost sight of this little one and have been searching ever since.<end>"
KO: "감사합니다. 이 아이가 보이지 않아서 계속 찾고 있었어요.<end>"

## 2200013
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec220-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ほんとにもう、この年頃になると<line-break>目が離せなくて…。<line-break>どうもお世話様でした。<end>"
EN: "Honestly, children this age need watching every second... Thank you again for all your help.<end>"
KO: "정말이지, 이 나이쯤 되면 눈을 뗄 수가 없어서…. 신세 많이 졌습니다.<end>"

## 2200014
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec220-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ありがとう。<line-break>お歌とおどり、とってもたのしかったよ。<end>"
EN: "Thank you! The singing and dancing were lots of fun!<end>"
KO: "고마워. 노래랑 춤, 정말 재미있었어.<end>"

## 2200015
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec220-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "子供か…。そう言えば、<line-break>もうあの子よりも<line-break>大きくなっているのかな…。<end>"
EN: "A child... Come to think of it, I wonder if the child I remember has grown bigger than her by now...<end>"
KO: "아이인가…. 그러고 보니, 이제 그 아이보다도더 커졌으려나….<end>"

## 2200016
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec220-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どうしたの？<end>"
EN: "What's wrong?<end>"
KO: "무슨 일이야?<end>"

## 2200017
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec220-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なんでもないよ。<line-break>それより何か食べに行かない？<line-break>もうお腹がぺこぺこだよ。<end>"
EN: "It's nothing. Anyway, want to get something to eat? I'm starving.<end>"
KO: "아무것도 아니야. 그것보다 뭐 먹으러 가지 않을래? 배가 완전히 고팠어.<end>"

## 2210003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec221-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "迷子のようです。<line-break>泣きやむ様子がありません。<end>"
EN: "He appears to be lost. He shows no sign of stopping crying.<end>"
KO: "길을 잃은 모양입니다. 울음을 그칠 기미가 없습니다.<end>"

## 2210004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec221-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "この子はどうして泣いているのですか？<end>"
EN: "Why is this child crying?<end>"
KO: "이 아이는 왜 울고 있는 건가요?<end>"

## 2210005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec221-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "母親と離れてしまったさびしさから<line-break>心理的に不安定になり、<line-break>そのため、泣いているのかと。<end>"
EN: "I believe the loneliness of being separated from his mother has left him emotionally unsettled, causing him to cry.<end>"
KO: "어머니와 떨어져 외로운 탓에 심리적으로 불안정해졌고, 그래서 울고 있는 것 같습니다.<end>"

## 2210006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec221-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あのねー。<line-break>冷静に分析するより先に<line-break>やることがあるでしょ！<end>"
EN: "Listen, before you start calmly analyzing him, don't you think there's something else we ought to do?<end>"
KO: "있잖아. 냉정하게 분석하기 전에 해야 할 일이 있잖아!<end>"

## 2210007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec221-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "こら。男の子がめったなことで<line-break>泣くもんじゃないぞ。<line-break>　<line-break>大丈夫。ボクがお母さんを<line-break>見つけてつれてきてあげるから。<end>"
EN: "Hey, now. A boy shouldn't cry over every little thing. Don't worry. I'll find your mother and bring her back to you.<end>"
KO: "자, 남자아이가 함부로 우는 거 아니야. 괜찮아. 내가 엄마를 찾아서 데려와 줄 테니까.<end>"

## 2210008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec221-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ほんと？　お姉ちゃんが<line-break>お母さんをつれてきてくれるの？<end>"
EN: "Really? You'll bring Mommy back, miss?<end>"
KO: "정말? 언니가 엄마를 데려와 주는 거야?<end>"

## 2210009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec221-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うん、約束するよ。<end>"
EN: "Yes. I promise.<end>"
KO: "응, 약속할게.<end>"

## 2210010
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec221-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ってなわけだから、<line-break>この子のこと、頼んだよ。<end>"
EN: "So that's that. I'm leaving him with you.<end>"
KO: "그런 사정이니까 이 아이를 부탁해.<end>"

## 2210011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec221-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さ、行こ、<value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "Come on, <value:$28>. Let's go.<end>"
KO: "자, 가자, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 2210013
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec221-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あの…。<line-break>こういう時はどうすれば<line-break>よろしいのでしょうか？<end>"
EN: "Um... What am I supposed to do in a situation like this?<end>"
KO: "저기… 이럴 때는 어떻게 하면 좋을까요?<end>"

## 2210014
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec221-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "すみません。私に訊かれても…。<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>………………。<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>ひとまず、あやしてみては<line-break>どうでしょうか？<end>"
EN: "I'm sorry, but I wouldn't know... Perhaps you could try soothing him for now?<end>"
KO: "죄송합니다. 저한테 물으셔도…. ………………. 우선 달래 보는 건 어떨까요?<end>"

## 2210015
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec221-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あやすとは、どういうことを<line-break>すればよいのですか？<end>"
EN: "What exactly does soothing him entail?<end>"
KO: "달랜다는 건 뭘 하면 되는 겁니까?<end>"

## 2210016
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec221-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうですね。<line-break>たとえば笑わせたり、<line-break>お話をしてなぐさめてあげたり…。<end>"
EN: "Well... We could try making him laugh, or talking to him to put him at ease.<end>"
KO: "그렇군요. 예를 들면 웃게 해 주거나, 이야기를 하며 달래 주거나….<end>"

## 2210017
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec221-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わかりました。<line-break>笑わせればよいのですね。<line-break>やれるだけ、やってみます。<end>"
EN: "Understood. I need only make him laugh. I shall do what I can.<end>"
KO: "알겠습니다. 웃게 하면 되는 거군요. 할 수 있는 만큼 해 보겠습니다.<end>"

## 2210018
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec221-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "びぇーん、びぇーん！<line-break>お母さん、こわいよーっ！<end>"
EN: "Waaah! Waaah! Mommy, I'm scared!<end>"
KO: "으아앙, 으아앙! 엄마, 무서워~!<end>"

## 2210019
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec221-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どうなってるの？<line-break>なんか、さっきより<line-break>泣いてるみたいだけど…。<end>"
EN: "What's going on? He looks like he's crying even harder than before.<end>"
KO: "어떻게 된 거야? 왠지 아까보다 더 울고 있는 것 같은데….<end>"

## 2210020
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec221-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いろいろと試みてはみたのですが…。<end>"
EN: "All sorts of things were tried, but...<end>"
KO: "여러 가지로 시도해 보긴 했습니다만….<end>"

## 2210021
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec221-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "難しいものですね…。<end>"
EN: "This is rather difficult...<end>"
KO: "어려운 일이네요….<end>"

## 2210022
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec221-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お母さん！<end>"
EN: "Mommy!<end>"
KO: "엄마!<end>"

## 2210023
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec221-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ここにいたのね。<line-break>心配したわよ。<end>"
EN: "There you are! I was so worried.<end>"
KO: "여기 있었구나. 걱정했잖아.<end>"

## 2210024
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec221-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どうも、すみませんでした。<line-break>ありがとうございます。<end>"
EN: "I'm terribly sorry for the trouble. Thank you so much.<end>"
KO: "정말 죄송했습니다. 감사합니다.<end>"

## 2210025
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec221-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あなたもちゃんとお礼を言いなさい。<line-break>この人たちがお母さんを<line-break>案内してくれたのよ。<end>"
EN: "You should thank them properly, too. These people showed Mommy where to find you.<end>"
KO: "너도 제대로 감사 인사를 해야지. 이분들이 엄마를 안내해 주셨단다.<end>"

## 2210026
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec221-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ありがとー！　お姉ちゃん。<end>"
EN: "Thank you, miss!<end>"
KO: "고마워-! 누나.<end>"

## 2210027
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec221-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "本物のフレアも<line-break>あんなふうに子供の頃が<line-break>あったのでしょうか？<end>"
EN: "I wonder if the real Flare was ever a child like that.<end>"
KO: "진짜 플레어도 저렇게 어린 시절이 있었을까요?<end>"

## 2210028
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec221-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "子供というのは不思議なものですね…。<end>"
EN: "Children truly are mysterious...<end>"
KO: "아이란 참 신기한 존재군요….<end>"

## 2220001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec222-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うぇーん、うぇーん！<line-break>痛いよーっ！<end>"
EN: "Waaah! It hurts!<end>"
KO: "으앙, 으앙! 아파아!<end>"

## 2220002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec222-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どうしたの？<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>ああ、転んでケガしちゃったんだ。<line-break>痛かったね～。<line-break>ほら、お姉ちゃんに見せてみて？<end>"
EN: "What's wrong? Oh, you fell and hurt yourself. That must have hurt. Here, let me have a look.<end>"
KO: "왜 그래? 아, 넘어져서 다쳤구나. 아팠지~. 자, 언니한테 보여 줄래?<end>"

## 2220003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec222-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よく泣きやんだね。えらいえらい。<line-break>もうちょっとガマンしててね。<line-break>今、手当てしてあげるから。<end>"
EN: "Good, you stopped crying. What a brave boy. Just hold still a little longer, all right? I'll patch you up.<end>"
KO: "잘 울음 그쳤네. 착하다, 착해. 조금만 더 참아. 지금 치료해 줄게.<end>"

## 2220004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec222-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ほら。お兄ちゃんもヴァンも<line-break>ボーッと突っ立っていないで<line-break>何かしてよ。<end>"
EN: "Come on, big brother. You and Van, stop standing around and do something.<end>"
KO: "자. 오빠도 반도멍하니 서 있지 말고뭐라도 해 봐.<end>"

## 2220005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec222-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "何かって…。<line-break>何すりゃいいんだよ？<end>"
EN: "Do something? Like what?<end>"
KO: "뭘 하라니…. 뭘 하면 되는데?<end>"

## 2220006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec222-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "たとえば、<line-break>気を紛らわせてあげれば、<line-break>少しは痛みも和らぐでしょ。<line-break>何か話をしてあげるとか、<line-break>面白いことをして見せるとかさ？<end>"
EN: "Distract him, for example. That might take his mind off the pain. Tell him a story, or do something funny for him.<end>"
KO: "예를 들어 다른 데 신경 쓰게 해 주면 조금은 아픔이 가시잖아. 뭔가 이야기를 해 준다든지, 재미있는 걸 보여 준다든지?<end>"

## 2220007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec222-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そんなことなら<line-break>おやすい御用だぜ。<line-break>なあ、<value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "That'll be easy. Right, <value:$28>?<end>"
KO: "그런 거라면 식은 죽 먹기지. 안 그래, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 2220008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec222-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やめとけ、<value:$28>。<line-break>そんな難しい冒険の話、<line-break>子供にわかるわけねえって。<end>"
EN: "Forget it, <value:$28>. A kid's never going to follow some complicated adventure story like that.<end>"
KO: "그만둬, <value:$28>. 그런 어려운 모험 이야기를 애가 알 리 없잖아.<end>"

## 2220009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec222-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いいから俺に任せとけって。<line-break>こういうのなら<line-break>得意中の得意なんだ。<end>"
EN: "Just leave it to me. This is my specialty.<end>"
KO: "됐으니까 나한테 맡겨. 이런 건 내가 제일 잘하거든.<end>"

## 2220010
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec222-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いいか、坊主。<line-break>今度はお兄ちゃんが<line-break>とっておきのやつを見せてやるぜ。<end>"
EN: "All right, kid. Your big brother's about to show you his best trick.<end>"
KO: "잘 봐라, 꼬마. 이번엔 형이비장의 걸 보여 주마.<end>"

## 2220011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec222-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

EN: "Hup! Hah! Hyah!<end>"

## 2220012
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec222-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そらよっと！<end>"
EN: "And there!<end>"
KO: "자, 간다!<end>"

## 2220013
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec222-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "男の子だけに、ダンシングってな！<line-break>ウププ…。<end>"
EN: "A little boy deserves a little boy-gie! Heh heh...<end>"
KO: "남자아이니까, 댄싱이라 이거지! 우푸푸….<end>"

## 2220016
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec222-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アハハハハハ。<line-break>どうだ、痛みもフッ飛んだだろ？<end>"
EN: "Hahahahaha! Well? Bet you forgot all about the pain, huh?<end>"
KO: "아하하하하. 어때, 아픈 것도 싹 날아갔지?<end>"

## 2220017
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec222-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さ、これでもう大丈夫。<end>"
EN: "There, all better now.<end>"
KO: "자, 이제 괜찮아.<end>"

## 2220018
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec222-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おねえちゃん、ありがとう。<end>"
EN: "Thank you, miss.<end>"
KO: "누나, 고마워.<end>"

## 2220019
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec222-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ありがとう。<end>"
EN: "Thank you.<end>"
KO: "고마워.<end>"

## 2220021
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec222-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あの子、照れちまって、<line-break>お礼言うの忘れたんだな…。<end>"
EN: "Guess he got shy and forgot to thank me...<end>"
KO: "그 애, 부끄러워서 감사 인사를 잊었나 보네….<end>"

## 2220022
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec222-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "でも、俺を見る目がどことなく<line-break>悲しげな感じがしたんだけど<line-break>なんだったんだろう…。<end>"
EN: "Still, there was something almost sad about the way he looked at me. I wonder what that was about...<end>"
KO: "그런데 날 바라보는 눈빛이 어딘가 슬퍼 보였어. 대체 뭐였을까….<end>"

## 2220023
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec222-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ほら。<value:$28>もヴァンも<line-break>ボーッと突っ立っていないで<line-break>何かしてよ。<end>"
EN: "Come on, <value:$28>. You and Van, stop standing around and do something.<end>"
KO: "자. <value:$28>도 반도 멍하니 서 있지 말고 뭐라도 해.<end>"

## 2230001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec223-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "感じるぞ、乙女が助けを呼ぶ声を！<line-break>ふっ、熱き魂の伝説がまたひとつ、<line-break>俺様のものになりたがってるようだな。<line-break>ふははは、乙女よ、待っているがいい！<line-break>未来の勇者は、悲しむ女性を裏切らん！<line-break>場所は確か聖光石の廃坑だったな！<end>"
EN: "I sense it: the voice of a maiden calling for help! Ha! It seems another legend of a fiery soul yearns to be mine. Hahahaha! Wait for me, maiden! A future Hero never fails a woman in distress! It was the abandoned Holy Light Stone mine, if I remember correctly!<end>"
KO: "느껴진다, 소녀가 도움을 부르는 목소리가! 후, 뜨거운 혼의 전설이 또 하나 이 나의 것이 되고 싶어 하는 모양이군. 후하하하, 소녀여 기다려라! 미래의 용자는 슬퍼하는 여인을 저버리지 않는다! 장소는 분명 성광석 폐광이었지!<end>"

## 2230002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec223-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なんなんだよ、あいつはいきなり！？<line-break>相変わらず、わけわかんねー。<line-break>　<line-break>でも、あいつまた何か<line-break>ろくでもないこと<line-break>しでかしそうな予感がするぜ…。<line-break>どうする？　ほっとくのか？<end>"
EN: "What's with Girldlan all of a sudden?! Same as ever, none of it makes any damn sense. Still, I've got a bad feeling Girldlan's about to get up to no good again... What do we do? Stay out of it?<end>"
KO: "대체 뭐야, 저 녀석 갑자기!? 여전히 이해가 안 되네. 그래도 또 뭔가 쓸데없는 일을 벌일 것 같은 예감이 들어…. 어떻게 할래? 내버려 둘 거야?<end>"

## 2230003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec223-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "心配だから追いかける<end>"
EN: "I'm worried, so let's go after Girldlan.<end>"
KO: "걱정되니 뒤쫓아간다.<end>"

## 2230004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec223-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これ以上関わりたくない<end>"
EN: "I don't want to get involved any further.<end>"
KO: "더 이상 관여하고 싶지 않다<end>"

## 2230005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec223-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "マジかよ、面倒見がよすぎだぜ。<line-break>ま、でもそこが<value:$28>の<line-break>いいところか…。<line-break>とりあえず、そうと決まったら<line-break>さっさと追いかけようぜ。<line-break>　<line-break>聖光石の廃坑って言ってたから<line-break>今からでも間に合うはずだ。<end>"
EN: "Seriously? You're too kind for your own good. Then again, I guess that's what's good about <value:$28>... Anyway, now that that's settled, let's hurry and catch up. Girldlan said it was the abandoned Holy Light Stone mine, so we should still make it if we go now.<end>"
KO: "진짜냐, 너무 잘 챙겨 주는 거 아냐? 뭐, 그게 <value:$28>의 좋은 점이긴 하지…. 어쨌든 정했으면 빨리 뒤쫓자. 성광석 폐광이라고 했으니 지금 가도 늦지 않을 거야.<end>"

## 2230006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec223-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…だよなぁ、ほっとこう。<line-break>あいつと関わるだけ損だもんな。<line-break>　<line-break>どうせ、ろくでもないことに<line-break>決まってるよ。<end>"
EN: "Yeah, you're right. Let's leave it alone. Getting involved is nothing but trouble. Whatever's going on, it can't be anything good.<end>"
KO: "…그렇지, 내버려 두자. 저 녀석과 엮여 봐야 손해잖아. 어차피 변변찮은 일일 게 뻔해.<end>"

## 2230007

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "名脇役ガルドラン物語1用終端<end>"
EN: ""
KO: "명조연 갈드란 이야기 1용 끝<end>"

## 2240001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec224-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ウッ…、ウッ…。<end>"
EN: "*Sob... sob...*<end>"
KO: "윽…, 윽….<end>"

## 2240002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec224-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふははははは！<line-break>男は、乙女の涙の数だけ強くなる！<line-break>未来の勇者ガルドラン、ついに参上！<line-break>さあ、可憐な少女よ<line-break>すぐさま悪党どもから助けてやる！<line-break>思うさま美しい笑顔の準備をするのだ。<line-break>未来の勇者の熱き魂を感じろ！<line-break>俺様に助けられる幸運をかみしめろ！<line-break>もはや涙など捨ててしまうがいい！<end>"
EN: "Fwahahahaha! A man grows stronger with every tear a maiden sheds! Girldlan, the Hero of the future, has arrived at last! Fair maiden, I shall rescue you from these villains at once! Prepare to smile your loveliest smile! Feel the fiery spirit of the future Hero! Savor the good fortune of being rescued by me! You may cast aside your tears now!<end>"
KO: "후하하하하하! 남자는 소녀가 흘린 눈물의 수만큼 강해지는 법! 미래의 용자 갈드란, 마침내 등장! 자, 가련한 소녀여. 당장 악당들에게서 구해 주마! 마음껏 아름다운 미소를 준비해라. 미래의 용자의 뜨거운 혼을 느껴라! 이 몸에게 구원받는 행운을 음미해라! 이제 눈물 따윈 버려도 좋다!<end>"

## 2240003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec224-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さあ、悪党どもめ、改心の時間だ！<line-break>俺の正義の心を槍から注入してやろう！<line-break>食らえ！　必殺！　悪者成敗勇者槍！<end>"
EN: "Now, villains, it is time to repent! I shall inject my righteous heart into you through my spear! Take this! Finishing move: Hero's Spear of Villain-Smiting!<end>"
KO: "자, 악당들아, 개심할 시간이다! 내 정의의 마음을 창을 통해 주입해 주마! 받아라! 필살! 악당응징 용사창!<end>"

## 2240004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec224-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フッ…。<line-break>また増やしてしまったな。<line-break>輝かしい英雄叙事詩の１ページを。<end>"
EN: "Hmph... Another page added to my glorious heroic epic.<end>"
KO: "훗…. 또 하나 늘려 버렸군. 찬란한 영웅 서사시의 한 페이지를.<end>"

## 2240005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec224-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さあ、もう怖がることはない。<line-break>こっちへおいで、お嬢さん。<end>"
EN: "There is nothing to fear now. Come here, young lady.<end>"
KO: "자, 이제 무서워할 것 없다. 이리 오렴, 아가씨.<end>"

## 2240006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec224-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ウッ…、ウッ…。<end>"
EN: "*Sob... sob...*<end>"
KO: "윽…, 윽….<end>"

## 2240007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec224-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "貴様ッ！　俺の彼女に手を出すなッ！<end>"
EN: "You! Stay away from my girl!<end>"
KO: "네놈! 내 여자친구한테 손대지 마!<end>"

## 2240008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec224-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…何だ、貴様は？<end>"
EN: "What...? Who are you?<end>"
KO: "…뭐냐, 네놈은?<end>"

## 2240009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec224-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "黙れ、この盗賊ッ！<line-break>今すぐ彼女から離れないと<line-break>たたじゃすまないぞッ！<end>"
EN: "Shut up, you bandit! Get away from her right now, or you'll pay for it!<end>"
KO: "닥쳐라, 이 도적놈아! 당장 그녀에게서 떨어지지 않으면무사하지 못할 줄 알아!<end>"

## 2240010
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec224-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふははは！　この早とちりさんめがっ！<line-break>見てわからんのか、この状況が？<line-break>これだから素人は困るのだ。<line-break>俺様の槍からほとばしる熱き魂に触れ<line-break>彼女は、小さな胸を打ち震わせ<line-break>感動の涙を流しているにすぎん！<end>"
EN: "Fwahahaha! You jumped to conclusions, you fool! Can't you see what's going on? This is the trouble with amateurs. The fiery spirit surging from my spear has set her little chest trembling and moved her to tears, that's all!<end>"
KO: "후하하하! 이 성급한 녀석 같으니! 이 상황을 보고도 모르겠나? 이래서 초보자는 곤란하다. 이 몸의 창에서 뿜어져 나오는 뜨거운 영혼에 감동해 그녀는 작은 가슴을 떨며 감격의 눈물을 흘리고 있을 뿐이다!<end>"

## 2240011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec224-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふっ、礼はいらん。<line-break>ただ、未来の勇者ガルドランの勇姿を<line-break>その魂に刻んでおくがいい…。<line-break>そして貴様も、思うさま<line-break>熱き魂をほとばしらせるのだ。<line-break>この俺様に憧れて、な。<end>"
EN: "Hmph. No thanks are necessary. Simply engrave the gallant figure of Girldlan, the Hero of the future, upon your soul. Then let your own fiery spirit surge forth to your heart's content, in admiration of me.<end>"
KO: "흥, 감사는 됐다. 다만 미래의 용사 갈드란의 용맹한 모습을 그 영혼에 새겨 두어라…. 그리고 네놈도 마음껏 뜨거운 영혼을 뿜어내라. 이 나님을 동경하면서 말이지.<end>"

## 2240012
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec224-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "待て、逃がすかッ！<end>"
EN: "Stop! You won't get away!<end>"
KO: "기다려, 놓칠까 보냐!<end>"

## 2240013
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec224-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どわっ！！<end>"
EN: "Gwaaah!!<end>"
KO: "으악!!<end>"

## 2240014
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec224-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…え？<line-break>俺様…刺され…た…？<end>"
EN: "What...? I've been... stabbed...?<end>"
KO: "…어? 나… 찔린… 건가…?<end>"

## 2240015
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec224-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おい、大丈夫か！？<line-break>しっかりしろ！<end>"
EN: "Hey, are you all right?! Stay with us!<end>"
KO: "야, 괜찮아!? 정신 차려!<end>"

## 2240016
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec224-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…ふっ、な、なんのことだ？<line-break>常識で考えろ！　…素人の攻撃を<line-break>未来の勇者が食らうはず…なかろう！<end>"
EN: "Hmph... W-what are you talking about? Use your common sense! The Hero of the future could never be struck by an amateur's attack... obviously!<end>"
KO: "…후, 무, 무슨 소리냐? 상식적으로 생각해라! …미래의 용사가 초짜의 공격을 맞을 리가… 없잖아!<end>"

## 2240017
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec224-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ぐわっ…。<end>"
EN: "Gwah...<end>"
KO: "크악….<end>"

## 2240018
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec224-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ダメだよ！<line-break>しばらくジッとしてて。<line-break>手当てしてあげるから。<end>"
EN: "No! Keep still for a while. I'll tend to your wound.<end>"
KO: "안 돼! 잠깐 가만히 있어. 치료해 줄게.<end>"

## 2240019
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec224-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふっ…心配するな、少年たちよ！<line-break>未来の勇者の力を見るがいい！<line-break>正義の力で傷がふさがってい…<line-break>んぐあっ！　痛い！　ふさがらないッ！<line-break>お、俺様に手傷を負わせるとは…<line-break>あの少年…なかなかやるな！<end>"
EN: "Hmph... Fear not, boys! Behold the power of the future Hero! The power of justice is closing my wound―ngwaaah! It hurts! It isn't closing! T-to think that boy managed to wound me... He's rather good!<end>"
KO: "후… 걱정 마라, 소년들이여! 미래의 용사의 힘을 보아라! 정의의 힘으로 상처가 아물고 있… 으갹! 아파! 안 아물잖아! 이, 이 몸에게 상처를 입히다니… 그 소년, 제법이군!<end>"

## 2240020
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec224-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "バカなこと言ってないで<line-break>おとなしくそこに座れよ…。<end>"
EN: "Stop talking nonsense and sit still...<end>"
KO: "바보 같은 소리 말고 얌전히 거기 앉아 있어….<end>"

## 2240021
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec224-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…ふっ、未来の勇者の危機を見過ごせぬ<line-break>貴様たちの正義の行い、受けてやろう！<line-break>…痛いっ！　…もっとやさしくしてッ！<end>"
EN: "Hmph... You cannot stand by while the future Hero is in peril. Very well! I shall accept your righteous aid! Ow! Be gentler!<end>"
KO: "…후, 미래의 용사의 위기를 지나치지 못하는 너희의 정의로운 행동, 받아 주마! …아파! …좀 더 살살 해 줘!<end>"

## 2240022
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec224-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よし！　これで大丈夫だよ。<end>"
EN: "There! You'll be all right now.<end>"
KO: "좋아! 이걸로 괜찮아.<end>"

## 2240023
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec224-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…おお、勇者復活！<end>"
EN: "Oh! The Hero is back!<end>"
KO: "…오오, 용사 부활!<end>"

## 2240024
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec224-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ねえ、ガルドラン。<line-break>まさかとは思うけどさっきの少年に<line-break>仕返しするつもりじゃないよね？<end>"
EN: "Hey, Girldlan. You're not seriously planning to get back at that boy, are you?<end>"
KO: "있잖아, 갈드란. 설마 아까 그 소년한테 복수할 생각은 아니지?<end>"

## 2240025
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec224-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふはははは、青い、青すぎるぞ！<line-break>逆恨み、やっかみ、仕返し、闇討ち…<line-break>そんな言葉は、俺様の辞書に存在せん！<line-break>未来の勇者の辞書にある言葉は<line-break>ただひとつ！<line-break>「熱き魂」のみ！<end>"
EN: "Fwahahahaha! How naive! Far too naive! Resentment, envy, revenge, ambush... Such words do not exist in my dictionary! The future Hero's dictionary contains but one phrase: “fiery spirit”!<end>"
KO: "후하하하하, 풋내기군, 너무 풋내기야! 앙심, 질투, 보복, 기습… 그런 말은 나님의 사전에 없다! 미래의 용사의 사전에 있는 말은 단 하나! '뜨거운 영혼'뿐이다!<end>"

## 2240026
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec224-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうなんだ。…ごめん。<end>"
EN: "Oh. Sorry.<end>"
KO: "그렇구나. …미안해.<end>"

## 2240027
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec224-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "少年が少しばかり早とちりのマヌケでも<line-break>俺様のおかげで少女が救われたのだ。<line-break>それで充分ではないか…。<end>"
EN: "The boy may be a bit of a fool who jumps to conclusions, but thanks to me, the girl was saved. Isn't that enough?<end>"
KO: "소년이 조금 성급한 바보였어도 이 몸 덕분에 소녀가 구원받았다. 그걸로 충분하지 않은가….<end>"

## 2240028
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec224-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ガルドラン…。お前ってやつは<line-break>確かに勇者の中の勇者だぜ…。<end>"
EN: "Girldlan... You really are a hero among heroes...<end>"
KO: "갈드란… 너란 녀석은 정말 용자 중의 용자야…<end>"

## 2240029
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec224-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フッ…。<line-break>わかってくれればいいんだ。<line-break>熱き魂の同志よ。<end>"
EN: "Hmph... That's all I ask, my comrade in fiery spirit.<end>"
KO: "훗… 알아줬으면 됐어. 뜨거운 혼의 동지여.<end>"

## 2240030
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec224-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さらばだ、諸君。<line-break>正義の心が呼び合う限り<line-break>また会える日もあるだろう。<line-break>くじけそうになった時は<line-break>夜空を見上げて俺様のことを<line-break>思い出すがいい。<line-break>未来の勇者、ガルドランを！<end>"
EN: "Farewell, all of you. So long as righteous hearts call to one another, the day may come when we meet again. Whenever you're about to lose heart, look up at the night sky and remember me: Girldlan, the Hero of the future!<end>"
KO: "잘 있어라, 제군. 정의의 마음이 서로를 부르는 한 다시 만날 날도 있을 것이다. 꺾일 것 같을 때는 밤하늘을 올려다보며 나님을 떠올려라. 미래의 용사, 갈드란을!<end>"

## 2240031
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec224-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "名脇役ガルドラン物語2用終端<end>"
EN: ""
KO: "명조연 갈드란 이야기 2용 끝<end>"

## 2250000
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec225-part99.toml

JP: "名脇役ガルドラン物語3用開始<end>"
