## 2250001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec225-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふっ、また会ったな。<line-break>困った奴め…。<line-break>　<line-break>隠すことはない！<line-break>未来の勇者が気になって気になって<line-break>追いかけてきてしまったのだな。<end>"
EN: "Heh, we meet again. What am I going to do with you...? No need to hide it! You couldn't stop thinking about the future Hero, so you came chasing after me.<end>"
KO: "훗, 또 만났군. 곤란한 녀석… 숨길 것 없다! 미래의 용자가 너무 신경 쓰여서 쫓아와 버린 것이지!<end>"

## 2250002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec225-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふっ、何も恥ずかしがることはない。<line-break>貴様のような思春期によくあることだ。<line-break>病気ではないから心配するな！<line-break>そんな貴様たちに教えておこう！<line-break>憧れだけではどうにもならない<line-break>厳しい「勇者の条件」を！<end>"
EN: "Heh, there's nothing to be ashamed of. It's perfectly common at your age. It's not an illness, so don't worry! Now let me teach you both something: the stringent conditions for becoming a Hero―conditions that mere admiration can never satisfy!<end>"
KO: "후, 부끄러워할 것 없다. 네 나이에는 흔한 일이야. 병이 아니니 걱정 마라! 그런 너희에게 알려 주지! 동경만으론 어쩔 수 없는 엄격한 '용사의 조건'을!<end>"

## 2250004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec225-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "勇者の条件…<line-break>それは勇者を心から慕う美しい王女！<line-break>それが答えだ！俺様のことを激しく慕いながら、<line-break>身分の壁に阻まれて、告白もできず<line-break>俺様に小さな胸を震わせる王女だ！<end>"
EN: "The requirement of a Hero... is a beautiful princess who adores him with all her heart! That is the answer! A princess who yearns passionately for me, yet cannot confess her love because the barrier of rank stands between us, leaving her little bosom trembling for me!<end>"
KO: "용사의 조건… 그것은 용사를 진심으로 사모하는 아름다운 왕녀! 그게 답이다! 나님을 격렬하게 사모하면서도 신분의 벽에 막혀 고백도 못 하고 나님 때문에 조그만 가슴을 떨고 있는 왕녀다!<end>"

## 2250005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec225-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アキュリースにひとり、<line-break>美しい巫女がいるという…。<line-break>彼女こそ、俺様を慕う王女に違いない！<line-break>だから、急いで彼女のもとへ<line-break>俺様は伝えにゆかねばならぬのだ！<line-break>勇者は身分の壁など軽く越えられると！<end>"
EN: "They say there is a beautiful Priestess in Aqyurius... She must be the princess who adores me! I must hurry to her side and tell her that no barrier of rank can stand in a Hero's way!<end>"
KO: "아큐류스에 아름다운 무녀가 한 명 있다고 한다…. 그녀야말로 이 몸을 사모하는 왕녀가 틀림없다! 그러니 서둘러 그녀에게 전하러 가야 한다! 용사에게 신분의 벽 따윈 아무것도 아니라고!<end>"

## 2250006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec225-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふっ、その点、俺様にぬかりはない！<line-break>ロストールの王女の所にはもう行った！<line-break>無粋な衛兵に阻まれてしまったがな。<line-break>そこで、ロストールの王女には悪いが<line-break>勇者を慕う王女はここの巫女に決めた。<line-break>巫女でも王女でも、俺様には変わらん！<end>"
EN: "Hah! I have already seen to that! I went to the princess of Rostorl, but some boorish guards barred my way. So, with apologies to her, I have decided that the princess who adores the Hero shall be the priestess here. Priestess or princess, it makes no difference to me!<end>"
KO: "후, 그 점은 이 몸에게 빈틈이 없지! 로스톨 왕녀에게는 이미 다녀왔다! 무례한 위병에게 막히고 말았지만 말이야. 그래서 로스톨 왕녀에겐 미안하지만 용자를 사모하는 왕녀는 이곳의 무녀로 정했다. 무녀든 왕녀든 이 몸에겐 다를 게 없어!<end>"

## 2250007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec225-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "全然、違うと思うけど…。<end>"
EN: "I don't think that's right at all...<end>"
KO: "전혀 다른 것 같은데….<end>"

## 2250008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec225-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "小さい…。<line-break><line-break><line-break>小さいぞ、小僧オオオオ！！<line-break>勇者になりたければ<line-break>もっと大局的に物事を見るのだ！<end>"
EN: "Such small thinking... Too small, booooooy! If you wish to become a Hero, you must learn to see the larger picture!<end>"
KO: "작다…. 작다고, 꼬마아아아!! 용자가 되고 싶다면 더 큰 안목으로 사물을 봐라!<end>"

## 2250009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec225-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そりゃあ、勇者には憧れるけど…。<line-break>そもそもボクだって<line-break>その巫女のひとりなんだよね…。<end>"
EN: "I mean, I do admire Heroes... But I'm one of those Priestesses myself, you know...<end>"
KO: "그야 용사는 동경하지만…. 애초에 나도 그 무녀 중 하나잖아….<end>"

## 2250010
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec225-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ワーハッハッハッ。<line-break>小僧、これも教えておくぞ。<line-break>巫女とは、女性だ。<end>"
EN: "Wahahahaha! Boy, let me teach you one more thing. Priestesses are women.<end>"
KO: "와하하하하. 꼬마야, 이것도 가르쳐 주마. 무녀란 여자다.<end>"

## 2250011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec225-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それとも、俺様に憧れるあまり、<line-break>俺様の恋人になろうと思ってるのか！？<line-break>恐ろしい…恐ろしい妄想だぞ、小僧！<end>"
EN: "Or do you admire me so much that you intend to become my sweetheart?! What a horrifying... truly horrifying fantasy, boy!<end>"
KO: "아니면 이 몸을 너무 동경한 나머지 내 연인이 되려는 건가!? 무서운… 무서운 망상이군, 꼬마!<end>"

## 2250012
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec225-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そ、そんなこと言われても…。<end>"
EN: "I-I don't know what you expect me to say to that...<end>"
KO: "그, 그런 말을 들어도….<end>"

## 2250013
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec225-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "小僧！　勇者になりたければ<line-break>ごちゃごちゃ言いわけはするな！<line-break>男らしくないぞ！<end>"
EN: "Boy! If you wish to become a Hero, stop making excuses! It's unmanly!<end>"
KO: "꼬마! 용사가 되고 싶다면 구질구질하게 변명하지 마라! 사내답지 못하다!<end>"

## 2250014
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec225-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お、男らしくないって…！？<end>"
EN: "U-unmanly?!<end>"
KO: "나, 남자답지 않다고…!?<end>"

## 2250015
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec225-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おっと、こうしてはおれん！<line-break>この間にも巫女は俺様に恋焦がれて<line-break>心を痛めているのだった！<line-break>さあ、巫女よ、可憐な胸をときめかせ、<line-break>勇者の熱き魂みなぎる両腕に<line-break>飛びこんでくるがいい！<end>"
EN: "Ah, I have no time to waste! Even now, the Priestess is pining for me, her heart aching with love! Come, fair Priestess! Let your tender heart race, and fling yourself into these arms, surging with the fiery spirit of a Hero!<end>"
KO: "앗, 이러고 있을 때가 아니다! 이 순간에도 무녀는 나를 애타게 사랑하며 가슴앓이를 하고 있을 터! 자, 무녀여, 가련한 가슴을 두근거리며 용사의 뜨거운 영혼이 넘치는 이 두 팔에 뛰어들어라!<end>"

## 2250016
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec225-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ううん、大丈夫…。<line-break>あまり気にしてないから…。<line-break>　<line-break>…でも、ボクって、<line-break>他の人には男の子に見えるのかな？<end>"
EN: "No, it's all right. It doesn't bother me that much... But do other people really think I look like a boy?<end>"
KO: "응, 괜찮아…. 별로 신경 쓰지 않으니까…. …그런데 나, 다른 사람들 눈에는 남자아이로 보이나?<end>"

## 2250017
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec225-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "可愛い女の子だよ<end>"
EN: "You're a cute girl.<end>"
KO: "귀여운 여자아이야<end>"

## 2250018
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec225-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "見える<end>"
EN: "You do.<end>"
KO: "보여<end>"

## 2250019
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec225-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "エヘヘ…。そうだよね。<line-break>ボク、正真正銘女の子だし。<line-break>ありがとう、<value:$28>。<line-break>あいつのせいで、ちょっと<line-break>自信をなくすところだったよ。<end>"
EN: "Hee hee... You're right. I am a girl, through and through. Thanks, <value:$28>. He nearly made me lose confidence in myself.<end>"
KO: "헤헤…. 그렇지. 나 틀림없는 여자아이니까. 고마워, <value:$28>. 그 녀석 때문에 자신감을 잃을 뻔했어.<end>"

## 2250020
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec225-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どうして？　…どうしてなの？<line-break>そんなに女の子として<line-break>魅力ないかなぁ、ボク…？<end>"
EN: "Why? Why do you think so? Am I really that unattractive as a girl...?<end>"
KO: "왜? …왜 그런 거야? 내가 여자애로서 그렇게 매력이 없나…?<end>"

## 2250021
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec225-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "えっ、違う！？　この服が？<line-break>女の子っぽく見えないって？<end>"
EN: "What, that's not it?! It's these clothes? They don't make me look feminine?<end>"
KO: "뭐, 아니라고!? 이 옷이? 여자아이처럼 안 보인다고?<end>"

## 2250022
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec225-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…そっか。じゃあ、女の子っぽい服を<line-break>着ていればいいんだね？<end>"
EN: "Oh, I see. So I just need to wear something more feminine?<end>"
KO: "…그렇구나. 그럼 여자애 같은 옷을 입고 있으면 되는 거지?<end>"

## 2250023
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec225-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わかった。<line-break>じゃあ今度、着てくるから、<line-break>楽しみに待っててよ！<end>"
EN: "All right. I'll wear something like that next time, so you have that to look forward to!<end>"
KO: "알았어. 그럼 다음엔 입고 올 테니까 기대하고 기다려!<end>"

## 2260001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec226-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やい、あっち行け！<end>"
EN: "Hey, get out of here!<end>"
KO: "야, 저리 가!<end>"

## 2260002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec226-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "こっちへ来るな、化け物！<end>"
EN: "Don't come any closer, monster!<end>"
KO: "이리 오지 마, 괴물!<end>"

## 2260003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec226-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ウッ…、ひっく…。<end>"
EN: "Ngh... hic...<end>"
KO: "윽…, 흑….<end>"

## 2260004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec226-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "こら、小僧どもッ！<line-break>弱い者いじめは悪の始まり。<line-break>この俺様が許さんぞ！<end>"
EN: "Hold it, you little rascals! Picking on the weak is the first step down the path of evil, and I will not stand for it!<end>"
KO: "이봐, 꼬마들! 약자를 괴롭히는 건 악의 시작이다. 이 몸이 용서하지 않겠다!<end>"

## 2260005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec226-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なんだよ、オッサン？<line-break>いきなり出てきて…。<end>"
EN: "What's your problem, old man? You just came barging in...<end>"
KO: "뭐야, 아저씨? 갑자기 나타나서는….<end>"

## 2260006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec226-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うるせえなぁ！<line-break>なんでこんな化け物をかばうんだよ！<line-break>こいつ、ハーフエルフなんだぜ？<end>"
EN: "Shut up! Why're you sticking up for a monster like this? She's a Half-Elf, you know!<end>"
KO: "시끄럽네! 왜 이런 괴물을 감싸는 거야! 이 녀석, 하프엘프라고?<end>"

## 2260007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec226-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それがどうした？<line-break>お前たちに何の不都合がある？<end>"
EN: "So what? What difference does that make to any of you?<end>"
KO: "그게 어쨌다는 거지? 너희에게 무슨 불편이 있나?<end>"

## 2260008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec226-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…えっ！？<end>"
EN: "Huh?!<end>"
KO: "…뭐!?<end>"

## 2260009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec226-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "邪悪な破壊神の手下か？<line-break>この勇者様の目にはそうは見えんぞ。<end>"
EN: "Do you think she's some servant of the evil God of Destruction? This great Hero sees nothing of the sort!<end>"
KO: "사악한 파괴신의 부하인가? 이 용자님의 눈에는 그렇게 안 보이는데.<end>"

## 2260010
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec226-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "何もしてない女の子を寄ってたかって<line-break>いじめているお前たちに正義はない。<line-break>　<line-break>俺様のように熱く燃える魂と<line-break>不屈の正義をもった大人になれるよう<line-break>天に代わってお仕置きしてやる！<end>"
EN: "There is no justice in ganging up on an innocent little girl! So that you may grow into adults with fiery souls and an unyielding sense of justice, just like me, I shall punish you on Heaven's behalf!<end>"
KO: "아무것도 하지 않은 여자아이를 떼로 괴롭히는 너희에게 정의는 없다. 나님처럼 뜨겁게 타오르는 영혼과 불굴의 정의를 가진 어른이 되도록 하늘을 대신해 벌을 내려 주마!<end>"

## 2260011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec226-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…う、うるせえやいっ！<end>"
EN: "S-shut up!<end>"
KO: "…시, 시끄러워!<end>"

## 2260012
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec226-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "バーカ！<line-break>お前の説教なんか誰が聞くもんか！<end>"
EN: "Idiot! Who'd listen to your stupid lecture?<end>"
KO: "바보야! 네 설교 따위 누가 듣냐!<end>"

## 2260013
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec226-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、コラ、待て。<line-break>話はまだ終わっていないぞ！<end>"
EN: "Hey! Wait! I haven't finished talking to you!<end>"
KO: "아, 이봐, 기다려. 이야기는 아직 안 끝났어!<end>"

## 2260014
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec226-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うーむ。このままではあの子たちの<line-break>心がゆがんだままになってしまう。<line-break>　<line-break>間違った道に進まぬよう、<line-break>悪の芽を摘み取ってやるのも<line-break>勇者の使命。<end>"
EN: "Hmm. At this rate, their hearts will remain twisted. A Hero must also nip evil in the bud and keep them from going down the wrong path.<end>"
KO: "음. 이대로는 저 아이들의 마음이 비뚤어진 채가 되어버리겠군. 잘못된 길로 가지 않도록 악의 싹을 뽑아주는 것도 용자의 사명.<end>"

## 2260015
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec226-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "待っていろ、小僧ども！<line-break>今こそ勇者ガルドラン様が<line-break>真の正義に目覚めさせてやるぞ！<end>"
EN: "Wait right there, you little rascals! Now the great Hero Girldlan shall awaken you to true justice!<end>"
KO: "기다려라, 꼬맹이들아! 지금이야말로 용사 갈드란 님이 진정한 정의에 눈뜨게 해 주마!<end>"

## 2260016
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec226-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やれやれ…。<line-break>これはちと面倒なことに<line-break>なりそうじゃ…。<line-break>あの男、悪いヤツではないんじゃが、<line-break>いささか思いこみが強すぎて<line-break>暴走するからのう。<end>"
EN: "Good grief... This may become rather troublesome. He is not a bad man, but once he gets an idea into his head, he tends to get carried away.<end>"
KO: "이런 이런… 이건 좀 성가신 일이 될 것 같군… 그 남자, 나쁜 녀석은 아니지만 너무 자기 생각이 강해서 폭주하거든.<end>"

## 2260017
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec226-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どうする、<value:$28>？<end>"
EN: "What will you do, <value:$28>?<end>"
KO: "어떻게 할래, <value:$28>?<end>"

## 2260018
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec226-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "行く<end>"
EN: "Go<end>"
KO: "간다<end>"

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

EN: "Don't go<end>"
KO: "안 간다<end>"

## 2260020
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec226-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やはり、それがいいじゃろう。<line-break>では、急ぐか。<line-break>　<line-break>あの方向からすると<line-break>おそらく廃城のほうじゃな。<end>"
EN: "Yes, I think that would be best. Then let us hurry. Judging by the direction he went, he is probably headed for the Ruined Castle.<end>"
KO: "역시 그게 좋겠군. 그럼 서두르자. 저 방향으로 보아 아마 폐성 쪽일 게다.<end>"

## 2260021
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec226-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ほう、あやつを信じるのか…。<line-break>お主のことじゃから、てっきり<line-break>追っていくと思っていたが…。<end>"
EN: "Oh? You trust him, then... Knowing you, I was certain you would go after him.<end>"
KO: "호오, 그 녀석을 믿는 건가…. 네 성격이라면 당연히 뒤쫓아 갈 줄 알았는데….<end>"

## 2260022
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec226-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まあ、それもよかろう。<line-break>わしの取り越し苦労かもしれん…。<end>"
EN: "Well, that is fine too. Perhaps I am worrying over nothing...<end>"
KO: "뭐, 그것도 좋겠지. 내가 괜한 걱정을 한 걸지도 몰라….<end>"

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 2270003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec227-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "未来の勇者ガルドラン参上！<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>どうした、小僧ども。<line-break>女の子をいじめることはできても<line-break>魔物に立ち向かう勇気はないのか？<end>"
EN: "Girldlan, future Hero, is here! What's wrong, you little whelps? Brave enough to pick on a girl, but not brave enough to face a monster?<end>"
KO: "미래의 용사 갈드란 등장! 왜 그러냐, 꼬맹이들. 여자애를 괴롭힐 용기는 있어도 마물에게 맞설 용기는 없나?<end>"

## 2270004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec227-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ごめんなさーい！<end>"

KO: "미안해요ー!<end>"

## 2270005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec227-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "僕たちが悪かったよ。<line-break>助けて、おじさん！<end>"
EN: "We were wrong. Help us, mister!<end>"
KO: "우리가 잘못했어. 도와줘, 아저씨!<end>"

## 2270006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec227-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わかればいいんだ、わかれば。<line-break>罪を憎んで人を憎まず。<line-break>あとはこの俺様に任せろ！<end>"
EN: "As long as you understand. Hate the sin, not the sinner. Now leave the rest to me!<end>"
KO: "알았으면 됐어, 알았으면. 죄를 미워하되 사람은 미워하지 말라. 나머지는 이 몸에게 맡겨!<end>"

## 2270007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec227-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "来い、化け物！<line-break>子供たちの無垢な魂を貴様ごときに<line-break>くれてやるわけにはいかん。<line-break>完璧超絶無敵勇者の俺様が<line-break>相手になってやる！<end>"
EN: "Come, beast! I won't let the likes of you have these children's innocent souls. You face the perfect, peerless, invincible Hero himself!<end>"
KO: "덤벼라, 괴물! 아이들의 순수한 영혼을 네놈 따위에게 내줄 순 없다. 완벽초절무적용사인 내가 상대해 주마!<end>"

## 2270008

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

EN: "Idiot! Run!<end>"

## 2270013
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec227-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おじさん！　起きてよぅ…。<end>"
EN: "Mister! Please wake up...<end>"
KO: "아저씨! 일어나 봐….<end>"

## 2270014
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec227-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ハァ…、ハァ…<line-break>ひとつだけ言っておく…。<line-break>おじさんではない…。勇者様だ。<end>"
EN: "Hah... hah... Let me tell you one thing... It's not “mister”... It's “Lord Hero.”<end>"
KO: "하아… 하아… 한 가지만 말해 두지…. 아저씨가 아니다…. 용자님이다.<end>"

## 2270015
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec227-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まだまだ勝負はこれからだ。<line-break>絶体絶命からの大逆転は<line-break>勇者のみに許される特権。<line-break>小僧ども、お前たちは幸運だぞ。<line-break>俺様英雄伝説第３３章最大の山場を<line-break>その目で見られるのだからな！<end>"
EN: "This battle is far from over. Only a Hero has the privilege of snatching victory from the jaws of certain defeat. Consider yourselves fortunate, you little whelps. You get to witness the grand climax of Chapter Thirty-Three of My Heroic Legend!<end>"
KO: "승부는 아직 이제부터다. 절체절명의 위기에서 대역전하는 건 용사에게만 허락된 특권. 꼬맹이들, 너희는 운이 좋아. 나님 영웅전설 제33장 최대의 절정을 직접 보게 되니까!<end>"

## 2270016
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec227-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いかん！<line-break>あやつひとりでは分が悪い。<line-break><value:$28>よ、行くぞ！<end>"
EN: "This is bad! The odds are against him on his own. <value:$28>, let's go!<end>"
KO: "안 돼! 저 녀석 혼자서는 불리하다. <value:$28>여, 가자!<end>"

## 2270017
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec227-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "待てっ！　勇者に助太刀は無用！<line-break>この熱く燃えたぎる魂ある限り<line-break>魔物ごときに屈することはない！<end>"
EN: "Wait! A Hero needs no aid! So long as this soul burns within me, I shall never yield to a mere monster!<end>"
KO: "기다려! 용사에게 도움은 필요 없다! 이 뜨겁게 타오르는 영혼이 있는 한 마물 따위에 굴복하지 않는다!<end>"

## 2270018
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec227-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "血気盛んなのはいいが、<line-break>人の親切は素直に受けるもんじゃぞ。<line-break>　<line-break>第一、勇者様の度量はそんなに<line-break>狭いものじゃなかろう。<end>"
EN: "There's nothing wrong with youthful fire, but you ought to accept a kindness when it's offered. Besides, surely a Hero isn't so small-minded as all that.<end>"
KO: "혈기왕성한 건 좋지만 남의 친절은 순순히 받는 법이야. 무엇보다 용사님의 도량이 그리 좁진 않겠지.<end>"

## 2270019
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec227-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "むっ、むむっ…。確かに。<line-break>では仕方あるまい。<line-break>助太刀を認めてやる。<line-break>ただし、足は引っ張るなよ。<end>"
EN: "Hrm... Hrrm... You have a point. Very well, then. I shall permit you to assist me. Just don't get in my way.<end>"
KO: "흠, 음…. 확실히 그렇군. 어쩔 수 없지. 조력을 허락하마. 다만 방해는 하지 마라.<end>"

## 2270020
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec227-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おじさん、大丈夫！？<end>"
EN: "Mister, are you all right?!<end>"
KO: "아저씨, 괜찮아!?<end>"

## 2270021
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec227-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フン…。<line-break>この程度のかすり傷、なんともない！<line-break>　<line-break>だが、小僧、その前にやることが<line-break>あるだろう？<end>"

## 2270022

## 2270025
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec227-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 2270026

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

EN: "But why did you save us?<end>"

## 2270027
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec227-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 2270028

## 2270029

source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec227-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

EN: "Oh...<end>"
KO: "그렇구나…<end>"

## 2270034
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec227-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 2270037
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec227-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "もうこれであの子たちは大丈夫だな。<end>"
EN: "Those children should be all right now.<end>"
KO: "이제 저 아이들은 괜찮겠군.<end>"

## 2270038
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec227-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "とりあえず礼を言っておくぞ。<line-break>俺様の英雄叙事詩が語られるとき、<line-break>貴様たちのことも付け加えてやろう。<line-break>あくまで脇役としてだが、な…。<end>"
EN: "I suppose I owe you my thanks. When my heroic epic is told, I shall see that you are mentioned as well. As supporting characters, of course...<end>"
KO: "일단 감사는 해 두지. 이 몸의 영웅 서사시가 전해질 때 너희 이야기도 덧붙여 주마. 어디까지나 조연으로 말이지만….<end>"

## 2270039
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec227-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺様には未来の勇者として果たすべき<line-break>崇高な使命が待っている。<line-break>さらばだ！<end>"
EN: "A noble mission awaits me, the future Hero. Farewell!<end>"
KO: "이 몸에겐 미래의 용자로서 완수해야 할 숭고한 사명이 기다리고 있다. 잘 있어라!<end>"

## 2270040
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec227-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わしの勘違いかもしれんが、<line-break>あの男、なかなかの大人物に<line-break>成長するかもしれんのう。<end>"
EN: "I may be mistaken, but that fellow might grow into a truly remarkable man one day.<end>"
KO: "내 착각일지도 모르지만 그 남자, 꽤 큰 인물로 성장할지도 모르겠군.<end>"

## 2270041
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec227-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それにしても、これがきっかけで、<line-break>あの子たちの周りが<line-break>変わっていけばいいんじゃがな…。<end>"
EN: "Still, I hope this will be the start of things changing around those children...<end>"
KO: "그래도 이 일을 계기로 그 아이들 주변이 조금씩 변해 가면 좋겠구먼….<end>"

## 2280001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec228-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "エルファス様ーっ！<end>"
EN: "Master Elfas!<end>"

## 2280002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec228-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "君はたしか以前……。<end>"
EN: "You're the one from before...<end>"
KO: "너, 전에 본 적이….<end>"

## 2280003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec228-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "はい。僕の友だちだったカメを<line-break>エルファス様に生き返らせて<line-break>もらいました！<end>"
EN: "Yes! You brought my turtle friend back to life!<end>"
KO: "네. 제 친구였던 거북이를 엘파스 님께서 되살려 주셨어요!<end>"

## 2280004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec228-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あの、僕、もう一度<line-break>エルファス様にお願いしたくて<line-break>待ってたんです。<end>"
EN: "Um, I've been waiting for you. I wanted to ask you for another favor.<end>"
KO: "저기, 저는 엘파스 님께 한 번 더 부탁드리고 싶어서 기다리고 있었어요.<end>"

## 2280005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec228-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お願いって？<end>"
EN: "What kind of favor?<end>"
KO: "부탁이라니?<end>"

## 2280006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec228-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "僕の飼っていた犬が、２日前、<line-break>死んじゃったんです…。<line-break>　<line-break>お願いです。エルファス様。<line-break>僕の犬を助けてください！<end>"
EN: "My dog died two days ago... Please, Master Elfas. Save my dog!<end>"
KO: "제가 기르던 개가 이틀 전에 죽었어요…. 부탁드립니다, 엘파스 님. 제 개를 살려 주세요!<end>"

## 2280008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec228-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…ダメだよ。<line-break>生き返らせることはできない…。<end>"
EN: "...No. I can't bring your dog back to life.<end>"
KO: "…안 돼. 되살릴 수는 없어….<end>"

## 2280009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec228-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どうして？<line-break>前はカメを助けてくれたのに…。<line-break>　<line-break>もう奇跡は起こせないの？<line-break>僕、毎日神様にお祈りできるよ。<end>"
EN: "Why not? You saved the turtle before... Can't you perform miracles anymore? I can pray to God every day.<end>"
KO: "왜? 전에는 거북이를 도와줬잖아…. 이제 기적은 못 일으켜? 나 매일 신께 기도할 수 있어.<end>"

## 2280010
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec228-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "すまない…。<end>"
EN: "I'm sorry...<end>"
KO: "미안하다….<end>"

## 2280011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec228-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "君は立派だったよ。<line-break>よくお墓を作ってあげたね。<end>"
EN: "You did a fine thing. You made sure your dog had a grave.<end>"
KO: "너 훌륭했어. 무덤도 잘 만들어 줬구나.<end>"

## 2280012
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec228-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これでよかったの？<line-break>エルファス様…。<end>"
EN: "Was this really the right thing to do, Master Elfas?<end>"
KO: "이걸로 괜찮았던 걸까요? 엘파스 님….<end>"

## 2280013
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec228-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "生き物にはすべて死が訪れ、<line-break>どんなに大切な友だちであっても<line-break>別れなければならない時が来る。<line-break>だからこそ、みんな命を大切にし、<line-break>死んだ時には悲しむんだ。<line-break>　<line-break>だけど悲しみだけじゃない…。<line-break>君に大切にされたことをあの犬も<line-break>きっと喜んでいるよ。<line-break>君の胸にだって、あの子との思い出が<line-break>温かいまま残っているだろう？<end>"
EN: "Death comes to every living thing. No matter how dear a friend may be, there comes a time when we must say goodbye. That is why we all cherish life, and why we grieve when death comes. But grief isn't all there is. I'm sure your dog is glad to have been loved by you. And the memories of your time together are still warm in your heart, aren't they?<end>"
KO: "모든 생명에는 죽음이 찾아오고, 아무리 소중한 친구라도 헤어져야 할 때가 와. 그래서 모두가 생명을 소중히 여기고, 죽으면 슬퍼하는 거야. 하지만 슬픔만 있는 건 아니야…. 네가 아껴 준 걸 그 개도 분명 기뻐했을 거야. 네 마음속에도 그 아이와의 추억이 따뜻하게 남아 있잖아?<end>"

## 2280014
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec228-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うん。本当だ。<line-break>ありがとう、エルファス様！<end>"
EN: "Yes. They are. Thank you, Master Elfas!<end>"
KO: "응. 정말이야. 감사합니다, 엘파스 님!<end>"

## 2280015
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec228-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "君は生き返らせるべきだったと<line-break>思うかい？<end>"
EN: "Do you think I should have brought the dog back to life?<end>"
KO: "너는 되살렸어야 했다고 생각해?<end>"

## 2280016
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec228-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>ならそう言うと<line-break>思っていたよ。<line-break>　<line-break>命は限りあるものだからこそ、<line-break>尊重されるべきなんだ。<line-break>　<line-break>生も死も、簡単に覆せるほど<line-break>軽いものじゃない。<line-break>君との旅で、僕はそう学んだよ…。<end>"
EN: "I thought you'd say that, <value:$28>. Life deserves respect precisely because it is finite. Neither life nor death is so trivial that it can simply be overturned. Traveling with you taught me that...<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>라면 그렇게 말할 줄 알았어. 생명은 한계가 있기 때문에 존중받아야 하는 거야. 삶도 죽음도 간단히 뒤집을 만큼 가벼운 게 아니지. 너와 여행하면서 나는 그렇게 배웠어….<end>"

## 2290001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec229-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…なあ、エステル。<line-break>ちょっと相談があるんだ。<end>"
EN: "Hey, Esther... Can I ask your advice about something?<end>"
KO: "…저기, 에스텔. 좀 상의할 게 있어.<end>"

## 2290002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec229-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "もちろん時間は取らせねえし、<line-break>そんな難しい話じゃないから。<end>"
EN: "It won't take long, and it's nothing too complicated.<end>"
KO: "물론 시간 뺏을 생각은 없고, 그렇게 어려운 이야기도 아니야.<end>"

## 2290004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec229-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…俺たち、なかなかセラと<line-break>なじめなくって困ってんだ。<line-break>アイツ、話しかけても乗ってこねえし。<end>"
EN: "We can't seem to get anywhere with Sera, and we're at a loss. Whenever we try talking to him, he just doesn't engage.<end>"
KO: "…우리, 세라랑 좀처럼 친해지질 못해서 곤란해. 그 녀석, 말 걸어도 반응이 영 없거든.<end>"

## 2290005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec229-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なあ、セラと仲よくなるには<line-break>どうしたらいいと思う？<end>"
EN: "How do you think we could make friends with Sera?<end>"
KO: "있지, 세라와 친해지려면 어떻게 하면 좋을까?<end>"

## 2290006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec229-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アハハハ！<line-break>深刻な顔して、どんな話かと思えば<line-break>そんなことだったの？<line-break>気にしすぎだよ、ふたりとも。<end>"
EN: "Hahaha! You both looked so serious, I wondered what this was about. You're worrying too much.<end>"
KO: "아하하하! 심각한 얼굴로 무슨 얘긴가 했더니 그런 거였어? 너무 신경 쓰지 마, 둘 다.<end>"

## 2290007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec229-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺が渾身のシャレを披露したのに、<line-break>アイツ、冷たい目で<line-break>無視しやがるんだよ。<end>"
EN: "I hit him with my very best pun, and he just gave me a cold look and ignored me.<end>"
KO: "내가 혼신의 농담을 했는데 녀석이 차가운 눈으로 무시해 버렸다고.<end>"

## 2290008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec229-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…プッ！　今思い出しても笑えるよ。<line-break>あのシャレは最高だったのに<line-break>なんでわかんないかな、セラは…。<end>"
EN: "Pfft! I still laugh just thinking about it. That pun was brilliant! How can Sera not get it?<end>"
KO: "…풉! 지금 생각해도 웃겨. 그 말장난은 최고였는데 왜 세라는 이해를 못 하는 걸까….<end>"

## 2290009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec229-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…ヴァンのシャレってそんなに<line-break>面白かったかなぁ…？<line-break>ねえ、<value:$28>？<end>"
EN: "Was Van's joke really that funny...? What do you think, <value:$28>?<end>"
KO: "…반의 농담이 그렇게 재미있었나…? 그렇지, <value:$28>?<end>"

## 2290010
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec229-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お、おもしろいよ。<end>"
EN: "I-It was funny.<end>"
KO: "재, 재미있어.<end>"

## 2290011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec229-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "全然面白くない。<end>"
EN: "It wasn't funny at all.<end>"
KO: "전혀 재미없어.<end>"

## 2290012
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec229-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ほらみろ。<line-break>面白いに決まってるよな。<line-break>よーし、調子が出てきたぜ。<line-break>ここで一発！<end>"
EN: "See? Of course it was funny! All right, I'm on a roll now. Here's another one!<end>"
KO: "거봐. 재미있을 게 뻔하잖아. 좋아, 이제 감이 왔어. 여기서 한 방!<end>"

## 2290013
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec229-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "リベルダムの時計塔を見ていた<line-break>エステルにひと言…。<line-break>　<line-break>時計なんか、ほっとけい！<end>"
EN: "Esther was looking at Liberdam's clock tower, so I said... Don't waste your time on it!<end>"
KO: "리벨덤 시계탑을 보던 에스텔에게 한마디…. 시계 같은 건 내버려 둬!<end>"

## 2290014
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec229-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ハハハッ！　最高だよ、ヴァン！<end>"
EN: "Hahaha! That was brilliant, Van!<end>"
KO: "하하핫! 최고야, 반!<end>"

## 2290015
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec229-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ねえ、<value:$28>…。<line-break>ボクはハッキリ言ってあげたほうが<line-break>いいと思うよ…。<end>"
EN: "Listen, <value:$28>... I think you'd better tell him the truth.<end>"
KO: "저기, <value:$28>…. 난 확실하게 말해 주는 편이 좋다고 생각해….<end>"

## 2290016
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec229-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うそだろ。面白くないなんて…。<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>…面白くないシャレは<line-break>やめなシャレってか？<line-break>ププッ…！<end>"
EN: "You can't be serious. It wasn't funny...? So I should stop―put an end to the pun-ishment? Pfft...!<end>"
KO: "거짓말이지. 재미없다니…. …재미없는 말장난은 그만하라니? 푸풋…!<end>"

## 2290017
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec229-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…エステルはともかく、姉ちゃんが<line-break>ヴァンのシャレを認めないなんて、<line-break>俺、信じられないよ…。<end>"
EN: "I can understand Esther, but I can't believe even my own sister doesn't appreciate Van's jokes...<end>"
KO: "…에스텔은 몰라도 누나가 반의 말장난을 인정하지 않는다니, 난 믿을 수가 없어….<end>"

## 2290018
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec229-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "待ってくれー、ヴァン！<line-break>さっきのシャレ、最高だったぜー！<end>"
EN: "Wait for me, Van! That joke was brilliant!<end>"
KO: "기다려, 반! 방금 그 농담 최고였어!<end>"

## 2290019
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec229-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ああやって、面白くもないダジャレで<line-break>無理やり笑わせようとするから、<line-break>セラに嫌われてるのにね…。<end>"

## 2300002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec230-part99.toml

## 2300003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec230-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

KO: "대체 이런 한밤중에 남자 둘이서 무슨 이야기야?<end>"

## 2300004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec230-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "セラのことだよ。<line-break>アイツ、俺のシャレに<line-break>ちっとも笑わないんだ…。<end>"
EN: "Sera. He never laughs at any of my jokes...<end>"
KO: "세라 말이야. 그 녀석, 내 말장난에 전혀 안 웃어…<end>"

## 2300005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec230-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

EN: "What jokes?<end>"
KO: "농담이라니?<end>"

## 2300006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec230-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "たとえば、こう言ってやったんだよ。<line-break>セラは優男だからプレートアーマーは<line-break>着さセラれないって。<line-break>ププッ。笑えるだろ？<end>"
EN: "For example, I told him, “Sera's such a pretty boy, you Sera-ly couldn't make him wear plate armor!” Heh heh. Funny, right?<end>"
KO: "예를 들면 이렇게 말해 줬지. 세라는 샌님이라 플레이트 아머를 입힐 수 없다고. 풉. 웃기지?<end>"

## 2300008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec230-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そしたら本気で怒っちゃって…。<line-break>俺たちは一生懸命、セラと<line-break>打ち解けようと思ってやってるのに。<end>"
EN: "Then he got really angry... We're trying so hard to break the ice with Sera.<end>"
KO: "그랬더니 진짜로 화를 내 버렸어…. 우리는 열심히 세라와 친해지려고 한 건데.<end>"

## 2300009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec230-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

KO: "…단언하겠는데, 문제는 그 썰렁한 말장난이야.<end>"

## 2300010

## 2300011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec230-part99.toml

## 2300012
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec230-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そんなありえないことを言ってるのは<line-break>チャカだけ。<end>"

## 2300013
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec230-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ヴァンのシャレには耐えられない。<line-break>有害よ。<line-break>とにかく、もうやめて。<end>"
EN: "Van's jokes are unbearable. They're harmful. Just stop already.<end>"
KO: "반의 말장난은 견딜 수 없어. 유해해. 아무튼 이제 그만해.<end>"

## 2300014
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec230-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

EN: "Don't you agree, <value:$28>?<end>"
KO: "그렇지 않아, <value:$28>?<end>"

## 2300015
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec230-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ちょっと言いすぎじゃぁ…<end>"
EN: "Aren't you going a little too far...?<end>"

## 2300016
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec230-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やめたほうがいい<end>"
EN: "You should stop.<end>"
KO: "그만두는 게 좋아<end>"

## 2300017
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec230-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 2300018
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec230-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…も、もしかして、<line-break>なんかヤバい雰囲気になってる？<line-break>　<line-break>オイフェって結構、アツくなると<line-break>見境がなくなるから…。<end>"
EN: "W-wait, are things getting ugly? Aoife loses all restraint when she gets worked up...<end>"
KO: "…서, 설마 뭔가 위험한 분위기가 된 거야? 오이페는 열받으면 꽤 앞뒤를 못 가리니까….<end>"

## 2300019
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec230-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ああ。弓矢を取り出さないうちに<line-break>トンズラしたほうがよさそうだな…。<end>"
EN: "Yeah. We should probably make a run for it before she gets her bow out...<end>"
KO: "그래. 활을 꺼내기 전에 튀는 게 좋겠군…<end>"

## 2300020
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec230-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…ひ、ひでえ！<line-break>そこまで言うのかよ…！<end>"
EN: "Th-that's cruel! Did you really have to go that far...?<end>"
KO: "…너, 너무하잖아! 그렇게까지 말하냐…!<end>"

## 2300021
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec230-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ま、待てよ！　もっとがんばれば<line-break>きっと姉ちゃんやオイフェにも<line-break>ヴァンのシャレのよさがわかるって！<end>"
EN: "W-wait! If we keep trying, I'm sure even Sis and Aoife will come to appreciate Van's jokes!<end>"
KO: "자, 잠깐! 좀 더 노력하면 분명 누나랑 오이페도 반의 말장난이 얼마나 좋은지 알게 될 거라고!<end>"

## 2300022
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec230-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

KO: "…말장난을 그만둘 생각은 없는 모양이네.<end>"

## 2310001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec231-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なによ、そのくたびれた顔。<line-break>見てるだけでイライラするわ。<line-break>　<line-break>とっととアタクシの視界から<line-break>消えなさいよぉ！<end>"
EN: "What's with that exhausted look? Just looking at you irritates me. Now hurry up and get out of my sight!<end>"
KO: "뭐야, 그 지친 얼굴은. 보기만 해도 짜증 나. 얼른 내 눈앞에서 사라져 버려!<end>"

## 2310002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec231-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そう言うなよ…。<line-break>俺たちもいろいろ悩んでるんだから。<end>"
EN: "Don't be like that. We've got a lot on our minds too, you know.<end>"
KO: "그렇게 말하지 마…. 우리도 여러 가지로 고민하고 있다고.<end>"

## 2310003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec231-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ハッ！　下等生物が悩む？<line-break>どんなくだらない悩みか知らないけど<line-break>アタクシ相談には乗らなくてよ？<end>"
EN: "Ha! Lower life-forms have problems? I don't know what petty nonsense is troubling you, but don't expect me to give you advice.<end>"
KO: "하! 하등생물이 고민한다고? 얼마나 시시한 고민인지는 모르겠지만 나는 상담 따윈 안 해 줘!<end>"

## 2310004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec231-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "こっちから願い下げだよ。<line-break>どうせ、フェティに相談しても<line-break>解決できっこねえし。<end>"
EN: "Wouldn't dream of asking. It's not like Fety could solve anything anyway.<end>"
KO: "나야말로 사양이야. 어차피 페티한테 상담해도 해결될 리 없고.<end>"

## 2310005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec231-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…し、失礼ね！<line-break>聞き捨てならないわ。<line-break>　<line-break>高貴なエルフのアタクシに<line-break>解決できないことなんて<line-break>この世に存在しないのよ！<line-break>いいから言ってみなさいよ！<line-break>ほら、言いなさいってば！<end>"
EN: "H-how rude! I can't let that pass. There is nothing in this world that I, a noble Elf, cannot solve! Now tell me! Go on, out with it!<end>"
KO: "…시, 실례잖아! 그냥 넘어갈 수 없어. 고귀한 엘프인 내가 해결 못 하는 일 따윈 이 세상에 없다고! 됐으니까 말해 봐! 자, 말하라니까!<end>"

## 2310007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec231-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "面白いシャレで打ち解けようと<line-break>がんばってるのにな。<end>"
EN: "We've been trying to break the ice with funny puns, too.<end>"
KO: "재미있는 농담으로 친해지려고 노력하고 있는데 말이야.<end>"

## 2310008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec231-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…シャレ？<end>"
EN: "Puns...?<end>"
KO: "…샤레?<end>"

## 2310009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec231-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ああ。たとえば、<line-break>セラはいつも冷静であセラない、<line-break>…とか。ウププ…。<end>"
EN: "Yeah. Like, Sera's always so calm, he never gets a-Sera-tated... Heh heh...<end>"
KO: "그래. 예를 들면, 세라는 언제나 침착해서 설라지 않는다, …같은 거. 우푸푸….<end>"

## 2310010
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec231-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "な？　な？　面白いだろ？<line-break>爆笑もんだよな？<end>"
EN: "Well? Well? Funny, right? Hilarious, isn't it?<end>"
KO: "그치? 그치? 재밌지? 빵 터질 만큼 웃기지?<end>"

## 2310011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec231-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "………………！<line-break>………………！<line-break>………………！<line-break>ぜんっぜん面白くないわ！<line-break>果てしなくつまらないわ！<line-break>聞いてるだけでつらくなるわ！！<end>"
EN: "...! ...! ...! That isn't funny at all! It's unspeakably dull! Just hearing it is painful!<end>"
KO: "………………! ………………! ………………! 하나도 안 웃겨! 끝도 없이 재미없어! 듣고 있는 것만으로 괴로워!!<end>"

## 2310012
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec231-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うわっ、そこまで言う！？<end>"
EN: "Whoa, you really had to go that far?!<end>"
KO: "우와, 거기까지 말해!?<end>"

## 2310013
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec231-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "当然でしょ。<line-break>よくそんなつまらないことで<line-break>悩めるわね。<line-break>いくら下等生物だからって<line-break>くだらないにもほどがあるわ！<end>"
EN: "Of course it was. How can you possibly agonize over something so ridiculous? Even for lower life-forms, there are limits to such stupidity!<end>"
KO: "당연하잖아. 어떻게 그런 하찮은 일로 고민할 수 있지? 아무리 하등생물이라도 정도가 있지!<end>"

## 2310014
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec231-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よくも高貴なアタクシの時間を<line-break>浪費させてくれたわね！<line-break>ありえないわ！　何様のつもり！<line-break>だいたいあんなムッツリと<line-break>打ち解けたいなんて、<line-break>発想からして間違ってるわ。<line-break>いかにも下等生物らしい下等な発想ね。<line-break>それにしたって、あなたたちのその<line-break>くだらないシャレは許されないわ！<line-break>アタクシが世界中を見てきた中で<line-break>これほど最低最悪でつまらない<line-break>ダジャレは初めてよ！<end>"
EN: "How dare you waste the time of someone as noble as me! Unbelievable! Who do you think you are? Wanting to get close to someone that sullen and tight-lipped was wrong from the start. The very notion is as base as you'd expect from a lower life-form. Even so, those ridiculous puns of yours are unforgivable! In all my travels around the world, I have never heard such a wretched, appalling, tedious pun!<end>"
KO: "감히 고귀한 이 몸의 시간을 낭비하게 하다니! 말도 안 돼요! 대체 뭐 하는 짓이에요! 애초에 저 말없는 녀석과 친해지고 싶다는 생각부터 틀려먹었어요. 하등생물다운 하등한 발상이네요. 그렇다 해도 당신들의 그 시시한 말장난은 용서 못 해요! 내가 온 세상을 돌아다녀 봤지만 이렇게 최악이고 재미없는 아재개그는 처음이에요!<end>"

## 2310015
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec231-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…ううっ、いつまで続くんだろ？<end>"
EN: "Ugh... How long is this going to last?<end>"
KO: "…으으, 언제까지 계속되는 거지?<end>"

## 2310016
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec231-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "黙って聞きなさいよぉ！<line-break>ありがたがって聞きなさいよぉ！<line-break>感謝の涙を流して聞きなさいよぉ！<line-break>あなたたちがド下等な上に下等すぎて<line-break>完全に、まったく、お話にならないから<line-break>アタクシが言ってあげてるのよー！？<end>"
EN: "Be quiet and listen! Listen and be grateful! Listen with tears of gratitude in your eyes! You are so far beneath even the lowest of lower life-forms that you are utterly, completely beyond hope, and that is why I am deigning to tell you all this!<end>"
KO: "닥치고 들으란 말이야! 감사하면서 들으란 말이야! 감격의 눈물을 흘리면서 들으란 말이야! 너희가 하등한 데다 너무 하등해서 완전히 말도 안 되니까 이 아타쿠시가 친히 말해 주는 거라고!?<end>"

## 2310017
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec231-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だいたい、そういう態度だから、<line-break>くだらないシャレしか<line-break>思いつかないのよ！<line-break>品性も感性もない下等生物は<line-break>アタクシの高貴な言葉を一言一句<line-break>よぉーく噛みしめるべきなのよ！<line-break>そして、少しくらい心に<line-break>しみる言葉というものを<line-break>覚えなさい！<line-break>こんなありがたい機会は、<line-break>下等生物のあなたたちには<line-break>もう二度となくってよー！<end>"
EN: "That attitude is precisely why you can think of nothing but worthless puns! Lower life-forms devoid of refinement or sensibility should savor every last one of my noble words! Then perhaps you might learn to say something that actually touches the heart! Lower life-forms like you will never receive such a precious opportunity again!<end>"
KO: "애초에 그런 태도니까 시시한 말장난밖에 못 떠올리는 거예요! 품위도 감성도 없는 하등생물은 이 고귀한 몸의 말을 한마디 한마디 잘 새겨들어야 해요! 그리고 조금은 마음에 스며드는 말이라는 걸 배우라고요! 이렇게 고마운 기회는 하등생물인 당신들에게 두 번 다시 없을 거예요!<end>"

## 2310018
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec231-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…つか、もう朝なんだけど。<end>"
EN: "I mean... it's morning already.<end>"
KO: "…아니, 벌써 아침인데.<end>"

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

EN: "Please, just let us go...<end>"

## 2310020
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec231-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ちょっとぉぉぉ！！<line-break>アタクシの高貴な話は<line-break>まだ終わってなくてよ！！<line-break>ほら、さっさと起きなさい！<line-break>下等生物！<end>"
EN: "Heeey!! I have not finished my noble discourse yet! Come on, wake up this instant! Lower life-forms!<end>"
KO: "잠깐어어어!! 이 고귀한 나의 이야기는 아직 안 끝났다고!! 자, 얼른 일어나! 하등생물!<end>"

## 2310021
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec231-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お願いだから解放して…。<end>"
EN: "Please, just let us go...<end>"
KO: "부탁이니까 풀어 줘….<end>"

## 2310022
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec231-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ちょっとぉぉぉ！！<line-break>アタクシの高貴な話は<line-break>まだ終わってなくてよ！！<line-break>ほら、さっさと起きなさい！<line-break>下等生物！<end>"
EN: "Heeey!! My noble discourse isn't over yet! Now hurry up and wake up, you lower life-forms!<end>"
KO: "잠깐어어어!! 이 고귀한 나의 이야기는 아직 안 끝났다고!! 자, 얼른 일어나! 하등생물!<end>"

## 2310023
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec231-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ごめんなさい…。<line-break>しばらくシャレは言いません。<line-break>顔を洗って出直します…。<end>"
EN: "I'm sorry... I won't make any more jokes for a while. I'll go wash my face and try again...<end>"
KO: "죄송합니다…. 한동안 말장난은 하지 않겠습니다. 얼굴 씻고 다시 오겠습니다….<end>"

## 2310024
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec231-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アタクシの高貴な言葉を理解できない<line-break>どころか、理解しようとすらしない…。<line-break>下等生物の下等生物たるゆえんだわ。<line-break>ああっ…。なんでアタクシが<line-break>こんな下等生物のために<line-break>時間を浪費しなくてはならないの？<end>"
EN: "Not only do you fail to understand my noble words, you don't even try. That's precisely what makes lower life-forms what they are. Ahh... Why must I waste my time on the likes of you?<end>"
KO: "이 몸의 고귀한 말을 이해하지 못하는 건 둘째치고 이해하려고도 하지 않다니…. 하등생물이 하등생물인 이유가 바로 이거야. 아아…. 왜 이 몸이 이런 하등생물 때문에 시간을 낭비해야 하는 거지?<end>"

## 2310025
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec231-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "自分で勝手に浪費したんじゃねえか…。<line-break>ううっ、眠たいよう。<end>"
EN: "You chose to waste it all on your own... Ugh, I'm so sleepy.<end>"
KO: "네가 멋대로 낭비한 거잖아… 으으, 졸려.<end>"

## 2320004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec232-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "できれば、面白いシャレを通じて…。<line-break>ほら、笑いは人間関係の潤滑油っつーか<line-break>心をなごませるだろ？<end>"

## 2320005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec232-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 2320006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec232-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "たとえば…。<line-break>依頼で、箱を運ぶ。<line-break>…なんて、ウププ。<end>"
EN: "Like... taking a job hauling a box. It was quite a moving experience. Heh heh.<end>"
KO: "예를 들면… 의뢰로 상자를 옮긴다. …같은 거, 우푸푸.<end>"

## 2320008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec232-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まだ持ちネタがあるんなら<line-break>どんどん言ってみてよ。<line-break>あたしが指導してあげるからさ。<end>"
EN: "If you've got any more, let's hear them. I'll coach you.<end>"
KO: "아직 써먹을 소재가 더 있으면 계속 말해 봐. 내가 지도해 줄 테니까.<end>"

## 2320009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec232-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

KO: "…그럼, 로센에서 짐을 내리센!<end>"

## 2320010
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec232-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

EN: "Can you sell armor in Ulkarne? Ul-can!<end>"

## 2320011

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 2320012
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec232-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 2320013
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec232-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "げぼぉっ！<end>"

KO: "우웩!<end>"

## 2320014
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec232-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ひ、ひでえじゃねえか、カルラ！<line-break>いきなり何すんだよ！？<end>"
EN: "Th-that's brutal, Karula! What'd you do that for?!<end>"
KO: "너, 너무하잖아 칼라! 갑자기 무슨 짓이야!?<end>"

## 2320015
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec232-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あまりにもつまらなかったから、<line-break>つい、手が出ちった。<line-break>　<line-break>あんたのダジャレって<line-break>こんなんで終わり？<line-break>ほら、さっさとする。次！<end>"
EN: "It was so painfully unfunny, my hand just slipped. Is that all the puns you've got? Come on, hurry it up. Next!<end>"
KO: "너무 재미없어서 나도 모르게 손이 나갔네. 네 말장난은 이걸로 끝이야? 자, 빨리 해. 다음!<end>"

## 2320016
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec232-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…えっと、じゃあ<line-break>…エルズへ、帰ルズ！<end>"
EN: "Uh... okay, then... Let's go back to Elz... go back-Elz!<end>"
KO: "…그럼, 엘즈로 돌아가자!<end>"

## 2320017
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec232-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ワケわかんないわ。次！<end>"
EN: "That doesn't even make sense. Next!<end>"
KO: "무슨 말인지 모르겠어. 다음!<end>"

## 2320018
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec232-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "冷たいな、姉ちゃん…。<line-break>自分だけさっさと寝ちまうなんて。<end>"
EN: "That's cold... You were the only one who went straight to bed.<end>"
KO: "너무하네, 누나…. 자기만 얼른 자 버리다니.<end>"

## 2320019
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec232-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ほら。次、次っ！<end>"
EN: "Come on. Next, next!<end>"
KO: "자. 다음, 다음!<end>"

## 2320020
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec232-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "起きてよ、姉ちゃん！<line-break>特訓の成果、聞いてほしいんだ。<end>"

KO: "일어나, 누나! 특훈 성과를 들어 줬으면 해.<end>"

## 2320021
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec232-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺、かなり上達したよな？<end>"
EN: "I've gotten a lot better, haven't I?<end>"
KO: "나, 꽤 늘었지?<end>"

## 2320022
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec232-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ああ、抱腹絶倒、間違いなし！<line-break>これでセラも大笑いさ。<end>"

KO: "그래, 배꼽 빠지게 웃을 게 틀림없어! 이걸로 세라도 크게 웃겠지.<end>"

## 2320023
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec232-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 2320026

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

KO: "대, 대단히 발전하고 있어…라고 생각해.<end>"

## 2320027
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec232-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だよね～。<line-break>あたしもダメだと思ったんだ。<end>"
EN: "Right? I didn't think it was working either.<end>"
KO: "그렇지~. 나도 안 될 줄 알았어.<end>"

## 2320028
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec232-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "世の中、あきらめたほうが<line-break>いいこともあるよ。<line-break>じゃね～。<end>"
EN: "Some things in life are better given up on. See you.<end>"
KO: "세상에는 포기하는 편이 좋은 일도 있어. 그럼 안녕~.<end>"

## 2320029
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec232-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺たち、いったい<line-break>何のためにやってたんだよ…。<line-break>あんなにがんばったのに…。<end>"
EN: "Then what was all that for...? We worked so hard...<end>"
KO: "우리 대체 뭘 위해서 했던 거야…. 그렇게 열심히 했는데….<end>"

## 2320030
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec232-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "でしょ。やっぱりね～。<end>"
EN: "See? I knew it.<end>"
KO: "그렇지. 역시 그렇다니까~.<end>"

## 2320031
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec232-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、でも、今面白いのって<line-break>夜中で、疲れてるせいだから。<line-break>　<line-break>夜が明けたら<line-break>絶対つまんないから。<end>"
EN: "Oh, but you only find it funny because it's late and you're tired. Once morning comes, it definitely won't be funny anymore.<end>"
KO: "아, 그래도 지금 재밌는 건 한밤중이고 피곤해서 그런 거야. 날이 밝으면 분명 재미없을 거야.<end>"

## 2330001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec233-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ねぇ、どうしたの？<line-break>ヴァンもチャカも、さえない顔して？<end>"
EN: "Hey, what's wrong? You and Chaka both look so glum, Van.<end>"
KO: "있지, 왜 그래? 반도 챠카도 표정이 안 좋은데?<end>"

## 2330002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec233-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どうせ俺のシャレは冴えないよ…。<end>"
EN: "Yeah, well, my jokes are pretty dull...<end>"
KO: "어차피 내 농담은 재미없어….<end>"

## 2330003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec233-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ルルアンタにはわかんねえだろうな。<line-break>ヴァンと俺がどうやっても、<line-break>セラとなじめない悩みなんて…。<end>"
EN: "You wouldn't understand, Lulluantha. No matter what Van and I do, we just can't seem to get along with Sera...<end>"
KO: "루루안타는 모르겠지. 반과 내가 아무리 해도 세라와 친해지지 못하는 이 고민을….<end>"

## 2330004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec233-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ええっ！？<line-break>そんなことでなやんでたの？<line-break>ルルアンタ、ちっともわかんなかった。<end>"
EN: "What!? That's what you were worried about? Lulluantha had no idea.<end>"
KO: "에엣!? 그런 걸로 고민하고 있었어? 루루안타는 전혀 몰랐어.<end>"

## 2330005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec233-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だったら、そっとしといてくれ…。<end>"
EN: "Then just let it drop...<end>"
KO: "그럼 그냥 내버려 둬….<end>"

## 2330006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec233-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "でも…なやむことないと思うよ？<line-break>だってセラは２人のこと<line-break>きらってないんだし。<end>"
EN: "But... I don't think you need to worry. Sera doesn't dislike either of you.<end>"
KO: "하지만… 고민할 필요 없다고 생각해. 세라는 두 사람을 싫어하는 게 아니잖아.<end>"

## 2330007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec233-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "適当なこと言うなよ…。<end>"
EN: "Don't just make things up...<end>"
KO: "적당한 소리 하지 마….<end>"

## 2330008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec233-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ううん。<line-break>セラは照れ屋さんだから、<line-break>いつもあんな感じなだけだよ。<line-break>本当はパーティーのみんなが<line-break>大好きなんだから。<end>"
EN: "I'm not. Sera's just shy. That's why he's always like that. He really loves everyone in the party.<end>"
KO: "아니. 세라는 부끄럼쟁이라 늘 저런 느낌일 뿐이야. 사실은 파티 모두를 정말 좋아하거든.<end>"

## 2330009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec233-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…って、本人から聞いたのか？<end>"
EN: "Did he actually tell you that?<end>"
KO: "…그걸 본인한테 들은 거야?<end>"

## 2330010
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec233-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふふ、<line-break>そんなの聞かなくっても<line-break>見てればわかるよぉ。<line-break>変な心配しなくたって、<line-break>ありのままの２人でセラと接すれば<line-break>きっと、もっとなかよくなれるって。<line-break>ルルアンタとだって、<line-break>こうしてなかよくなれたじゃない。<line-break>　<line-break>ほら、がんばって！<line-break>ルルアンタ応援してるからね！<end>"
EN: "Hee hee. Lulluantha can tell just by watching. She doesn't need to ask. There's no need to worry like that. Just be yourselves around Sera, and you'll become better friends. You became friends with Lulluantha, didn't you? Come on, keep trying! Lulluantha's cheering for you!<end>"
KO: "후후, 그런 건 묻지 않아도 보고 있으면 알 수 있어. 괜한 걱정하지 말고 있는 그대로의 두 사람으로 세라를 대하면 분명 더 친해질 수 있을 거야. 루루안타와도 이렇게 친해졌잖아. 자, 힘내! 루루안타가 응원할게!<end>"

## 2330011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec233-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今まで気づかなかったけど、<line-break>ルルアンタって、いい娘だよなぁ…。<end>"
EN: "I never noticed before, but Lulluantha's a really sweet girl...<end>"
KO: "지금까지 몰랐는데 루루안타, 좋은 애였구나…<end>"

## 2330012
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec233-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ホント、姉ちゃんとは大違い…。<line-break>将来いいお嫁さんになるんだろうなぁ。<end>"
EN: "She sure is. Nothing like my older sister... She'll make a wonderful wife someday.<end>"
KO: "정말 누나랑은 딴판이네…. 나중에 좋은 아내가 되겠구나.<end>"

## 2330013
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec233-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ルルアンタにホレるるアンタ、ってか？<end>"
EN: "So you're a Lulluantha lover, are ya?<end>"
KO: "루루안타에게 반해버린 루루안타, 랄까?<end>"

## 2330014
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec233-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "プッ…。<end>"
EN: "Pfft...<end>"
KO: "풋….<end>"

## 2330016
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec233-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 2340001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec234-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さっきからコソコソと…。<line-break>俺に何か用か？<end>"
EN: "You've been whispering over there for a while now. What do you want with me?<end>"
KO: "아까부터 몰래몰래…. 나한테 무슨 볼일이야?<end>"

## 2340002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec234-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "別に用ってほどでもないけど…。<line-break>俺たちは、ただセラと<line-break>仲よくなりたくてさ…。<end>"
EN: "It's nothing in particular. We just thought it'd be nice to get to know you, Sera.<end>"
KO: "딱히 볼일이 있는 건 아니지만…. 우린 그냥 세라와 친해지고 싶어서….<end>"

## 2340003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec234-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "たまたま<value:$28>と<line-break>行動を共にして<line-break>お前たちと知り合ったまでだ。<line-break>親しくなる必要はない。<end>"
EN: "I only came to know you because I happened to be traveling with <value:$28>. We have no need to become friends.<end>"
KO: "우연히 <value:$28>과 행동을 함께하다 너희와 알게 됐을 뿐이다. 친해질 필요는 없어.<end>"

## 2340004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec234-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まあ、そう邪険にすんなよ…。<line-break>俺たちはセラが仲間になって<line-break>喜んだんだぜ。<line-break>強い味方が、見っかった！ってな。<line-break>ウププ…。<end>"
EN: "Come on, don't be like that. We were glad when you joined us, Sera. We'd found a mighty ally―my, what a find! Heh heh...<end>"
KO: "뭐, 그렇게 쌀쌀맞게 굴지 마…. 우리는 세라가 동료가 돼서 기뻤다고. 강한 아군을 찾았다! 싶었지. 우푸푸….<end>"

## 2340005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec234-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "プッ…。<line-break>ヴァンのシャレは、おしゃれだな。<line-break>アハハハ…。<end>"
EN: "Pfft... Van sure puts the style in wordplay. Ahahaha...<end>"
KO: "풋… 반의 말장난은 멋지네. 아하하하…<end>"

## 2340007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec234-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アハハハハ！<line-break>面白いだろ、セラ？<end>"
EN: "Ahahahaha! Funny, right, Sera?<end>"
KO: "아하하하하! 재밌지, 세라?<end>"

## 2340008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec234-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "もうひとつ、おまけに…。<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>こんな面白いダジャレを言うのは<line-break>誰じゃ？<line-break>なんつって。ウププ…。<end>"
EN: "And one more for good measure... Who tells jokes this punny? Someone funny, that's who! Heh heh...<end>"
KO: "하나 더 서비스로…. 이런 재미있는 말장난을 하는 사람은 누구냐고? 하하, 우푸푸….<end>"

## 2340010
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec234-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お、面白かったよな、セラ？<line-break>ガマンしないで笑っていいんだぜ！<end>"
EN: "Y-you thought it was funny, right, Sera? Don't hold back―you can laugh!<end>"
KO: "재, 재미있었지, 세라? 참지 말고 웃어도 돼!<end>"

## 2340011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec234-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "大丈夫。セラにだって、<line-break>この面白さは伝わってるはずさ！<end>"
EN: "Don't worry. I'm sure Sera gets how funny it is too!<end>"
KO: "괜찮아. 세라도 이 재미를 분명 알고 있을 거야!<end>"

## 2340013
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec234-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、ああ…。<line-break>面白かった…と思う。<line-break>なんとなくだが…。<end>"
EN: "Y-yes... I suppose it was funny. In a way...<end>"
KO: "아, 아아… 재미있었던… 것 같아. 왠지 모르게…<end>"

## 2340014
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec234-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "き、聞いたか、チャカ？<end>"
EN: "D-did you hear that, Chaka?<end>"
KO: "드, 들었나, 차카?<end>"

## 2340015
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec234-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "もちろんさ、ヴァン。<line-break>セラがわかってくれたよ！<line-break>俺たちの苦労が報われたんだ！<end>"
EN: "Of course, Van. Sera understands us! All our hard work has finally paid off!<end>"
KO: "물론이지, 반. 세라가 알아줬어! 우리 고생이 보답받은 거야!<end>"

## 2340016
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec234-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "姉ちゃん、聞いてくれよ。<line-break>やっと俺たちの思いが通じたんだ。<line-break>　<line-break>セラだって、やっぱり俺たちと<line-break>同じ感性だったんだよ！<end>"
EN: "Hey, listen to this! We finally got through to him. Sera really does have the same sense of humor we do!<end>"
KO: "누나, 들어 봐. 드디어 우리 마음이 통한 거야. 세라도 역시 우리와 같은 감성이었던 거라고!<end>"

## 2340017
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec234-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、いや。勘違いするな。<line-break>俺はただ…。<end>"
EN: "No, wait. Don't get the wrong idea. I only...<end>"
KO: "아, 아니. 오해하지 마. 난 그냥….<end>"

## 2340018
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec234-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "とにかく今日から名実ともに<line-break>セラは俺たちの仲間だ！<end>"
EN: "Anyway, as of today, Sera is officially one of us in every sense!<end>"
KO: "어쨌든 오늘부터 명실상부하게 세라는 우리 동료다!<end>"

## 2340019
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec234-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これからは３人でがんばろうぜ！<end>"
EN: "From now on, let's give it our all, the three of us!<end>"
KO: "앞으로는 셋이서 힘내자!<end>"

## 2340020
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec234-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アハハハハハ！<end>"
EN: "Ahahahahaha!<end>"
KO: "아하하하하하!<end>"

source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec234-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アハハハハハ！<end>"

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 2350002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec235-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おや？　君はたしか…。<end>"
EN: "Oh? You're the one who...<end>"
KO: "어라? 너는 분명….<end>"

## 2350004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec235-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "エルファス様。<line-break>ありがとうございました！<end>"
EN: "Master Elfas, thank you so much!<end>"
KO: "엘파스 님. 감사합니다!<end>"

## 2350005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec235-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いや…。<line-break>僕には礼を受ける資格なんて…。<end>"
EN: "No... I don't deserve your thanks...<end>"
KO: "아니…. 난 감사받을 자격 같은 건 없어….<end>"

## 2350007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec235-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "エルファス様。<line-break>こっちの人はだぁれ？<end>"
EN: "Master Elfas, who's this?<end>"
KO: "엘파스 님. 이쪽 사람은 누구예요?<end>"

## 2350009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec235-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "従者だよ<end>"
EN: "My attendant.<end>"
KO: "종자야<end>"

## 2350010
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec235-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "大事な人だよ<end>"
EN: "Someone important to me.<end>"
KO: "소중한 사람이야<end>"

## 2350011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec235-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そ、そう。僕の従者だよ。<line-break>確か冒険者ギルドで雇ったんだ。<line-break>よく働いてくれている。<end>"
EN: "Y-yes. My attendant. Hired through the Adventurers' Guild, if I remember correctly. Very hardworking.<end>"
KO: "그, 그래. 내 수행원이야. 분명 모험가 길드에서 고용했어. 아주 열심히 일해 주고 있지.<end>"

## 2350012
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec235-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "へえ。さすがはエルファス様。<line-break>とっても強そうな人を<line-break>従者につれているんだね。<end>"
EN: "Wow. I should've known, Master Elfas. Your attendant looks really strong!<end>"
KO: "헤에. 역시 엘파스 님이네. 엄청 강해 보이는 사람을 수행원으로 데리고 있구나.<end>"

## 2350013
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec235-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフッ…。<line-break>強いことは確かだよ。<line-break>　<line-break>その辺の魔物だって<line-break><value:$28>を見たら<line-break>裸足で逃げ出すんじゃないかな。<line-break>ねえ、<value:$28>？<end>"
EN: "Heh... Strong, certainly. I imagine even the monsters around here would run for their lives at the sight of <value:$28>. Isn't that right, <value:$28>?<end>"
KO: "후후…. 강한 건 확실해. 웬만한 마물도 <value:$28>을 보면 맨발로 도망치지 않을까. 그렇지, <value:$28>?<end>"

## 2350014
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec235-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うわぁ。カッコいい。<line-break>こんな人に守られているなら、<line-break>エルファス様も安心だね。<end>"
EN: "Wow, that's so impressive! With someone like that protecting you, you don't have anything to worry about, Master Elfas.<end>"
KO: "우와. 멋있다. 이런 사람에게 보호받고 있다면 엘파스 님도 안심이겠네.<end>"

## 2350015
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec235-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "もちろんさ。<line-break>さあ、お母さんが心配しているよ。<line-break>そろそろ家にお帰り。<end>"
EN: "Of course not. Now, your mother must be worried. It's about time you went home.<end>"
KO: "물론이지. 자, 어머니가 걱정하고 계셔. 이제 집으로 돌아가렴.<end>"

## 2350016
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec235-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うん。またね。<line-break>エルファス様！<line-break>それと従者さん！<end>"
EN: "Okay. See you again, Master Elfas! And you too, attendant!<end>"
KO: "응. 또 봐. 엘파스 님! 그리고 수행원 씨도!<end>"

## 2350017
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec235-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "僕はあんな子供の命まで<line-break>利用しようとしていた…。<line-break>　<line-break>それは許されることでは<line-break>ないのかもしれない。<end>"
EN: "I was even going to use that child's life... Perhaps that can't be forgiven.<end>"
KO: "나는 그런 아이의 목숨까지 이용하려 했어…. 그건 용서받을 일이 아닐지도 몰라.<end>"

## 2350018
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec235-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうだね。もう迷っている暇はない。<line-break>僕は救世主であることを捨て、<line-break>違う道を選んだんだ。<line-break>今はその道を信じて進もう。<line-break>君と一緒に…。<end>"
EN: "Yes. There's no time to waver anymore. I gave up being the Savior and chose a different path. Now I'll trust in that path and follow it, together with you...<end>"
KO: "그래. 이제 망설일 시간은 없어. 나는 구세주이기를 버리고 다른 길을 골랐어. 지금은 그 길을 믿고 나아가자. 너와 함께…<end>"

## 2350019
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec235-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "へぇ。親友なんだぁ！<end>"
EN: "Oh, so you're best friends!<end>"
KO: "헤에. 절친이구나!<end>"

## 2350021
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec235-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ともに同じ目的に向かって進む<line-break>仲間と言うか、同志と言うか…。<line-break>えーと、とにかく違うんだよ。<end>"
EN: "We're companions pursuing the same goal. Or comrades, perhaps... Anyway, we're not best friends.<end>"
KO: "함께 같은 목적을 향해 나아가는 동료랄까, 동지랄까…. 어쨌든 그런 건 아니야.<end>"

## 2350022
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec235-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あー、コホン。ふむ。<line-break>そろそろ、君のお母さんが<line-break>心配しているころだ。<line-break>早く家にお帰り。<end>"
EN: "Er, ahem. Hmm. Your mother must be getting worried by now. Hurry home.<end>"
KO: "아, 흠흠. 슬슬 네 어머니가 걱정하실 때다. 어서 집으로 돌아가렴.<end>"

## 2350023
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec235-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うん。それじゃまたね。<line-break>エルファス様！<line-break>それとエルファス様の大事な人！<end>"
EN: "Okay, see you later, Master Elfas! And you too, the person who's so important to Master Elfas!<end>"
KO: "응. 그럼 또 봐. 엘파스 님! 그리고 엘파스 님의 소중한 사람도!<end>"

## 2350024

EN: "I was even going to use that child's life... Perhaps that can't be forgiven.<end>"
KO: "나는 그런 아이의 목숨까지 이용하려 했어…. 그건 용서받을 일이 아닐지도 몰라.<end>"

source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec235-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 2350026
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec235-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わかった。恋人なんだ！<end>"
EN: "I know! You're lovers!<end>"
KO: "알겠다. 연인이구나!<end>"

## 2350027
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec235-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そ、そんなんじゃない！<end>"
EN: "N-no, it's nothing like that!<end>"
KO: "그, 그런 거 아니야!<end>"

## 2350028
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec235-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、いや。そのつまり…。<line-break>そういう意味の大事な人ではなく、<line-break>えーと、とにかく違うんだよ。<end>"
EN: "No, wait. I mean... not important in that sense. Um, anyway, that's not it.<end>"

## 2350029
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec235-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

EN: "Er, ahem. Hmm. Your mother must be getting worried by now. Hurry home.<end>"

## 2350031
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec235-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "僕はあんな子供の命まで<line-break>利用しようとしていた…。<line-break>　<line-break>それは許されることでは<line-break>ないのかもしれない。<end>"
EN: "I was even going to use that child's life... Perhaps that can't be forgiven.<end>"

## 2350032
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec235-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうだね。もう迷っている暇はない。<line-break>僕は救世主であることを捨て、<line-break>違う道を選んだんだ。<line-break>今はその道を信じて進もう。<line-break>君と一緒に…。<end>"
EN: "Yes. There's no time to waver anymore. I gave up being the Savior and chose a different path. Now I'll trust in that path and follow it, together with you...<end>"
KO: "그래. 이제 망설일 시간은 없어. 나는 구세주이기를 버리고 다른 길을 골랐어. 지금은 그 길을 믿고 나아가자. 너와 함께…<end>"

## 2350033
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec235-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "施文院は解体されたよ…。<line-break>僕が長として率いていた告死天使も<line-break>二度と暗躍することはない。<end>"
EN: "The Inscription Order has been dissolved. And the Angels of Death, which I once led, will never again work from the shadows.<end>"
KO: "시문원은 해체됐어…. 내가 수장으로 이끌던 고사천사도 두 번 다시 암약하지 않을 거야.<end>"

## 2350034
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec235-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "でも、それですべてが<line-break>終わったわけじゃない。<line-break>　<line-break>姉さんを失った哀しみ…。<line-break>ネメアへの憎しみ…。<line-break>　<line-break>告死天使の長として<line-break>多くの命を奪ってきた過去…。<line-break>　<line-break>さらに僕は<line-break>救世主という名のもとに、<line-break>世界を滅ぼそうとした…。<end>"
EN: "But that doesn't mean it's all over. The grief of losing my sister. My hatred for Nemea. My past as Leader of the Angels of Death, taking so many lives. And then, in the name of the Savior, I tried to destroy the world.<end>"
KO: "하지만 그렇다고 모든 게 끝난 건 아니야. 누나를 잃은 슬픔…. 네메아에 대한 증오…. 고사천사의 우두머리로서 수많은 목숨을 앗아 온 과거…. 게다가 나는 구세주라는 이름 아래 세계를 멸망시키려 했어….<end>"

## 2350035
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec235-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "でも、そのすべてを含めて、<line-break>今の僕なんだ…。<line-break>それを否定したりはしない。<end>"
EN: "But all of that is part of who I am now. I won't deny it.<end>"
KO: "하지만 그 모든 걸 포함해서 지금의 나야…. 그걸 부정하진 않을 거야.<end>"

## 2350036
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec235-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これからは、自分の過去と向き合い、<line-break>償いをする旅に出るよ。<line-break>　<line-break>その旅がどれだけの時間を<line-break>必要とするかはわからない。<line-break>でも…。<line-break>無茶な願いを承知の上で、<line-break>その旅に一緒に<line-break>ついてきてほしいんだ。<line-break><value:$28>…。<end>"
EN: "Now I'm setting out on a journey to face my past and make amends. I don't know how long it will take. But... I know I'm asking too much, and still, I want you to come with me, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "이제부터는 내 과거와 마주하고 속죄하는 여행을 떠날 거야. 그 여행이 얼마나 오래 걸릴지는 모르겠어. 그래도…. 무리한 부탁인 줄 알지만 그 여행에 함께 따라와 줬으면 해. <value:$28>….<end>"

## 2350037
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec235-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ありがとう。<value:$28>…。<line-break>僕は君に約束するよ。<line-break>　<line-break>君という光を見つめ続けて、<line-break>僕は歩いていく。<line-break>二度と道を誤らないように…。<end>"
EN: "Thank you, <value:$28>. I promise you this: I'll keep walking with you as my guiding light, so that I never lose my way again.<end>"
KO: "고마워. <value:$28>… 난 네게 약속할게. 너라는 빛을 계속 바라보며 걸어가겠어. 다시는 길을 잘못 들지 않도록…<end>"

## 2350038
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec235-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "施文院は解体されたよ…。<line-break>僕が長として率いていた告死天使も<line-break>二度と暗躍することはない。<end>"
EN: "The Inscription Order has been dissolved. And the Angels of Death, which I once led, will never again work from the shadows.<end>"
KO: "시문원은 해체됐어…. 내가 수장으로 이끌던 고사천사도 두 번 다시 암약하지 않을 거야.<end>"

## 2350039
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec235-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "でも、それですべてが<line-break>終わったわけじゃない。<line-break>　<line-break>姉さんを失った哀しみ…。<line-break>ネメアへの憎しみ…。<line-break>　<line-break>告死天使の長として多くの命を<line-break>奪ってきた過去…。<line-break>　<line-break>さらに僕は<line-break>救世主という名のもとに、<line-break>世界を滅ぼそうとした…。<end>"
EN: "But that doesn't mean it's all over. The grief of losing my sister. My hatred for Nemea. My past as Leader of the Angels of Death, taking so many lives. And then, in the name of the Savior, I tried to destroy the world.<end>"
KO: "하지만 그렇다고 모든 게 끝난 건 아니야. 누나를 잃은 슬픔…. 네메아에 대한 증오…. 고사천사의 우두머리로서 수많은 목숨을 앗아 온 과거…. 게다가 나는 구세주라는 이름 아래 세계를 멸망시키려 했어….<end>"

## 2350040
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec235-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "でも、そのすべてを含めて、<line-break>今の僕なんだ…。<line-break>それは否定しない。<end>"
EN: "But all of that is part of who I am now. I won't deny it.<end>"
KO: "하지만 그 모든 것을 포함해서 지금의 나야… 그건 부정하지 않아.<end>"

## 2350041
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec235-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これからは、自分の過去と向き合い、<line-break>償いをする旅に出るよ。<line-break>　<line-break>その旅がどれだけの時間を<line-break>必要とするかはわからない。<line-break>　<line-break>たぶん長い旅になると思う。<line-break>もしかすると生きている間には<line-break>終わらないかもしれない。<line-break>でも…。それでも僕は君に<line-break>ついてきてほしいんだ。<line-break><value:$28>…。<end>"
EN: "Now I'm setting out on a journey to face my past and make amends. I don't know how long it will take. It will probably be a long journey. It may not even end within my lifetime. But even so... I want you to come with me, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "이제부터는 내 과거와 마주하고 속죄하는 여행을 떠날 거야. 그 여행이 얼마나 오래 걸릴지는 모르겠어. 아마 긴 여행이 되겠지. 어쩌면 살아 있는 동안 끝나지 않을지도 몰라. 그래도…. 그래도 난 네가 함께 와 줬으면 해. <value:$28>….<end>"

## 2350042
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec235-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ありがとう。<value:$28>…。<line-break>僕は君に約束するよ。<line-break>　<line-break>君という光を見つめ続けて、<line-break>僕は歩いていく。<line-break>二度と道を誤らないように…。<end>"
EN: "Thank you, <value:$28>. I promise you this: I'll keep walking with you as my guiding light, so that I never lose my way again.<end>"
KO: "고마워. <value:$28>… 난 네게 약속할게. 너라는 빛을 계속 바라보며 걸어가겠어. 다시는 길을 잘못 들지 않도록…<end>"

## 2350043
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec235-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "PSP用エルファス新イベント用<end>"
EN: ""
KO: "PSP용 엘파스 신규 이벤트용<end>"

## 2360001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec236-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "外の世界に…私を…？<end>"
EN: "Me... into the outside world?<end>"
KO: "바깥세상으로… 나를…?<end>"

## 2360002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec236-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ここが私にとっての世界です。<line-break>他は知りません。<end>"
EN: "This is the whole of my world. I know nothing beyond it.<end>"
KO: "이곳이 제게는 세계의 전부입니다. 다른 곳은 모릅니다.<end>"

## 2360003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec236-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "すでに道具としての私の役目は<line-break>終わりました。<line-break>　<line-break>私にはもう存在する理由も<line-break>価値もないのです。<end>"
EN: "My purpose as a tool has already been fulfilled. I no longer have any reason to exist, nor any worth.<end>"
KO: "도구로서의 제 역할은 이미 끝났습니다. 제게는 이제 존재할 이유도 가치도 없습니다.<end>"

## 2360004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec236-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "新しい役目…？<line-break><line-break><line-break>それを探すべきだと言うのですか？<line-break>あなたと一緒に…？<end>"
EN: "A new purpose...? You believe I should search for one? Together with you...?<end>"
KO: "새로운 역할…? 그걸 찾아야 한다는 건가요? 당신과 함께…?<end>"

## 2360005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec236-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "たとえ、かりそめのもので<line-break>あったとしても<line-break>　<line-break>この命が続いている限り<line-break>私にはやるべきことが<line-break>残されていると…？<end>"
EN: "Even if this life is only temporary... you believe that as long as it continues, there is still something left for me to do?<end>"
KO: "설령 잠시뿐인 목숨이라 해도, 이 생명이 이어지는 동안 내게 해야 할 일이 남아 있다는 건가…?<end>"

## 2360006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec236-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "………………。<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>わかりました。<line-break>もしも私に<line-break>まだ役目が残されているのなら…<line-break>それを探すため、あなたとともに<line-break>外の世界へ参りましょう。<end>"
EN: "...Very well. If there is still some purpose left for me... I shall go with you into the outside world and search for it.<end>"
KO: "………………. 알겠습니다. 만약 제게 아직 역할이 남아 있다면… 그것을 찾기 위해 당신과 함께 바깥 세계로 가겠습니다.<end>"

## 2360007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec236-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ここを出るのは初めてです。<line-break>できる限りのことはしますので<line-break>よろしくお願いします。<end>"
EN: "This will be my first time leaving this place. I shall do everything I can, and I hope I will be of service.<end>"
KO: "여기서 나가는 건 처음입니다. 할 수 있는 데까지 해 볼 테니 잘 부탁드립니다.<end>"

## 2360008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec236-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わかりました。<line-break>では、その猫屋敷で呼ばれるまで、<line-break>お待ちしております。<end>"
EN: "Very well. Then I shall wait at Cat Manor until I am summoned.<end>"
KO: "알겠습니다. 그럼 그 고양이 저택에서 부르실 때까지 기다리고 있겠습니다.<end>"

## 2360009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec236-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "外の世界に…私を…？<end>"
EN: "Me... into the outside world?<end>"
KO: "바깥세상으로… 나를…?<end>"

EN: "My purpose as a tool has already been fulfilled. I no longer have any reason to exist, nor any worth.<end>"

## 2360012
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec236-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あなたが、ラドラスの王であった<line-break>シャロームに造られた人間…？<end>"
EN: "You are a human created by Shalom, the former king of Ladras...?<end>"
KO: "당신이 라드라스의 왕이었던 샬롬에게 만들어진 인간…?<end>"

## 2360013
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec236-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あなたは自分の意志で生きるべき道を<line-break>探しているのですね。<end>"
EN: "You are searching, of your own free will, for the path you should take through life.<end>"
KO: "당신은 자신의 의지로 살아갈 길을 찾고 있는 거군요.<end>"

## 2360014
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec236-part99.toml
