## 2370001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec237-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>様、<line-break>エルファスという方が言っていた<line-break>救いとは何でしょう？<line-break>人はなぜ、救われたいと<line-break>願っているのですか？<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, what is this salvation Elfas spoke of? Why do people wish to be saved?<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>님, 엘파스라는 분이 말한 구원이란 무엇인가요? 사람은 왜 구원받고 싶어 하는 건가요?<end>"

## 2370002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec237-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "何故だろう？<end>"
EN: "I wonder why.<end>"
KO: "왜일까?<end>"

## 2370003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec237-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "幸せになりたいからだ<end>"
EN: "Because people want to be happy.<end>"
KO: "행복해지고 싶어서야.<end>"

## 2370004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec237-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうですか。<line-break><value:$28>様にも<line-break>わからないことがあるのですね。<end>"
EN: "I see. So there are things even you don't know, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "그렇습니까. <value:$28>님도 모르는 것이 있군요.<end>"

## 2370005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec237-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "救いについて<line-break><value:$28>に訊いても<line-break>無意味だよ。<end>"
EN: "There's no point asking <value:$28> about salvation.<end>"
KO: "구원에 대해 <value:$28>에게 물어도 소용없어.<end>"

## 2370007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec237-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>は冒険者だ。<line-break>救いについて説くことなんて<line-break>できやしない。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28> is an adventurer, not someone who can preach about salvation.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>는 모험자야. 구원에 대해 설교할 순 없어.<end>"

## 2370008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec237-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "救世主と名乗っているあなたならば<line-break>救いについて語れるのですか？<end>"
EN: "You call yourself the Savior. Can you tell me what salvation is?<end>"
KO: "구세주라 자칭하는 당신이라면 구원에 대해 말할 수 있습니까?<end>"

## 2370009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec237-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ひとつだけ教えてあげよう。<line-break>人が救いを求めるのは、神にすがり、<line-break>心の支えを得たいからだ。<end>"
EN: "I'll tell you one thing. People seek salvation because they want to turn to God and find something to sustain them.<end>"
KO: "한 가지만 알려 주지. 사람이 구원을 원하는 건 신에게 매달리고 마음의 버팀목을 얻고 싶기 때문이야.<end>"

## 2370010
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec237-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "幸せ…。<line-break>人は幸せを求めるものなのですか？<end>"
EN: "Happiness... Is that what people seek?<end>"
KO: "행복…. 사람은 행복을 추구하는 존재인가요?<end>"

## 2370011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec237-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "救いについて<line-break><value:$28>に訊いても<line-break>無意味だよ。<end>"
EN: "There's no point asking <value:$28> about salvation.<end>"
KO: "구원에 대해 <value:$28>에게 물어도 소용없어.<end>"

## 2370013
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec237-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "君にとって大事なものは何だい？<end>"
EN: "What matters most to you?<end>"
KO: "너에게 소중한 건 뭐지?<end>"

## 2370014
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec237-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "役目を果たすことです。<line-break>そのためだけに私は生きてきました。<end>"
EN: "Fulfilling my duty. That is all I have ever lived for.<end>"
KO: "역할을 다하는 것입니다. 오직 그것만을 위해 저는 살아왔습니다.<end>"

## 2370015
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec237-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうだ。<line-break>それが君の支えであり、<line-break>生きる理由だった。<line-break>同じように、<line-break>人はみな心の支えを求めている。<line-break>　<line-break>だが、何を支えに選ぶかは<line-break>人によって違う。<end>"
EN: "Exactly. That was what sustained you and gave you a reason to live. In the same way, everyone seeks something to sustain them. But what that is differs from one person to another.<end>"
KO: "그래. 그것이 네 버팀목이자 살아가는 이유였다. 마찬가지로 사람은 누구나 마음의 버팀목을 찾는다. 하지만 무엇을 버팀목으로 삼을지는 사람마다 다르지.<end>"

## 2370016
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec237-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "彼らは幸せになるために、<line-break>心の支えとして救いを選んだ。<line-break>　<line-break>そして、その救いを僕に…。<line-break>いや、僕を通して神に求めたのさ。<end>"
EN: "They chose salvation as something to sustain them, so they could find happiness. And they sought that salvation from me... No, from God, through me.<end>"
KO: "그들은 행복해지기 위해 마음의 버팀목으로 구원을 택했어. 그리고 그 구원을 내게… 아니, 나를 통해 신에게 구한 거지.<end>"

## 2370017
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec237-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "神にすがってでも幸せを願う彼らを<line-break>誰も否定はできない。<line-break>　<line-break>たとえ、救いを求めることが<line-break>人の心の弱さから<line-break>来るものだとしてもね。<line-break>何にも頼らずに生きていけるほど<line-break>人は強くないんだ…。<end>"
EN: "No one can condemn them for wishing for happiness, even if they must cling to God for it. The desire for salvation may spring from weakness of heart, but people aren't strong enough to live without something to rely on...<end>"
KO: "신에게 매달려서라도 행복을 바라는 그들을 누구도 부정할 수 없어. 설령 구원을 바라는 마음이 인간 마음의 나약함에서 나오는 것이라 해도 말이야. 아무것에도 기대지 않고 살아갈 만큼 인간은 강하지 않으니까….<end>"

## 2370018
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec237-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "君も僕も同じだよ。<line-break>僕は救世主という道を選び、<line-break>君は役目を果たすという道を選んだ。<end>"
EN: "You and I are no different. I chose the path of the Savior, and you chose the path of fulfilling your duty.<end>"
KO: "너나 나나 똑같아. 나는 구세주라는 길을 택했고, 너는 역할을 다하는 길을 택했지.<end>"

## 2370019
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec237-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そして、今もそれを支えにして<line-break>生きている…。<end>"
EN: "And even now, we still live by it...<end>"
KO: "그리고 지금도 그걸 버팀목 삼아 살아가고 있어….<end>"

## 2370020
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec237-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>様、<line-break>エルファスという方が言っていた<line-break>救いとは何でしょう？<line-break>人はなぜ、救われたいと<line-break>願っているのですか？<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, what is the salvation Elfas spoke of? Why do people long to be saved?<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>님, 엘파스라는 분이 말한 구원이란 무엇인가요? 사람은 왜 구원받고 싶어 하는 건가요?<end>"

## 2370021
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec237-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "何故だろう？<end>"
EN: "I wonder why.<end>"
KO: "왜일까?<end>"

## 2370022
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec237-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "幸せになりたいからだ<end>"
EN: "Because people want to be happy.<end>"
KO: "행복해지고 싶어서야.<end>"

## 2370023
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec237-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうですか。<line-break><value:$28>様にも<line-break>わからないことがあるのですね。<end>"
EN: "I see. So there are things even you do not know, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "그렇습니까. <value:$28>님도 모르는 것이 있군요.<end>"

## 2370024
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec237-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "救いについて<line-break><value:$28>に訊いても<line-break>無意味だよ。<end>"
EN: "There is no point asking <value:$28> about salvation.<end>"
KO: "구원에 대해 <value:$28>에게 물어도 소용없어.<end>"

## 2370026
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec237-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>は冒険者だ。<line-break>救いについて説くことなんて<line-break>できやしない。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28> is an adventurer, not someone who can preach about salvation.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>는 모험자야. 구원에 대해 설교할 순 없어.<end>"

## 2370027
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec237-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "救世主と名乗っているあなたならば<line-break>救いについて語れるのですか？<end>"
EN: "And since you call yourself the Savior, can you speak of salvation?<end>"
KO: "구세주라 자칭하는 당신이라면 구원에 대해 말할 수 있습니까?<end>"

## 2370028
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec237-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ひとつだけ教えてあげよう。<line-break>人が救いを求めるのは、神にすがり、<line-break>心の支えを得たいからだ。<end>"
EN: "I will tell you one thing. People seek salvation because they want to cling to God and find something to sustain them.<end>"
KO: "한 가지만 알려 주지. 사람이 구원을 원하는 건 신에게 매달리고 마음의 버팀목을 얻고 싶기 때문이야.<end>"

## 2370029
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec237-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "幸せ…。<line-break>人は幸せを求めるものなのですか？<end>"
EN: "Happiness... Is that what people seek?<end>"
KO: "행복…. 사람은 행복을 추구하는 존재인가요?<end>"

## 2370030
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec237-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "救いについて<line-break><value:$28>に訊いても<line-break>無意味だよ。<end>"
EN: "There is no point asking <value:$28> about salvation.<end>"
KO: "구원에 대해 <value:$28>에게 물어도 소용없어.<end>"

## 2370032
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec237-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "君にとって大事なものは何だい？<end>"
EN: "What matters most to you?<end>"
KO: "너에게 소중한 건 뭐지?<end>"

## 2370033
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec237-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "役目を果たすことです。<line-break>そのためだけに私は生きてきました。<end>"
EN: "Fulfilling my duty. That alone is what I have lived for.<end>"
KO: "역할을 다하는 것입니다. 오직 그것만을 위해 저는 살아왔습니다.<end>"

## 2370034
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec237-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうだ。<line-break>それが君の支えであり、<line-break>生きる理由だった。<line-break>同じように、<line-break>人はみな心の支えを求めている。<line-break>　<line-break>だが、何を支えに選ぶかは<line-break>人によって違う。<end>"
EN: "Exactly. It sustained you and gave you a reason to live. In the same way, everyone seeks something to sustain them. But what they choose is different for each person.<end>"
KO: "그래. 그것이 네 버팀목이자 살아가는 이유였다. 마찬가지로 사람은 누구나 마음의 버팀목을 찾는다. 하지만 무엇을 버팀목으로 삼을지는 사람마다 다르지.<end>"

## 2370035
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec237-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "彼らは幸せになるために、<line-break>心の支えとして救いを選んだ。<line-break>　<line-break>そして、その救いを僕に…。<line-break>いや、僕を通して神に求めたのさ。<end>"
EN: "They chose salvation as the thing that would sustain them and bring them happiness. And they sought that salvation from me... No, from God through me.<end>"
KO: "그들은 행복해지기 위해 마음의 버팀목으로 구원을 택했어. 그리고 그 구원을 내게… 아니, 나를 통해 신에게 구한 거지.<end>"

## 2370036
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec237-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "神にすがってでも幸せを願う彼らを<line-break>誰も否定はできない。<line-break>　<line-break>たとえ、救いを求めることが<line-break>人の心の弱さから<line-break>来るものだとしてもね。<line-break>何にも頼らずに生きていけるほど<line-break>人は強くないんだ…。<end>"
EN: "No one can condemn them for longing for happiness, even if they must cling to God to find it―even if the desire for salvation springs from human weakness. People aren't strong enough to live without relying on anything...<end>"
KO: "신에게 매달려서라도 행복을 바라는 그들을 누구도 부정할 수 없어. 설령 구원을 바라는 마음이 인간 마음의 나약함에서 나오는 것이라 해도 말이야. 아무것에도 기대지 않고 살아갈 만큼 인간은 강하지 않으니까….<end>"

## 2370037
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec237-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "君も僕も同じだよ。<line-break>僕は救世主という道を選び、<line-break>君は役目を果たすという道を選んだ。<end>"
EN: "You and I are no different. I chose the path of the Savior, and you chose the path of fulfilling your duty.<end>"
KO: "너나 나나 똑같아. 나는 구세주라는 길을 택했고, 너는 역할을 다하는 길을 택했지.<end>"

## 2370038
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec237-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そして、今もそれを支えにして<line-break>生きている…。<end>"
EN: "And even now, those paths are what keep us going.<end>"
KO: "그리고 지금도 그걸 버팀목 삼아 살아가고 있어….<end>"

source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec237-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうだ。<line-break>それが君の支えであり、<line-break>生きる理由だった。<line-break>同じように、<line-break>人はみな心の支えを求めている。<line-break>　<line-break>だが、何を支えに選ぶかは<line-break>人によって違う。<end>"
EN: "Exactly. That was what sustained you and gave you a reason to live. In the same way, everyone seeks something to sustain them. But what they choose differs from person to person.<end>"
KO: "그래. 그것이 네 버팀목이자 살아가는 이유였다. 마찬가지로 사람은 누구나 마음의 버팀목을 찾는다. 하지만 무엇을 버팀목으로 삼을지는 사람마다 다르지.<end>"

## 2370073
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec237-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "彼らは幸せになるために、<line-break>心の支えとして救いを選んだ。<line-break>　<line-break>そして、その救いを僕に…。<line-break>いや、僕を通して神に求めたのさ。<end>"
EN: "They chose salvation as something to sustain them in their pursuit of happiness. And they sought that salvation from me... No, from God through me.<end>"
KO: "그들은 행복해지기 위해 마음의 버팀목으로 구원을 택했어. 그리고 그 구원을 내게… 아니, 나를 통해 신에게 구한 거지.<end>"

## 2370074
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec237-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "神にすがってでも幸せを願う彼らを<line-break>誰も否定はできない。<line-break>　<line-break>たとえ、救いを求めることが<line-break>人の心の弱さから<line-break>来るものだとしてもね。<line-break>何にも頼らずに生きていけるほど<line-break>人は強くないんだ…。<end>"
EN: "No one can condemn them for seeking happiness, even if it means clinging to God. Even if the desire for salvation springs from weakness in the human heart. People aren't strong enough to live without relying on anything...<end>"
KO: "신에게 매달려서라도 행복을 바라는 그들을 누구도 부정할 수 없어. 설령 구원을 바라는 마음이 인간 마음의 나약함에서 나오는 것이라 해도 말이야. 아무것에도 기대지 않고 살아갈 만큼 인간은 강하지 않으니까….<end>"

## 2370075
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec237-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "君も僕も同じだよ。<line-break>僕は救世主という道を選び、<line-break>君は役目を果たすという道を選んだ。<end>"
EN: "You and I are the same. I chose the path of the Savior, and you chose the path of fulfilling your duty.<end>"
KO: "너나 나나 똑같아. 나는 구세주라는 길을 택했고, 너는 역할을 다하는 길을 택했지.<end>"

## 2370076
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec237-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そして、今もそれを支えにして<line-break>生きている…。<end>"
EN: "And even now, that is what sustains us and keeps us alive...<end>"
KO: "그리고 지금도 그걸 버팀목 삼아 살아가고 있어….<end>"

## 2370077
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec237-part99.toml

EN: ""
KO: "PSP용 플레어 신규 이벤트 02용<end>"

source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec238-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "PSP用フレア新イベント03用<end>"

KO: "PSP용 플레어 신규 이벤트 03용<end>"

## 2380001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec238-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>様…。<line-break>少しお話ししてもよろしいですか？<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>... may I speak with you for a moment?<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>님…. 잠시 이야기해도 괜찮을까요?<end>"

## 2380002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec238-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あのエルファスというお方は、<line-break>私も道を選んで生きていると<line-break>言っていました。<line-break>でも、今まで私は<line-break>他者から与えられた役目を<line-break>果たしてきただけです…。<line-break>それでも、私は<line-break>選んでいると言えるのでしょうか？<end>"
EN: "Elfas said that I, too, am living by choosing my own path. But until now, all I have done is fulfill the roles others gave me... Can that truly be called choosing?<end>"
KO: "그 엘파스라는 분은 자신도 길을 선택하며 살아가고 있다고 말했습니다. 하지만 지금까지 저는 다른 이가 준 역할을 다해 왔을 뿐입니다…. 그래도 제가 선택하며 살고 있다고 말할 수 있을까요?<end>"

## 2380004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec238-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "言える<end>"
EN: "You can.<end>"
KO: "말할 수 있어<end>"

## 2380005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec238-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "つまらないことを訊いてしまいました。<line-break>どうか忘れてください。<end>"
EN: "I should not have troubled you with such a foolish question. Please forget I asked.<end>"
KO: "쓸데없는 걸 물었군요. 부디 잊어주세요.<end>"

## 2380006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec238-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おやすみなさい…。<end>"
EN: "Good night...<end>"
KO: "안녕히 주무세요….<end>"

## 2380007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec238-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…ですが、私は<line-break>ただの土くれにすぎません。<line-break>　<line-break>そんな物でも、いつの日か<line-break>自分で何かを選び取ることが<line-break>可能でしょうか？<end>"
EN: "...But I am nothing more than a lump of earth. Could even a thing like that someday choose something for itself?<end>"
KO: "…하지만 저는 그저 흙덩이에 불과합니다. 그런 것이라도 언젠가 스스로 무언가를 선택할 수 있을까요?<end>"

## 2380008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec238-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "もしも、いつの日か<line-break>私自身が心から欲しいと望むものが<line-break>できたとしたら…。<line-break>それは自分で選んだということに<line-break>なるのでしょうか？<end>"
EN: "If someday there were something I truly desired with all my heart... would that mean I had chosen it for myself?<end>"
KO: "만약 언젠가 제 자신이 진심으로 원하는 것이 생긴다면… 그건 제가 스스로 선택한 것이 되는 걸까요?<end>"

## 2380009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec238-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "もし、その時が来たのなら、<line-break>迷うことなく手を伸ばすべきだと<line-break>おっしゃるのですね？<end>"
EN: "You are saying that if that time comes, I should reach for it without hesitation?<end>"
KO: "만약 그때가 온다면 망설이지 말고 손을 뻗어야 한다는 말씀이시군요?<end>"

## 2380010
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec238-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>様の手は温かい。<line-break>今はこの温もりだけが、<line-break>私に安らぎを与えてくれます。<end>"
EN: "Your hand is warm, <value:$28>. Right now, this warmth alone brings me peace.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>님의 손은 따뜻하군요. 지금은 이 온기만이 제게 안식을 줍니다.<end>"

## 2380011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec238-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "外の世界に出てまだ日の浅い私には<line-break>理解できないことばかりで<line-break>とまどっています。<line-break>でも、いつの日かあなたと同じように<line-break>自分で道を選び、心から欲しいものを<line-break>得ることができれば…。<line-break>そして、かなうのならば、その時に<line-break>あなたがそばにいてくれれば…。<line-break>そう願っています。<end>"
EN: "Having spent so little time in the outside world, I am bewildered by how much I still do not understand. But I hope that someday, like you, I may choose my own path and attain what I truly desire... and, if possible, that you will be beside me when that time comes. That is my wish.<end>"
KO: "바깥세상에 나온 지 얼마 되지 않은 저는 이해할 수 없는 것투성이라 혼란스럽습니다. 하지만 언젠가 당신처럼 스스로 길을 고르고 진심으로 원하는 것을 얻을 수 있다면…. 그리고 가능하다면 그때 당신이 곁에 있어 준다면…. 그렇게 바라고 있습니다.<end>"

source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec238-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "PSP用フレア新イベント03用<end>"
EN: ""
KO: "PSP용 플레어 신규 이벤트 03용<end>"

## 2390002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec239-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "危ないっ！<end>"
EN: "Look out!<end>"
KO: "위험해!<end>"

## 2390003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec239-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "間一髪だったな。<line-break>………………？<line-break>　<line-break>まさか、こんな所で<line-break>無限のソウルの可能性を<line-break>秘めている者と出会うとは…。<end>"
EN: "That was close. ...? Never did I expect to encounter someone with the potential to bear an Infinite Soul in a place like this...<end>"
KO: "간발의 차였군. …………? 설마 이런 곳에서 무한의 소울 가능성을 지닌 자를 만나게 될 줄이야….<end>"

## 2390004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec239-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いったい、ここで何をしていた？<end>"
EN: "What in the world were you doing here?<end>"
KO: "도대체 여기서 뭘 하고 있었지?<end>"

## 2390005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec239-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "気がついたらここにいただと？<line-break>それ以外は何も覚えていないのか？<line-break>　<line-break>…ならば名前はどうだ？<end>"
EN: "You came to and found yourself here? And you remember nothing else? ...What about your name?<end>"
KO: "정신을 차려 보니 여기였다고? 그 밖에는 아무것도 기억나지 않나? …그럼 이름은?<end>"

## 2390006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec239-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>か。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, is it?<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>인가.<end>"

## 2390007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec239-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おそらく魔物に襲われた恐怖で<line-break>一時的に記憶が失われているのだろう。<end>"
EN: "The terror of being attacked by a monster has probably caused some temporary memory loss.<end>"
KO: "아마 마물에게 습격당한 공포 때문에 일시적으로 기억을 잃은 거겠지.<end>"

## 2390008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec239-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "仕方がない。出口まではつれていく。<line-break>あとは自分で何とかしたまえ。<end>"
EN: "Very well. I will take you as far as the exit. After that, you must fend for yourself.<end>"
KO: "어쩔 수 없군. 출구까지는 데려다주겠다. 나머지는 스스로 어떻게든 하게.<end>"

## 2390009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec239-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これからどうすればいいかだと？<line-break>それは私の関知することではない。<line-break>　<line-break>私の名はベルゼーヴァ。<line-break>これでも一国の宰相なのでな。<line-break>思いのほか、忙しい身だ。<end>"
EN: "What should you do now, you ask? That is no concern of mine. My name is Beelzeva. I may not look it, but I am the chancellor of a nation. I am rather busier than you might imagine.<end>"
KO: "앞으로 어떻게 해야 하냐고? 그건 내가 관여할 일이 아니다. 내 이름은 벨제바. 이래 봬도 한 나라의 재상이라 생각보다 바쁜 몸이다.<end>"

## 2390010
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec239-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "待て、<value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "Wait, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "기다려, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 2390011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec239-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "とりあえず<line-break>当座の金が必要ならば<line-break>町のギルドにでも行くのだな。<line-break>そこで冒険者として仕事をこなせば<line-break>食い物と寝場所には困らない。<end>"
EN: "If you need money to tide you over, go to the Guild in town. Take on work there as an adventurer, and you should have no trouble finding food and a place to sleep.<end>"
KO: "당장 돈이 필요하다면 마을 길드라도 가 봐. 거기서 모험자로 일을 하면 먹을 것과 잘 곳은 걱정하지 않아도 된다.<end>"

## 2390012
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec239-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "もうひとつ忠告しておく。<line-break>この魔道の塔に再び入ろうなどとは<line-break>ゆめゆめ思わぬことだ。<line-break>少なくとも、それ相応の実力が<line-break>ともなわぬうちはな。<end>"
EN: "One more word of warning: do not even think of entering the Arcane Tower again. Not, at least, until you possess the requisite ability.<end>"
KO: "한 가지 더 충고하지. 이 마도의 탑에 다시 들어올 생각은 꿈에도 하지 마라. 적어도 그에 걸맞은 실력을 갖추기 전에는.<end>"

## 2390013
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec239-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ひとつだけ忠告しておくが<line-break>この魔道の塔に再び入ろうなどは<line-break>ゆめゆめ思わぬことだ。<line-break>今回は運良く私が通りかかったが、<line-break>そんな偶然は二度と<line-break>期待せぬほうがいい。<end>"
EN: "One word of warning: do not even think of entering the Arcane Tower again. You were fortunate that I happened by this time. Do not expect such luck twice.<end>"
KO: "한 가지만 충고해 두지. 이 마도의 탑에 다시 들어올 생각은 꿈에도 하지 마라. 이번에는 운 좋게 내가 지나가다 발견했지만, 그런 우연이 두 번이나 일어날 거라 기대하지 않는 게 좋다.<end>"

## 2390014
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec239-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いったい、ここで何をしていた？<end>"
EN: "What in the world were you doing here?<end>"
KO: "도대체 여기서 뭘 하고 있었지?<end>"

## 2400001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec240-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いらっしゃい。冒険者登録かね？<line-break>だったら、ここに名前を書いておくれ。<end>"
EN: "Welcome. Here to register as an adventurer? Then write your name here.<end>"
KO: "어서 오게. 모험자 등록인가? 그렇다면 여기에 이름을 써 줘.<end>"

## 2400002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec240-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふむ。名前は<value:$28>と。<line-break>幻術計振れておらず、<line-break>犯罪登録にも当てはまらず…。<line-break>よし、これで登録は終了だ。<line-break>お前さんは今日から冒険者だよ。<line-break>　<line-break>ああ、そうそう。あんたは<line-break>初心者だから最初に教えておこう。<line-break>手に入れたソウルはちゃんと宿すこと。<line-break>最近これを忘れる冒険者が多くてね。<line-break>ああそれと、こいつも注意して<line-break>おかなきゃならんな。<line-break>冒険者は、町の中ではひとりで行動し、<line-break>町の外に出て冒険するときだけ<line-break>パーティーと一緒になるのが基本だ。<line-break>だから町の中を歩いているときは、<line-break>何かあっても仲間が助けに来る<line-break>とは限らない。油断しないようにな。<end>"
EN: "Hmm. Name: <value:$28>. No response on the illusion gauge, and no match in the criminal registry... Good. That completes your registration. As of today, you're an adventurer. Oh, one more thing. Since you're new, let me give you a little advice. Make sure you take in any Souls you acquire. A lot of adventurers have been forgetting that lately. And there's something else you should bear in mind. As a rule, adventurers act alone in town and only join a party when venturing outside. So if something happens while you're walking around town, don't count on your companions coming to your aid. Keep your guard up.<end>"
KO: "흠. 이름은 <value:$28>…. 환술계에 걸려 있지도 않고 범죄 등록에도 해당하지 않고…. 좋아, 이걸로 등록은 끝이다. 넌 오늘부터 모험자야. 아, 그래. 넌 초보니까 먼저 알려 두지. 얻은 소울은 제대로 깃들일 것. 요즘 이걸 잊는 모험자가 많아서 말이야. 그리고 이것도 주의해야 해. 모험자는 마을 안에서는 혼자 행동하고 마을 밖으로 나가 모험할 때만 파티와 함께하는 게 기본이다. 그러니 마을 안을 걸을 때는 무슨 일이 생겨도 동료가 도우러 올 거라고 장담할 수 없어. 방심하지 마.<end>"

## 2400003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec240-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まぁ、町の中にある塔や廃城とかを<line-break>調べるときなんかは、一緒に行動する<line-break>こともあるんだろうがな。<line-break>説明はこんなところだ。<line-break>さあ、<value:$28>。　<line-break>これからは、バリバリ仕事して<line-break>名を上げていってくれよ。<end>"
EN: "Though I suppose you might work together when investigating a tower in town, or the Ruined Castle. That's about all there is to explain. Now then, <value:$28>. Get out there, work hard, and make a name for yourself.<end>"
KO: "뭐, 마을 안의 탑이나 폐성을 조사할 때는 함께 행동할 수도 있겠지. 설명은 이 정도다. 자, <value:$28>. 이제부터 열심히 일해서 이름을 날려 줘.<end>"

## 2400004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec240-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "この感覚……無限のソウル…。<end>"
EN: "This sensation... the Infinite Soul...<end>"
KO: "이 감각……무한의 소울….<end>"

## 2400005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec240-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あっ、ごめんなさい。<line-break>あなたがわたしの知り合いに<line-break>似てたから…。<line-break>その人のことを<line-break>ちょっと思い出しちゃって…。<end>"
EN: "Oh, I'm sorry. You look like someone I know... It brought back a few memories.<end>"
KO: "아, 미안해요. 당신이 아는 사람을 닮아서…. 그 사람 생각이 좀 나 버렸어요….<end>"

## 2400006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec240-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふーん、あなた<line-break><value:$28>っていうんだ。<end>"
EN: "So your name is <value:$28>, huh?<end>"
KO: "흐응, 네 이름이 <value:$28>구나.<end>"

## 2400007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec240-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ちょうどいいわ。私ひとりなの。<line-break>一緒に組まない？<line-break>　<line-break>自分で言うのも何だけど、<line-break>そこいらの冒険者より<line-break>よっぽど役に立つと思うわ。<end>"
EN: "Perfect timing. I'm on my own right now. Want to team up? I know it sounds boastful, but I think I'd be a lot more useful than most adventurers around here.<end>"
KO: "마침 잘됐네. 나 혼자야. 같이 할래? 내 입으로 말하긴 뭐하지만 웬만한 모험자보다 훨씬 도움 될 거야.<end>"

## 2400008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec240-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "悩む必要なんてないでしょ。<line-break><value:$28>は初心者で、<line-break>あたしは経験者なんだから。<end>"
EN: "What's there to think about? You're a beginner, <value:$28>, and I'm the experienced one.<end>"
KO: "고민할 필요 없잖아. <value:$28>은 초보자고 난 경험자니까.<end>"

## 2400009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec240-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ありがとう。<line-break>あたしはイーシャ。よろしくね。<line-break>　<line-break>そうと決まれば、さっそく仕事の<line-break>依頼を見てみましょ。<end>"
EN: "Thanks. I'm Iria. It's nice to meet you. Now that that's settled, let's take a look at the available jobs.<end>"
KO: "고마워. 나는 이샤야. 잘 부탁해. 그렇게 정했으면 바로 일 의뢰를 보러 가자.<end>"

## 2400010
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec240-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今ある依頼はこんなところだな。<end>"
EN: "These are all the requests we have at the moment.<end>"
KO: "지금 있는 의뢰는 이 정도군.<end>"

## 2400011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec240-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうね。これが手頃かしら。<line-break>賢者の森でナジラネの果実を採取。<line-break>　<line-break>賢者の森ならここから近いし、<line-break>ナジラネの果実はけっこう臭いけど<line-break>我慢できないほどじゃないわ。<end>"
EN: "Let's see... This one should be manageable. Gather Najirane Fruit in Sage's Forest. Sage's Forest is close by, and while Najirane Fruit smells pretty bad, it's not unbearable.<end>"
KO: "그래. 이 정도가 적당하겠네. 현자의 숲에서 나지라네 열매를 채집. 현자의 숲이면 여기서 가깝고 나지라네 열매는 꽤 냄새나지만 못 참을 정도는 아니야.<end>"

## 2400012
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec240-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これがいいわ。決定ね。<line-break>ほら、さっさと行きましょ。<end>"
EN: "This one, then. It's settled. Come on, let's get going.<end>"
KO: "이걸로 하자. 결정이야. 자, 얼른 가자.<end>"

source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec240-part99.toml

source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec241-part99.toml

EN: ""

## 2410001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec241-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "団長。本当にこの先に<line-break>賢者が住んでいる屋敷が<line-break>あるんでゴブか？<end>"
EN: "Captain, is there really a mansion up ahead where a sage lives, gob?<end>"
KO: "단장. 정말 이 앞에 현자가 사는 저택이 있는 고브?<end>"

## 2410002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec241-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うむ。間違いはないゴブ。<line-break>信じて進むゴブ。<end>"
EN: "Indeed. There's no mistake, gob. Have faith and press on.<end>"
KO: "음. 틀림없다 고브. 믿고 나아간다 고브.<end>"

## 2410003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec241-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "でも噂によると選ばれた者しか<line-break>行き着けないと聞いたゴブ…。<end>"
EN: "But I heard only the chosen can ever reach it, gob...<end>"
KO: "하지만 소문으로는 선택받은 자만 도달할 수 있다고 들었다 고브…<end>"

## 2410004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec241-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "む、心配ないゴブ。<line-break>聖杯を得た我らゴフゴブ団が<line-break>選ばれし者でないわけがないゴブ。<end>"
EN: "No need to worry, gob. We of the Gob-Gob Gang obtained the Grail. How could we possibly not be the chosen ones?<end>"
KO: "음, 걱정 없다 고브. 성배를 손에 넣은 우리 고브고브단이 선택받은 자가 아닐 리 없다 고브.<end>"

## 2410005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec241-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そ、そうでゴブね。<line-break>何か希望が出てきたでゴブ。<end>"
EN: "Y-You're right, gob. I'm starting to feel hopeful.<end>"
KO: "그, 그렇군 고브. 뭔가 희망이 생긴 것 같아 고브.<end>"

## 2410006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec241-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "えっ？<line-break>なんで、ゴブリンが<line-break>人間の言葉をしゃべれるの？<line-break>こいつら外見こそゴブリンだけど<line-break>ひょっとして新種の魔物かしら？<end>"
EN: "What? How can Goblins speak the human tongue? They look like Goblins, but could they be some new kind of monster?<end>"
KO: "어? 왜 고블린이 인간 말을 할 수 있는 거야? 이 녀석들 겉모습은 고블린인데 혹시 신종 마물인가?<end>"

## 2410008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec241-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やっぱり、このゴブリンたち<line-break>人の言葉をしゃべってる！？<line-break>　<line-break><value:$28>、<line-break>最近、世界中で<line-break>おかしなことが起こっているの。<line-break>大地震もそうだし、<line-break>隠れ里が魔人に、<line-break>焼き払われたって話も聞いたわ…。<end>"
EN: "They really are speaking the human tongue! <value:$28>, strange things have been happening all over the world lately. There was that great earthquake, and I heard a hidden village was burned to the ground by demons...<end>"
KO: "역시 이 고블린들, 사람 말을 하고 있어!? <value:$28>, 요즘 세계 곳곳에서 이상한 일이 벌어지고 있어. 대지진도 그렇고, 숨겨진 마을이 마인에게 불탔다는 이야기도 들었어….<end>"

## 2410009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec241-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "むっ！？　この妄想女。<line-break>栄えある我らゴブゴブ団が<line-break>村を焼き討ちしたと言ってるゴブ。<end>"
EN: "What!? That deluded woman is accusing the illustrious Gob-Gob Gang of burning down the village, gob!<end>"
KO: "뭣!? 이 망상녀. 영광스러운 우리 고브고브단이 마을을 불태웠다고 말하는 고브.<end>"

## 2410010
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec241-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "えっ！？<line-break>そ、そんなこと<line-break>言ってない…。<end>"
EN: "What!? I-I never said that...<end>"
KO: "뭐!? 그, 그런 말 안 했어….<end>"

## 2410011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec241-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "焼き討ちの罪を<line-break>我らにかぶせるつもりゴブね！？<line-break>なんでそんなヒドイことをするゴブか？<end>"
EN: "You're trying to pin the burning on us, gob!? Why would you do something so awful?<end>"
KO: "방화죄를 우리한테 뒤집어씌우려는 거냐 고브!? 왜 그런 심한 짓을 하는 거냐 고브?<end>"

## 2410012
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec241-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "マルーン、<line-break>最近、世界中で<line-break>おかしなことが起こってるゴブ。<line-break>大地震もそうだし、<line-break>隠れ里が魔人に<line-break>焼き払われたという話も聞いたゴブ。<line-break>それでこの妄想女も<line-break>気が動転してしまって、<line-break>妄想癖がひどくなってるゴブよ。<end>"
EN: "Maroon, strange things have been happening all over the world lately, gob. There was that great earthquake, and I heard a hidden village was burned to the ground by demons. All that must have rattled this deluded woman and made her delusions even worse.<end>"
KO: "마룬, 요즘 세상 곳곳에서 이상한 일이 벌어지고 있다 고브. 대지진도 그렇고, 숨겨진 마을이 마인에게 불태워졌다는 이야기도 들었다 고브. 그래서 이 망상녀도 정신이 나가서 망상벽이 더 심해졌다 고브.<end>"

## 2410013
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec241-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…そうだったでゴブか<line-break>そういう意味では、この妄想女も<line-break>被害者だったゴブね？<end>"
EN: "Oh... Is that what happened, gob? Then I suppose this deluded woman is a victim too?<end>"
KO: "…그랬던 거 고브? 그런 의미에선 이 망상녀도 피해자였던 거 고브네?<end>"

## 2410014
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec241-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "もう！　被害者違う！！<line-break>って言うか、それ、<line-break>さっき私が言ったこと…！<end>"
EN: "Enough! I'm not a victim! And anyway, you just repeated exactly what I said...!<end>"
KO: "정말! 피해자가 다르잖아!! 아니, 그거, 아까 내가 한 말이잖아…!<end>"

## 2410015
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec241-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それにしてもこの妄想女の<line-break>礼儀知らずにもほどがあるゴブ！<line-break>団長、どうするゴブ？<end>"
EN: "Still, this deluded woman's rudeness knows no bounds, gob! Captain, what should we do?<end>"
KO: "그건 그렇고 이 망상 여자는 무례해도 너무 무례한 고브! 단장, 어떻게 할 고브?<end>"

## 2410016
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec241-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よし、マルーン。<line-break>お前がひとつ懲らしめてやるでゴブ。<end>"
EN: "Very well, Maroon. You teach her a lesson, gob.<end>"
KO: "좋아, 마룬. 네가 한 번 혼내 줘라 고브.<end>"

## 2410017
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec241-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ええっ！？<end>"
EN: "What!?<end>"
KO: "뭐!?<end>"

## 2410018
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec241-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "親分、<line-break>こんなところで争っても<line-break>幸せにはなれないゴブ。<line-break>賢者様の屋敷を<line-break>探すのが先ゴブ。<end>"
EN: "Boss, fighting here won't bring us happiness, gob. Finding the sage's mansion comes first.<end>"
KO: "두목, 이런 데서 싸워 봐야 행복해질 수 없다 고브. 현자님의 저택을 찾는 게 먼저다 고브.<end>"

## 2410020
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec241-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よし、いくゴブ！<line-break>ゴブゴブ団の幸せに向かって<line-break>全速前進ゴブ！<end>"
EN: "Let's go, gob! Full speed ahead toward the Gob-Gob Gang's happiness!<end>"
KO: "좋아, 간다 고브! 고브고브단의 행복을 향해 전속 전진 고브!<end>"

## 2410021
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec241-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あっ、見て。逃げるわよ！<line-break>追ってみましょ！<end>"
EN: "Oh, look! They're getting away! Let's follow them!<end>"
KO: "아, 봐. 도망간다! 쫓아가 보자!<end>"

## 2420001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec242-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おや、お客様ですか。<line-break>珍しいですね。<end>"
EN: "Oh, a visitor? How unusual.<end>"
KO: "어라, 손님인가요. 드문 일이네요.<end>"

## 2420002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec242-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なるほど。人間の言葉を<line-break>しゃべるゴブリンですか…。<line-break>　<line-break>彼らを追って、この屋敷に<line-break>たどり着いたのですね？<end>"
EN: "I see. Goblins who speak the human tongue... And you followed them here to this manor?<end>"
KO: "그렇군요. 인간 말을 하는 고블린입니까…. 그들을 쫓다가 이 저택까지 오신 거군요?<end>"

## 2420003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec242-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私よりもそのゴブリンたちに<line-break>詳しい者がいますよ。<line-break>　<line-break>まあ、ここで立ち話もなんですから。<line-break>お入りください。<line-break>お茶でもいれましょう。<end>"
EN: "There is someone here who knows more about those Goblins than I do. But this is hardly a conversation to have on the doorstep. Please, come in. I'll make some tea.<end>"
KO: "저보다 그 고블린들을 더 잘 아는 사람이 있습니다. 뭐, 여기서 서서 이야기하기도 그러니 들어오세요. 차라도 내오죠.<end>"

## 2420004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec242-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なるほど。<value:$28>の話は<line-break>よくわかりました。<end>"
EN: "I understand your story now, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "그렇군요. <value:$28>의 이야기는 잘 알겠습니다.<end>"

## 2420005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec242-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なんだ、コイツは？<line-break>怪しい匂いがプンプンするぜ。<line-break>　<line-break>たとえるなら、ガーゴイルのよだれと<line-break>リッチの死臭が<line-break>混じり合ったような感じかぁ？<end>"
EN: "What the hell is this? This one reeks of trouble. Like Gargoyle drool mixed with a Lich's stench of death, maybe?<end>"
KO: "뭐야, 이 녀석은? 수상한 냄새가 풀풀 나는데. 비유하자면 가고일의 침과 리치의 시취가 뒤섞인 느낌인가?<end>"

## 2420006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec242-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お客様を前にして<line-break>意味不明なたとえは<line-break>やめてもらえますか、ネモ。<end>"
EN: "Nemo, would you please refrain from making incomprehensible comparisons in front of our guest?<end>"
KO: "손님 앞에서 뜻도 모를 비유는 그만해 주시겠습니까, 네모.<end>"

## 2420007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec242-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "すみませんね。<line-break>あまり気になさらないでください。<end>"
EN: "My apologies. Please don't pay him any mind.<end>"
KO: "미안하군요. 너무 신경 쓰지 마세요.<end>"

## 2420008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec242-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まあ、そのゴブリンたちはともかく、<line-break><value:$28>の話は<line-break>なかなか興味深いですね。<line-break>魔道の塔で目覚め、それ以前の<line-break>記憶がないというのは…。<end>"
EN: "Those Goblins aside, your story is quite intriguing, <value:$28>. You awoke in the Arcane Tower with no memory of anything that came before...<end>"
KO: "뭐, 그 고블린들은 그렇다 치고 <value:$28>의 이야기는 꽤 흥미롭군요. 마도의 탑에서 깨어났고 그 이전의 기억이 없다니…<end>"

## 2420009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec242-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "マジで当たりだ。こいつはヤバイ。<line-break>禁呪よりも破壊的な何かを<line-break>もたらしそうな…。<end>"
EN: "I knew it. This one's dangerous. Looks like this one could bring about something even more destructive than a Forbidden Spell...<end>"
KO: "진짜 정답이야. 이건 위험해. 금주보다 더 파괴적인 무언가를 불러올 것 같은…<end>"

## 2420010
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec242-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あなたは聖杯の件があったのに<line-break>まだ懲りていないようですね。<line-break>　<line-break>じつは人語を解するゴブリンたちも<line-break>もとを正せば、<line-break>このネモの仕業なのですよ。<end>"
EN: "It seems the affair with the Grail taught you nothing. In fact, those Goblins who understand human speech are Nemo's doing as well.<end>"
KO: "성배 건이 있었는데도 아직 정신을 못 차린 모양이군요. 사실 인간의 말을 하는 고블린들도 원인을 따지면 이 네모의 짓입니다.<end>"

## 2420011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec242-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフン。今でこそこんな格好だが、<line-break>俺様は、破壊神の円卓騎士の<line-break>ひとりにして最強の魔人、<line-break>その名も高きネモ様よ！<end>"
EN: "Hmph! I may look like this now, but I am one of the Knights of the Round Table serving the God of Destruction, the mightiest of all demons―the great and renowned Lord Nemo!<end>"
KO: "흥. 지금은 이런 꼴이지만, 이 몸은 파괴신의 원탁기사 중 하나이자 최강의 마인, 이름 높은 네모 님이다!<end>"

## 2420012
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec242-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なぜかネモはこんな姿が<line-break>気に入らないらしく、<line-break>　<line-break>もとの姿に戻るために、<line-break>あのゴブリンたちを操って<line-break>聖杯を盗ませたのです。<end>"
EN: "For some reason, Nemo seems displeased with his present form. To regain his original body, he took control of those Goblins and had them steal the Grail.<end>"
KO: "이상하게도 네모는 이 모습이 마음에 들지 않는 모양이라 원래 모습으로 돌아가기 위해 그 고블린들을 조종해 성배를 훔치게 했습니다.<end>"

## 2420013
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec242-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それで途中までは順調だったんだが<line-break>あのゴブリンども、<line-break>いらねえ知恵つけやがって。<line-break>こんな身体でなかったら<line-break>八つ裂きにしているところだぜ。<end>"
EN: "Everything was going smoothly at first, but then those damned Goblins got too clever for their own good. If I weren't stuck in this body, I'd tear them limb from limb.<end>"
KO: "그래서 중간까진 순조로웠는데 그 고블린 놈들이 쓸데없는 꾀를 익혀 버렸어. 이런 몸만 아니었으면 갈기갈기 찢어 놨을 텐데.<end>"

## 2420014
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec242-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "くすっ。<line-break>彼らが聖杯を持って逃げたんですよ。<line-break>首謀者よりも賢い子たちですね。<end><value:$28>は聖杯が<line-break>どういうものかご存じですか？<end>"
EN: "Heh heh. They took the Grail and ran off with it. Cleverer little fellows than the mastermind behind the scheme, wouldn't you say?<end>Do you know what the Grail is, <value:$28>?<end>"
KO: "후후. 그들이 성배를 들고 도망쳤어요. 주모자보다 똑똑한 아이들이군요.<end><value:$28>은 성배가 어떤 물건인지 알고 계신가요?<end>"

## 2420015
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec242-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "この世に１２あるという闇の神器。<line-break>そのひとつが禁断の聖杯なのです。<line-break>　<line-break>その名の通り、闇の神器は<line-break>闇に落ちた破壊神、ウルグの復活に<line-break>用いられる魔道器と伝えられています。<line-break>だからこそ闇の神器には、<line-break>途方もない力が秘められています。<end>"
EN: "There are said to be twelve Dark Artifacts in this world. The Forbidden Grail is one of them. As the name suggests, legend holds that the Dark Artifacts are used to resurrect Ulugh, the God of Destruction who fell into darkness. That is why each Dark Artifact contains unfathomable power.<end>"
KO: "이 세상에 열두 개가 있다는 어둠의 신기. 그중 하나가 금단의 성배입니다. 이름 그대로 어둠의 신기는 어둠에 떨어진 파괴신 울그의 부활에 쓰이는 마도기라고 전해집니다. 그렇기에 어둠의 신기에는 상상을 초월하는 힘이 숨겨져 있습니다.<end>"

## 2420016
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec242-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なぜ私が、あなたにこのような<line-break>話をするのか不思議そうですね。<end>"
EN: "You seem to be wondering why I would tell you all this.<end>"
KO: "왜 제가 당신에게 이런 이야기를 하는지 이상한 모양이군요.<end>"

## 2420017
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec242-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "自分が何者なのかも知らない<line-break><value:$28>にこういうことを<line-break>言うのもなんですが…。<line-break>あなたの中に眠っている<line-break>無限のソウルと、闇の神器は<line-break>無関係ではない気がするのですよ。<end>"
EN: "It may be strange to say this when even you don't know who you are, <value:$28>, but I have a feeling the Infinite Soul sleeping within you is not unrelated to the Dark Artifacts.<end>"
KO: "자기가 누구인지도 모르는 <value:$28>에게 이런 말을 하는 것도 그렇지만…. 당신 안에 잠든 무한의 소울과 어둠의 신기는 무관하지 않은 것 같아요.<end>"

## 2420018
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec242-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "申し遅れました。<line-break>私はオルファウスと言います。<line-break>この猫屋敷の主です。<line-break>あなたがこの館を訪れたのも<line-break>何らかの意味があるのでしょう。<line-break>　<line-break>偶然でここにたどり着くことは<line-break>決してありませんからね。<end>　<line-break>だからこそ、あなたに<line-break>闇の神器の話をしたのです。<end>"
EN: "Forgive me for not introducing myself sooner. My name is Orphaus, and I am the master of the Cat Manor. There must be some meaning in your coming here. No one ever finds this manor by mere chance.<end>That is precisely why I told you about the Dark Artifacts.<end>"
KO: "인사가 늦었습니다. 저는 오르파우스라고 합니다. 이 고양이 저택의 주인이지요. 당신이 이 저택을 찾은 것도 무언가 의미가 있겠지요. 우연히 이곳에 도달하는 일은 결코 없으니까요.<end> 그렇기에 당신에게 어둠의 신기에 관한 이야기를 한 것입니다.<end>"

## 2420019
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec242-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "すでに闇の神器をめぐって<line-break>さまざまな人の思惑が<line-break>絡みあっています。<line-break>人々の思惑は時に事件を起こし、<line-break>時に悲劇を生むかもしれません。<line-break>　<line-break>それにあなたが巻き込まれることも<line-break>あるでしょう。<line-break>　<line-break>ですが、彼らの意志や理念に<line-break>ふれることで<value:$28>が<line-break>気づくこともあるはずです。<line-break>人々と語らい、その思いに触れる中で<line-break>あなたが求めているものも<line-break>おのずと見えてくると思いますよ。<end>"
EN: "Many people's designs are already entwined around the Dark Artifacts. Those designs may lead to incidents, or even give rise to tragedy. You may find yourself caught up in them. But by encountering the convictions and ideals of those involved, there are things <value:$28> may come to understand. As you speak with people and come to know their thoughts and feelings, I believe what you seek will gradually become clear.<end>"
KO: "이미 어둠의 신기를 둘러싸고 여러 사람의 의도가 뒤얽혀 있습니다. 사람들의 의도는 때로 사건을 일으키고, 때로 비극을 낳을지도 모릅니다. 당신도 그 일에 휘말릴 수 있겠지요. 하지만 그들의 의지와 이념에 접하면서 <value:$28>이 깨닫는 것도 있을 겁니다. 사람들과 이야기를 나누고 그 마음을 접하다 보면 당신이 찾는 것도 자연스레 보이기 시작할 거예요.<end>"

## 2420020
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec242-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "えっ…！？<line-break>いったい、どうなってるの？<end>"
EN: "What...!? What's going on?<end>"
KO: "어…!? 대체 어떻게 된 거야?<end>"

## 2420021
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec242-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "逆に、別れたい仲間を、<line-break>望む所に送り出してやることも<line-break>できます。<end>"
EN: "You can also send companions you wish to part with wherever they would like to go.<end>"
KO: "반대로 헤어지고 싶은 동료를 원하는 곳으로 보내 줄 수도 있습니다.<end>"

## 2420022
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec242-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ど、どうなってるの、これ！？<end>"
EN: "W-what is going on here!?<end>"
KO: "어, 어떻게 된 거야, 이거!?<end>"

## 2420023
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec242-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ありがとうございます。<end>"
EN: "Thank you.<end>"
KO: "감사합니다.<end>"

## 2420024
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec242-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "でも、聖杯を追うにしたって<line-break>あのゴブリンたちの行方について<line-break>何か手がかりがないと…。<end>"
EN: "But there's no going after the Grail without some clue as to where those Goblins went...<end>"
KO: "하지만 성배를 쫓는다 해도 그 고블린들의 행방에 대해 뭔가 단서가 없으면….<end>"

## 2420025
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec242-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ネモの話だと、<line-break>彼らはエンシャントに<line-break>向かったそうです。<end>"
EN: "According to Nemo, they were headed for Ancient.<end>"
KO: "네모의 말로는 그들이 엔샨트로 향했다고 합니다.<end>"

## 2420026
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec242-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうね。<line-break>人間の言葉をしゃべるゴブリンなんて<line-break>どうしたって人目につくでしょうし。<line-break>酒場なんかで情報を集めれば、<line-break>居場所もつかめるかも。<end>"
EN: "Right. Goblins who speak the human tongue are bound to attract attention. If we ask around at taverns and the like, we may be able to track them down.<end>"
KO: "그렇네. 인간 말을 하는 고블린이라면 아무래도 눈에 띄겠지. 술집 같은 데서 정보를 모으면 행방도 알아낼 수 있을지 몰라.<end>"

## 2420027
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec242-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "聖杯はここに持ってきてくれれば<line-break>私が預かります。<end>"
EN: "If you bring the Grail here, I will keep it safe.<end>"
KO: "성배는 여기로 가져오시면 제가 맡겠습니다.<end>"

## 2420028
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec242-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "PSP用新スタートイベント05用終端<end>"
EN: ""
KO: "PSP용 신규 시작 이벤트 05용 끝<end>"

## 2430000
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec243-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "PSP用新スタートイベント06用開始<end>"
EN: ""
KO: "PSP용 신규 시작 이벤트 06용 시작<end>"

## 2430001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec243-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "待ちたまえ。<line-break><value:$28>ではないか。<end>"
EN: "Wait. Is that you, <value:$28>?<end>"
KO: "잠깐. <value:$28> 아닌가.<end>"

## 2430002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec243-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どうだ、あれから記憶は戻ったか？<end>"
EN: "Well? Have any of your memories returned since then?<end>"
KO: "어때, 그 뒤로 기억은 돌아왔나?<end>"

## 2430003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec243-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうか。まだ思い出せぬか。<line-break>残念だな。<line-break>　<line-break>自分が何者なのか<line-break>わからぬというのは、<line-break>さぞやもどかしいだろう。<line-break>だが、焦らぬことだ。<line-break>浅慮は時として、破滅につながる。<end>"
EN: "So. You still cannot remember. A pity. It must be frustrating not to know who you are. But do not rush. A rash decision can sometimes lead to ruin.<end>"
KO: "그런가. 아직 기억나지 않는가. 유감이군. 자신이 누구인지 모른다는 건 몹시 답답하겠지. 하지만 조급해하지 마라. 경솔한 판단은 때로 파멸로 이어진다.<end>"

## 2430004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec243-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フッ、<value:$28>のことを<line-break>心配しているわけではない。<line-break>ただ、理不尽さを覚えたまでだ。<line-break>無限のソウルの持ち主が<line-break>己を生かすことさえ知らず、<line-break>いたずらに力を浪費していることにな。<end>"
EN: "Hmph. Do not mistake this for concern, <value:$28>. I merely find it absurd that the bearer of the Infinite Soul does not even know how to fulfill that potential, and simply squanders the power within.<end>"
KO: "후, <value:$28>를 걱정하는 게 아니다. 그저 부조리함을 느꼈을 뿐. 무한의 소울을 지닌 자가 자기 자신을 살리는 법조차 모르고 헛되이 힘을 낭비하고 있으니.<end>"

## 2430005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec243-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今の君では、無限のソウルも<line-break>宝の持ち腐れだ。<line-break>　<line-break>無限の可能性が、<line-break>ただの可能性のままで終わってしまう。<line-break>　<line-break>君には、その身に秘めている力を、<line-break>ぜひとも磨いてもらいたいのだ。<line-break>　<line-break>君個人のためでなく、この世界にとって<line-break>大いなる貢献を成し遂げるために。<end>"
EN: "As you are now, even the Infinite Soul is wasted on you. All that infinite potential will remain mere potential. I want you to hone the power hidden within you―not for your own sake, but to make a great contribution to this world.<end>"
KO: "지금의 너로선 무한의 소울도 보물 썩히기일 뿐이다. 무한한 가능성이 그저 가능성으로 끝나 버리겠지. 네 안에 잠든 힘을 반드시 갈고닦아 주었으면 한다. 너 개인을 위해서가 아니라 이 세계에 위대한 공헌을 이루기 위해서.<end>"

## 2430006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec243-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "呼び止めてすまなかったな。<line-break>縁があったら、また会おう。<line-break><value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "Forgive me for detaining you. If fate allows, we shall meet again, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "불러 세워서 미안했다. 인연이 있다면 또 만나자. <value:$28>.<end>"

## 2430007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec243-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "待ちたまえ。<line-break><value:$28>ではないか。<end>"
EN: "Wait. Is that you, <value:$28>?<end>"
KO: "잠깐. <value:$28> 아닌가.<end>"

## 2430008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec243-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どうだ、あれから記憶は戻ったか？<end>"
EN: "Well? Have any of your memories returned since then?<end>"
KO: "어때, 그 뒤로 기억은 돌아왔나?<end>"

## 2430009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec243-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 2430011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec243-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今の君では、無限のソウルも<line-break>宝の持ち腐れだ。<line-break>　<line-break>無限の可能性が、<line-break>ただの可能性のままで終わってしまう。<line-break>　<line-break>君には、その身に秘めている力を、<line-break>ぜひとも磨いてもらいたいのだ。<line-break>　<line-break>君個人のためでなく、この世界にとって<line-break>大いなる貢献を成し遂げるために。<end>"

## 2430012
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec243-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふむ。<line-break>魔道の塔に興味があるのか…。<end>"
EN: "Hmm. So the Arcane Tower interests you.<end>"
KO: "흠. 마도탑에 관심이 있나….<end>"

## 2430013
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec243-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "行きたい気持ちはわかるが、<line-break>賛成しかねるな。<line-break>　<line-break>今の君の力ではむざむざと<line-break>魔物に殺されにいくようなものだ。<line-break>　<line-break>それでもあえて赴くというのなら、<line-break>私に止める権利はない。<line-break>　<line-break>だが、せめてもっと腕を上げてから<line-break>挑んでも遅くはあるまい、<line-break>…とだけ忠告しておこう。<end>"
EN: "I understand why you want to go, but I cannot approve. At your present strength, you would only be going there to be killed by monsters. If you choose to go regardless, I have no right to stop you. Still, there would be no harm in waiting until you have improved your skills before taking on the Tower. Let me at least leave you with that warning.<end>"
KO: "가고 싶은 마음은 알겠지만 찬성할 수는 없군. 지금 네 힘으로는 괜히 마물에게 죽으러 가는 거나 다름없다. 그래도 굳이 가겠다면 내가 막을 권리는 없지. 다만 적어도 실력을 더 키운 뒤 도전해도 늦지 않을 거라고 충고해 두마.<end>"

## 2440000
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec244-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "PSP用新スタートイベント07用開始<end>"
EN: ""
KO: "PSP용 신규 시작 이벤트 07용 시작<end>"

## 2440001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec244-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おや、<value:$28>ですか。<line-break>いらっしゃい。<line-break>　<line-break>どうです。<line-break>記憶は戻りましたか？<end>"
EN: "Ah, <value:$28>. Welcome. Well? Has your memory returned?<end>"
KO: "어라, <value:$28>인가요. 어서 와요. 어때요? 기억은 돌아왔나요?<end>"

## 2440002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec244-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうですか…。<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break><value:$28>がいた<line-break>魔道の塔に行けば、<line-break>何か思い出すかもしれませんね。<line-break>そこで目覚めたという記憶が<line-break>あるのでしょう？<end>"
EN: "I see... Perhaps going back to the Arcane Tower will help you remember something, <value:$28>. You remember waking up there, don't you?<end>"
KO: "그렇군요…. <value:$28>가 있었던 마도의 탑에 가면 뭔가 떠오를지도 모르겠네요. 거기서 깨어났다는 기억이 있는 거죠?<end>"

## 2440003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec244-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ならば、魔道の塔に<line-break>手がかりが残っている可能性も<line-break>充分あると思いますよ。<line-break>少々の危険を冒してでも<line-break>行ってみる価値はあるはずです。<end>"
EN: "Then there is every chance that some clue remains in the Arcane Tower. It should be worth going, even if it means braving a little danger.<end>"
KO: "그렇다면 마도의 탑에 단서가 남아 있을 가능성도 충분합니다. 약간 위험을 감수하더라도 가 볼 가치는 있을 겁니다.<end>"

## 2440004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec244-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "でも、<value:$28>。<line-break>これだけは覚えておいてください。<line-break>　<line-break>大事なのは過去を知ることではなく、<line-break>未来において何を為し得るかを<line-break>見つけることですよ。<line-break>過去にとらわれるあまり<line-break>未来を見失ってしまっては<line-break>本末転倒です。<line-break>人は過去にではなく、<line-break>未来に向かってしか<line-break>生きられないのですから。<end>"
EN: "But <value:$28>, remember this: what matters is not learning about the past, but discovering what you can accomplish in the future. If you become so bound by the past that you lose sight of the future, you defeat the whole purpose. We can only live toward the future, never the past.<end>"
KO: "하지만 <value:$28>. 이것만은 기억해 주세요. 중요한 것은 과거를 아는 게 아니라 미래에 무엇을 이룰 수 있는지를 찾는 것입니다. 과거에 사로잡힌 나머지 미래를 잃어버리면 본말전도예요. 사람은 과거가 아니라 미래를 향해서만 살아갈 수 있으니까요.<end>"

## 2450000
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec245-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "PSP用新スタートイベント08用開始<end>"
EN: ""
KO: "PSP용 신규 시작 이벤트 08용 시작<end>"

## 2450001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec245-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "妖術宰相の隠れ家にようこそ…。<line-break>何も出せんが<line-break>まあ、ゆっくりしていくといい。<end>"
EN: "Welcome to the Sorcerous Chancellor's hideaway. I have nothing to offer you, but do make yourself at home.<end>"
KO: "요술 재상의 은신처에 온 걸 환영하지…. 내놓을 건 없지만, 뭐 느긋하게 쉬다 가게.<end>"

## 2450002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec245-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ほう。自分が何者かを知りたいとな？<line-break>フォッフォッフォッ…。<line-break>それこそ徒労というものじゃ。<line-break>自分の出自や記憶を知ったところで、<line-break>人は誰しも運命にはあらがえぬ。<line-break>受け入れ、流されることしかできぬ。<line-break>それがたとえ、<line-break>作られた運命であったとしてもな。<end>"
EN: "Oh? You wish to know who you are? Ho ho ho... A futile pursuit. Finding out about your origins or your memories will do you no good. No one can defy fate. All anyone can do is accept it and be swept along, even if that fate was fashioned.<end>"
KO: "호오. 자신이 누구인지 알고 싶다고? 호호호…. 그야말로 헛수고지. 자신의 출신이나 기억을 알아낸다 해도 누구도 운명에는 거스를 수 없다. 받아들이고 흘러갈 수밖에 없지. 설령 그것이 만들어진 운명이라 해도 말이다.<end>"

## 2450003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec245-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フォッフォッフォッ…。<line-break>どういう意味か…じゃと？<line-break>　<line-break>無知たることのなんと幸せなことよ…<line-break>お前を見ておると、<line-break>つい、そう思ってしまうわ。<line-break>その存在を知らぬ者にとっては、<line-break>運命も、人の世の因果も<line-break>あってなきがごとしなのかもしれん。<end>"
EN: "Ho ho ho... You ask what I mean? Ah, what bliss there is in ignorance. Looking at you, I cannot help but think so. To anyone unaware of its existence, perhaps fate and the chain of cause and effect in the world of men might as well not exist.<end>"
KO: "호호호…. 무슨 뜻이냐고…? 무지하다는 것이 얼마나 행복한 일인지…. 네 모습을 보고 있으면 문득 그런 생각이 드는구나. 그 존재를 모르는 자에게는 운명도, 인간 세상의 인과도 있으나 마나 한 것일지도 모르지.<end>"

## 2450004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec245-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いずれ時が来れば<line-break>お前にもわかるだろう。<line-break>そう急ぐ必要もあるまい。<end>"
EN: "When the time comes, you will understand. There is no need to hurry.<end>"
KO: "때가 오면 너도 알게 되겠지. 그렇게 서두를 필요도 없을 거다.<end>"

## 2450005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec245-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "PSP用新スタートイベント08用終端<end>"
EN: ""
KO: "PSP용 신규 시작 이벤트 08용 끝<end>"

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

EN: ""
KO: "PSP용 신규 시작 이벤트 11용 시작<end>"

## 2460001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec246-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>か…。<line-break>よく来たな。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>... You have done well to come.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>인가…. 잘 왔다.<end>"

## 2460002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec246-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふっ、自分が何者なのか知りたいのか？<end>"
EN: "Heh. You wish to know who you are?<end>"
KO: "후, 자신이 누구인지 알고 싶나?<end>"

## 2460003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec246-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふむ。まあよかろう。<line-break>ここまでたどり着けたのであれば、<line-break>ことさら隠す必要もあるまい。<line-break>心して聞くがよい。<line-break><value:$28>よ…。<line-break>　<line-break>汝は余によって造られた人間だ。<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>果たして人間と呼んでいいかは<line-break>わからぬがな。<end>"
EN: "Very well. Having made it this far, there is little reason to keep it from you. Steel yourself and listen, <value:$28>. You are a human being of my creation―though whether you can truly be called human is another matter.<end>"
KO: "흠. 뭐 좋다. 여기까지 도달했다면 굳이 숨길 필요도 없겠지. 마음 단단히 먹고 들어라. <value:$28>여…. 그대는 짐이 만든 인간이다. 과연 인간이라 불러도 되는지는 모르겠다만.<end>"

## 2460004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec246-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "かつて余は幽閉される前に<line-break>先人類の魔道技術を使って<line-break>不死の研究を続けていた。<line-break>その結果、偶発的ではあったが<line-break>無限のソウルを宿せる肉体が<line-break>完成した…。<line-break>ただし、余の望んだ不死の能力は<line-break>備わっていなかったがな…。<line-break>　<line-break>それが汝だ。<value:$28>よ。<line-break>汝は、研究の副産物として誕生し、<line-break>余ととともに封印されていたのだ。<line-break>よもや汝がこの時代に目覚めるとは<line-break>余にとっても想定外だったがな…。<end>"
EN: "Before I was imprisoned, I used the magical technology of earlier mankind to pursue my research into immortality. By chance, that research produced a body capable of containing an Infinite Soul. It did not, however, possess the immortality I sought. That body was you, <value:$28>. You were born as a by-product of my research and sealed away with me. Even I did not foresee that you would awaken in this age.<end>"
KO: "예전에 짐은 유폐되기 전 선인류의 마도 기술을 이용해 불사의 연구를 계속하고 있었다. 그 결과 우연이긴 했지만 무한의 소울을 담을 수 있는 육체가 완성되었다…. 다만 짐이 바라던 불사의 능력은 갖추지 못했지만 말이다…. 그것이 바로 너다, <value:$28>. 너는 연구의 부산물로 태어나 짐과 함께 봉인되어 있었다. 설마 네가 이 시대에 깨어날 줄은 짐에게도 예상 밖이었지만 말이다….<end>"

## 2460005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec246-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "クックック。愚かな…。<line-break>教えてほしいと望んだのは汝自身だぞ。<line-break>　<line-break>たかだか出生の秘密を知ったところで、<line-break>動揺して何になる？<line-break>　<line-break>父母から命をもらおうと、<line-break>研究によって誕生しようと<line-break>瑣末な問題だ。取るに足らぬ。<end>"
EN: "Heh heh heh. What foolishness. You were the one who asked to know. Why let a mere secret of your birth disturb you so? Whether life is given by parents or bestowed through research is a trivial distinction, of no consequence whatsoever.<end>"
KO: "큭큭큭. 어리석군…. 알려 달라고 원한 건 그대 자신이다. 고작 출생의 비밀을 알았다고 동요해서 무엇 하겠나? 부모에게 생명을 받았든 연구로 태어났든 사소한 문제다. 하찮은 일이지.<end>"

## 2460006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec246-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ほう。自分が造られた存在で<line-break>あることに激昂したか。<line-break>人間並みの感情はできているようだな。<line-break>だが、封印された余の肉体に<line-break>汝の剣では傷ひとつつけられぬ。<line-break>　<line-break>それに余は汝の敵ではない。<line-break>汝にとって創造者であり、<line-break>唯一の理解者なのだぞ。<end>"
EN: "Oh? Enraged to learn that you were created? It seems you have developed emotions worthy of a human. But your sword cannot leave so much as a scratch upon my sealed body. Besides, I am not your enemy. I am your creator―and the only one who truly understands you.<end>"
KO: "호오. 자신이 만들어진 존재라는 사실에 격분했나. 인간다운 감정은 제대로 갖췄나 보군. 하지만 봉인된 짐의 육체에는 네 검으로 상처 하나 낼 수 없다. 게다가 짐은 네 적이 아니다. 네 창조자이자 유일한 이해자다.<end>"

## 2460007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec246-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフフ。そうだ…。<line-break>おとなしく余の話を聞くがよい。<end>"
EN: "Heh heh. Yes... Be still and hear me out.<end>"
KO: "후후후. 그래…. 얌전히 짐의 이야기를 듣거라.<end>"

## 2460008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec246-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>よ。<line-break>余は汝の力を必要としている。<line-break>　<line-break>余が完全に復活するためには、<line-break>その器となる肉体が必要なのだ。<line-break>　<line-break>特に余の息子、ベルゼーヴァの<line-break>身体であれば申し分ない。<line-break>　<line-break>余は復活し、人類の革新に邪魔な<line-break>竜どもを完全に滅ぼさねばならぬ。<line-break>特に余を封じた竜王は、な。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, I have need of your power. To restore myself completely, I require a body to serve as my vessel. The body of my son Beelzeva would be ideal. Once restored, I must utterly destroy the dragons that obstruct Humanity's Evolution―especially the Dragon King who sealed me away.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>여. 나는 네 힘이 필요하다. 내가 완전히 부활하려면 그 그릇이 될 육체가 필요하지. 특히 내 아들 벨제바의 몸이라면 더할 나위 없다. 나는 부활해 인류의 혁신을 방해하는 용들을 완전히 멸해야 한다. 특히 나를 봉인한 용왕을 말이지.<end>"

## 2460009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec246-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わかるか、<value:$28>よ。<line-break>汝は生みの親である余に<line-break>協力するのだ。<line-break>そうすれば、余は汝に、<line-break>人類の革新という生きる目的と<line-break>価値ある意義を授けよう。<end>"
EN: "Do you understand, <value:$28>? You will aid me, the one who gave you life. In return, I shall grant you a purpose for living, a worthy cause: Humanity's Evolution.<end>"
KO: "알겠느냐, <value:$28>여. 그대는 창조자인 나에게 협력해야 한다. 그러면 나는 인류의 혁신이라는 삶의 목적과 가치 있는 의의를 주겠다.<end>"

## 2460010
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec246-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフフ。まさかベルゼーヴァの言動や<line-break>ネメアごときを信じているのか？<line-break>　<line-break>それこそ夢想というものだ。<line-break>所詮、あのふたりでは<line-break>人類の革新は成し得まい。<end>"
EN: "Heh heh. Surely you do not place your faith in Beelzeva's words and deeds, or in a man like Nemea? That is the true fantasy. Those two could never bring about Humanity's Evolution.<end>"
KO: "후후후. 설마 벨제바의 말과 행동이나 네메아 따위를 믿는 건가? 그야말로 몽상이지. 결국 그 둘로는 인류의 혁신을 이룰 수 없을 것이다.<end>"

## 2460011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec246-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よく考えてみるがよい。<line-break>人間だけの力で本当に<line-break>人類の革新が可能かどうかを…。<line-break>もし可能であれば、とうの昔に<line-break>人類は完成し、すべてを超越した<line-break>至高の存在となっているだろう。<line-break>竜王ごときもこの世におるまい。<line-break>それが現実はどうだ？<line-break>　<line-break>竜王は依然として世界を裏から操り、<line-break>人類の進化を停滞せしめている。<line-break>　<line-break>あくことなき愚かな争いが続く、<line-break>暗黒に満ちた不安定な混沌の世界…<line-break>それこそまさに竜王の望むもの。<end>"
EN: "Consider it carefully. Can humanity truly achieve Humanity's Evolution through its own power? If it could, mankind would have achieved perfection long ago and become the supreme existence, transcending all things. There would be no Dragon King in this world. Yet what is the reality? The Dragon King still manipulates the world from the shadows, keeping Humanity's Evolution at a standstill. A dark and precarious world of chaos, riven by ceaseless, senseless conflict... That is precisely what the Dragon King desires.<end>"
KO: "잘 생각해 보아라. 인간만의 힘으로 정말 인류의 혁신이 가능한지를…. 가능했다면 인류는 진작 완성되어 모든 것을 초월한 지고의 존재가 되었겠지. 용왕 따위도 이 세상에 없었을 것이다. 하지만 현실은 어떤가? 용왕은 여전히 뒤에서 세계를 조종하며 인류의 진화를 정체시키고 있다. 끝없는 어리석은 싸움이 이어지는 어둠과 불안으로 가득한 혼돈의 세계…. 바로 그것이 용왕이 바라는 것이다.<end>"

## 2460012
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec246-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>よ。汝は余とともに<line-break>神々や竜王の支配を断ち切り、<line-break>新しき人類の未来を築くのだ。<line-break>汝のように優れた資質を持った者と、<line-break>余の魔道技術が合わさってこそ、<line-break>人類の革新は果たせよう。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, together we shall break the dominion of the gods and the Dragon King and build a new future for humanity. Only by combining your exceptional gifts with my magical technology can Humanity's Evolution be achieved.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>여. 너는 짐과 함께 신들과 용왕의 지배를 끊고 새로운 인류의 미래를 세우는 것이다. 너처럼 뛰어난 자질을 지닌 자와 짐의 마도기술이 합쳐져야 인류의 혁신을 이룰 수 있으리라.<end>"

## 2460013
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec246-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まだ時間はある。<line-break>じっくりと思案してみることだな。<line-break>　<line-break>余とベルゼーヴァ、<line-break>どちらが人類の革新を…<line-break>そして真の平和をもたらすかを。<line-break>余に力を貸す決心がついたら<line-break>またここに来るがよい。<end>"
EN: "There is still time. Consider the matter at your leisure: which of us, Beelzeva or I, can bring about Humanity's Evolution... and true peace. Once you have resolved to lend me your power, return here.<end>"
KO: "아직 시간은 있다. 충분히 생각해 봐라. 짐과 벨제바, 어느 쪽이 인류의 혁신을… 그리고 진정한 평화를 가져올지. 짐에게 힘을 빌려주기로 결심하면 다시 이곳에 오너라.<end>"

## 2460014
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec246-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まだ時間はある。<line-break>じっくりと思案してみることだな。<line-break>　<line-break>余とベルゼーヴァ…。<line-break>どちらが人類の革新を…。<line-break>そして真の平和をもたらすかを。<line-break>余に力を貸す決心がついたら<line-break>またここに来るがいい。<end>"
EN: "There is still time. Think it over carefully. Beelzeva or I―which of us will bring about Humanity's Evolution... and true peace? Once you have decided to lend me your strength, return here.<end>"
KO: "아직 시간은 있다. 천천히 생각해 보아라. 짐과 벨제바…. 어느 쪽이 인류의 혁신을, 그리고 진정한 평화를 가져올지. 짐에게 힘을 보태기로 결심하면 다시 이곳으로 오너라.<end>"

EN: ""

## 2470001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec247-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>じゃないですか。<end>"
EN: "Why, if it isn't <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28> 아니십니까.<end>"

## 2470002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec247-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どうしました？<line-break>顔色が冴えませんね。<end>"
EN: "What's wrong? You don't look well.<end>"
KO: "무슨 일이죠? 안색이 좋지 않네요.<end>"

## 2470003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec247-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まあ、立ち話もなんですし、<line-break>中に入りませんか？<end>"
EN: "Well, this is hardly the place to talk. Shall we go inside?<end>"
KO: "뭐, 서서 이야기하기도 그렇고 안으로 들어가지 않겠습니까?<end>"

## 2470004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec247-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なるほど、知ってしまったのですね、<line-break>あなたの過去を…<line-break>あなたが造られた人間であることを…。<line-break>それで動揺していたわけですか。<end>"
EN: "I see. So you've learned about your past... that you're an artificially created human being. Is that why you've been so shaken?<end>"
KO: "그렇군요, 알아 버렸군요. 당신의 과거를… 당신이 만들어진 인간이라는 걸…. 그래서 동요했던 거군요.<end>"

## 2470005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec247-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "でも、これで<line-break>失われた過去をあれこれ思うことも<line-break>なくなったわけです。<line-break>要は考え方次第だと思いますよ、<line-break><value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "But at least you no longer have to wonder about the past you lost. It all depends on how you choose to look at it, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "그래도 이걸로 잃어버린 과거를 이것저것 생각할 일은 없어졌잖아요. 결국 생각하기 나름이라고 봐요, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 2470006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec247-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "過去は変えることができません。<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>ですが、これから先の未来は<line-break>あなた自身の手にゆだねられています。<line-break>誰ひとり束縛も強制もできません。<line-break>大事なのは何に生まれたかではなく、<line-break>どう生きるのかではないですか？<end>"
EN: "The past cannot be changed. But the future is in your own hands. No one can bind you or force you to do anything. What matters is not what you were born as, but how you choose to live. Wouldn't you agree?<end>"
KO: "과거는 바꿀 수 없습니다. 하지만 앞으로의 미래는 당신 자신의 손에 맡겨져 있습니다. 누구도 속박하거나 강요할 수 없습니다. 중요한 건 무엇으로 태어났느냐가 아니라 어떻게 살아가느냐가 아닐까요?<end>"

## 2470007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec247-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "通常の人間ではないとわかったところで<line-break>あなたの存在が変わるわけでも、<line-break>消えるわけでもありません。<end>"
EN: "Learning that you are not an ordinary human being does not change your existence, nor does it erase it.<end>"
KO: "당신이 평범한 인간이 아니라는 걸 알게 되었다고 해서 당신의 존재가 바뀌거나 사라지는 건 아닙니다.<end>"

## 2470008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec247-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "過去を持たないということは<line-break>いわば白紙ということ。<line-break>　<line-break>白紙であればこそ、<line-break>あなたの望む、思い通りの絵が<line-break>描けるのではないでしょうか。<end>"
EN: "To have no past is, in a sense, to have a blank canvas. And because it is blank, can you not paint whatever picture you wish upon it?<end>"
KO: "과거가 없다는 것은 말하자면 백지라는 뜻입니다. 백지이기에야말로 당신이 원하는 대로 그림을 그릴 수 있지 않을까요.<end>"

## 2470009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec247-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まあ、これはあくまで<line-break>私個人の見解です。<line-break>他の人にも訊いてみるといいでしょう。<line-break>たとえば、ベルゼーヴァさんは<line-break>あなたに強い関心がおありのようです。<line-break>よい助言をしてくれると思いますよ。<end>"
EN: "But that is only my own view. You may want to ask others as well. Beelzeva, for instance, seems to have taken a keen interest in you. I believe he would offer you sound advice.<end>"
KO: "뭐, 이건 어디까지나 제 개인적인 견해입니다. 다른 사람에게도 물어보세요. 예를 들면 벨제바 씨는 당신에게 강한 관심이 있는 듯하니 좋은 조언을 해 줄 겁니다.<end>"

## 2470010
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec247-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>じゃないですか。<end>"
EN: "Why, if it isn't <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28> 아니십니까.<end>"

## 2470019
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec247-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どうしました、ケリュネイア？<line-break>何か言いたそうですが…。<end>"
EN: "What is it, Ceryneia? You look as though you have something to say.<end>"
KO: "무슨 일이죠, 케류네이아? 뭔가 하고 싶은 말이 있는 것 같은데….<end>"

## 2470020
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec247-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "別に…。<end>"
EN: "It's nothing...<end>"
KO: "별로….<end>"

## 2470021
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec247-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "本当にそうですか？<end>"
EN: "Are you sure?<end>"
KO: "정말 그런가요?<end>"

## 2470023
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec247-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "くすっ、そうですか。<line-break>だったら、よいのですけどね。<end>"
EN: "Heh. I see. Well, that's all right, then.<end>"
KO: "후후, 그렇군요. 그렇다면 다행이네요.<end>"

source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec248-part99.toml

## 2480001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec248-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>…。<line-break>父さんはああ言っていたけど<line-break>私の意見はまったく正反対よ。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>... Father may have said all that, but I couldn't disagree more.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>…. 아버지는 그렇게 말했지만 내 생각은 완전히 반대야.<end>"

## 2480002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec248-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私はハーフエルフとして<line-break>生きてきたからわかるの。<line-break>　<line-break>どう生きていくのかが大事だ、<line-break>なんて言うのは簡単よ…。<line-break><line-break>だけど<line-break>実際にそうやって生きていくのが<line-break>どれほどつらいことか想像できる？<end>"
EN: "I know because I've lived as a Half-Elf. It's easy to say that what matters is how you choose to live... But can you imagine how hard it is to actually live that way?<end>"
KO: "나는 하프엘프로 살아왔으니 알아. 어떻게 살아갈지가 중요하다고 말하는 건 쉽지…. 하지만 실제로 그렇게 살아가는 게 얼마나 힘든지 상상할 수 있어?<end>"

## 2480003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec248-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私は人間からもエルフからも<line-break>疎まれて生きてきたわ。<line-break><line-break>私という人格や個性とは関係なく<line-break>私がハーフエルフであるという<line-break>ただそれだけの事実で迫害してくるの。<end>"
EN: "I've lived my whole life shunned by humans and Elves alike. They persecute me simply because I'm a Half-Elf, without the slightest regard for who I am as a person.<end>"
KO: "나는 인간에게도 엘프에게도 미움받으며 살아왔어. 나라는 인격이나 개성과 상관없이 내가 하프엘프라는 그 사실 하나만으로 박해해.<end>"

## 2480004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec248-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いくら私自身が変わろうと、<line-break>他人は私の意志や行動を<line-break>理解しようとはしない。<line-break>ハーフエルフという外見だけが、<line-break>私への扱いを決定しているの。<line-break>　<line-break>そして、それは私が死ぬまで<line-break>つきまとってくる現実…。<end>"
EN: "No matter how much I change, no one tries to understand my intentions or actions. My Half-Elf appearance alone determines how they treat me. And that is a reality that will follow me until the day I die...<end>"
KO: "아무리 내가 변해도 다른 사람들은 내 의지나 행동을 이해하려 하지 않아. 하프엘프라는 겉모습만으로 나를 대하는 태도를 결정해. 그리고 그건 내가 죽을 때까지 따라붙는 현실이야….<end>"

## 2480005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec248-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それでも、私が生きてこられたのは<line-break>孤独じゃなかったからよ。<line-break>　<line-break>父さんがいる。兄さんがいる。<line-break>そして、決して多くはないけれど<line-break>ハーフエルフの仲間だっているわ。<end>"
EN: "Even so, I've managed to go on living because I wasn't alone. I have my father. I have my brother. And though there aren't many of them, I have Half-Elf companions too.<end>"
KO: "그래도 내가 살아올 수 있었던 건 외롭지 않았기 때문이야. 아버지가 있고, 오빠가 있고, 많지는 않지만 하프엘프 동료도 있어.<end>"

## 2480006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec248-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "でも、あなたは私とは違う。<line-break>たったひとりぼっちの存在…。<line-break>　<line-break>異分子として奇異の目で見られ、<line-break>疎まれ、遠ざけられるでしょうね。<line-break>　<line-break>世界はたぶんあなたを受け入れられない。<line-break>あなたという異能者を認められるほど、<line-break>まだ世のなかは成熟していないから…。<line-break>そんな世界であなたは生きていけるの？<end>"
EN: "But you're different from me. You are completely alone... People will see you as something alien, stare at you, shun you, keep their distance. The world probably won't accept you. It isn't mature enough yet to acknowledge someone with abilities like yours... Can you live in a world like that?<end>"
KO: "하지만 당신은 나와 달라. 세상에 단 하나뿐인 존재…. 이질적인 존재로 이상한 시선을 받고 미움을 사고 멀리 배척당하겠지. 세계는 아마 당신을 받아들이지 못할 거야. 당신 같은 이능자를 인정할 만큼 아직 세상은 성숙하지 않았으니까…. 그런 세계에서 당신은 살아갈 수 있어?<end>"

## 2480007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec248-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そう。あなたにはそれだけの<line-break>覚悟があるのね。<line-break>だったらもう何も言う必要はないわ。<end>"
EN: "I understand. You're prepared to face all that. Then there's nothing more I need to say.<end>"
KO: "그래. 너에겐 그만한 각오가 있구나. 그렇다면 더는 아무 말도 할 필요 없겠어.<end>"

## 2480008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec248-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それにあなたには<line-break>無限のソウルがある…。<line-break>　<line-break>もしかするとその可能性に<line-break>希望を抱いた多くの人たちが集い、<line-break>孤独を埋めてくれるのかもしれない。<end>"
EN: "Besides, you have the Infinite Soul... Perhaps many people will find hope in its potential and gather around you, easing your loneliness.<end>"
KO: "게다가 당신에게는 무한의 소울이 있어요…. 그 가능성에 희망을 품은 많은 사람들이 모여 외로움을 채워 줄지도 모르죠.<end>"

## 2480009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec248-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "PSP用新スタートイベント13用終端<end>"
EN: ""
KO: "PSP용 신규 시작 이벤트 13용 끝<end>"

## 2490001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec249-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…無限のソウル？<line-break>そうか。<line-break>お前が<value:$28>か。<end>"
EN: "…The Infinite Soul? So you're <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "…무한의 소울? 그렇군. 네가 <value:$28>인가.<end>"

## 2490002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec249-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なるほど、よい瞳だ。<line-break>お前のことはベルゼーヴァから<line-break>聞いている。<end>"
EN: "You have fine eyes. Beelzeva has told me about you.<end>"
KO: "과연, 좋은 눈빛이군. 네 이야기는 벨제바에게 들었다.<end>"

## 2490003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec249-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "表情に覇気がないな。<line-break>いや、いきなりすまない。<line-break>ただ、迷いが感じられてな。<end>"
EN: "You look dispirited. Forgive me. That was rather sudden. It's just that I sense some hesitation in you.<end>"
KO: "표정에 패기가 없군. 아니, 갑자기 미안하다. 그저 망설임이 느껴져서 말이야.<end>"

## 2490004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec249-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "自分が背負っている運命？<line-break>ふむ。それについて考えたことが<line-break>あるかと問うのか…。<end>"
EN: "The fate I bear? Hmm. You ask whether I have ever given it any thought...<end>"
KO: "내가 짊어진 운명? 흠. 그것에 대해 생각해 본 적이 있느냐는 질문인가…<end>"

## 2490005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec249-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "かつて私は友にこう言ったことがある。<line-break>私が戦う理由はただひとつ。<line-break>運命にあらがうためだと。<end>"
EN: "I once told a friend that I fight for one reason alone: to defy fate.<end>"
KO: "예전에 나는 친구에게 이렇게 말한 적이 있다. 내가 싸우는 이유는 단 하나, 운명에 맞서기 위해서라고.<end>"

## 2490006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec249-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "運命は<value:$28>の未来を<line-break>消そうとしているのではない。<line-break>　<line-break>お前の内に眠る可能性が目覚め、<line-break>運命に打ち勝つことを待っているのだ。<end>"
EN: "Fate is not trying to erase your future, <value:$28>. It is waiting for the potential dormant within you to awaken and overcome it.<end>"
KO: "운명은 <value:$28>의 미래를 지우려는 게 아니다. 네 안에 잠든 가능성이 깨어나 운명을 이기기를 기다리고 있는 것이다.<end>"

## 2490007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec249-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お前が抱えている問題を私は知らない。<line-break>だが、これだけは言える。<line-break>　<line-break>人は、<line-break>それぞれの運命を背負って生まれる。<line-break>そして、それを選ぶことはできない。<line-break>しかし、運命に流されることを拒否し、<line-break>どのように立ち向かうかを<line-break>決めることはできる。<line-break>運命に立ち向かった者すべてが<line-break>それを乗り越えられたわけでは<line-break>ないだろう。<line-break>だが、運命を乗り越えた者は、<line-break>立ち向かった者の中にしかいないのだ。<end>"
EN: "I do not know what troubles you, but I can tell you this. We are each born bearing our own fate, and we cannot choose it. But we can refuse to be swept along by it and decide how we will face it. Not everyone who has stood against fate has overcome it. But no one has ever overcome fate without standing against it.<end>"
KO: "네가 안고 있는 문제가 무엇인지는 모른다. 하지만 이것만은 말할 수 있다. 사람은 저마다의 운명을 짊어지고 태어난다. 그리고 그 운명을 고를 수는 없다. 하지만 운명에 휩쓸리기를 거부하고 어떻게 맞설지는 정할 수 있다. 운명에 맞선 모든 사람이 그것을 이겨 낸 것은 아닐 것이다. 하지만 운명을 넘어선 사람은 맞선 사람들 가운데서만 나왔다.<end>"

## 2490008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec249-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "PSP用新スタートイベント14用終端<end>"
EN: ""
KO: "PSP용 신규 시작 이벤트 14용 끝<end>"

source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec250-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "PSP用新スタートイベント15用開始<end>"
EN: ""
KO: "PSP용 신규 시작 이벤트 15용 시작<end>"

## 2500001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec250-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "人類の革新には<line-break>太古の魔道技術が必要…か。<line-break>　<line-break>ふん、あのウジ虫らしいな、<line-break>そんなたわ言を吹きこむとは。<end>"
EN: "So Humanity's Evolution requires ancient magical technology... is that it? Hmph. Just like that maggot to put such nonsense into your head.<end>"
KO: "인류의 혁신에는 태고의 마도 기술이 필요하다…라. 흥, 그 구더기다운 소리군. 그런 헛소리를 불어넣다니.<end>"

## 2500002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec250-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>。よく聞いてくれ。<line-break>革新は人間自身の手で達成せねば<line-break>意味がないのだ。<line-break>革新そのものはもちろん重要だ。<line-break>しかし、それ以上に大事なことがある。<line-break><line-break>人間は、自分たちの力だけで<line-break>革新を成し遂げるだけの強さを<line-break>手に入れねばならないのだ。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, listen carefully. Humanity's Evolution means nothing unless human beings achieve it for themselves. Humanity's Evolution itself is important, of course. But something matters even more: humanity must gain the strength to achieve it through its own power alone.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>. 잘 들어 줘. 혁신은 인간 자신의 손으로 이루지 않으면 의미가 없다. 혁신 자체도 물론 중요하다. 하지만 그보다 더 중요한 게 있어. 인간은 자기 힘만으로 혁신을 이뤄낼 수 있을 만큼의 강함을 손에 넣어야 한다.<end>"

## 2500003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec250-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "仮に先人類の魔道技術や<line-break>人間ならざるものの力を借りて<line-break>革新を果たしたところで…<line-break>弱いままの人類にどうして<line-break>その先の未来が築けよう？<end>"
EN: "Even if humanity achieved Humanity's Evolution with the aid of its precursors' magical technology or the power of nonhuman beings, what future could a still-weak humanity build beyond that?<end>"
KO: "설령 선인류의 마도 기술이나 인간 아닌 존재의 힘을 빌려 혁신을 이룬다 해도… 약한 채인 인류가 어떻게 그 너머의 미래를 세울 수 있겠나?<end>"

## 2500004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec250-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今すぐ君に結論を出せとは言わない。<line-break><line-break><line-break>だが、私の説く人類の革新と<line-break>あのウジ虫の虚言…<line-break>どちらが正しいのか、よく考えてくれ。<end>"
EN: "I am not asking you to reach a conclusion right now. But think carefully about which is right: my vision of Humanity's Evolution, or that maggot's lies.<end>"
KO: "지금 당장 결론을 내리라는 건 아니다. 하지만 내가 말하는 인류의 혁신과 그 구더기의 허언… 어느 쪽이 옳은지 잘 생각해 봐라.<end>"

## 2500005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec250-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "申し訳ないが、予定がある。<line-break>今日はこれまでにしよう。<line-break>また来てくれ。<end>"
EN: "My apologies, but I have another engagement. Let us leave it there for today. Come and see me again.<end>"
KO: "미안하지만 일정이 있다. 오늘은 여기까지 하자. 또 와 줘.<end>"

## 2500006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec250-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "人類の革新には<line-break>太古の魔道技術が必要…か。<line-break>　<line-break>ふん、あのウジ虫らしいな、<line-break>そんなたわ言を吹きこむとは。<end>"
EN: "So Humanity's Evolution requires ancient magical technology... is that it? Hmph. Just like that maggot to put such nonsense into your head.<end>"
KO: "인류의 혁신에는 태고의 마도 기술이 필요하다…라. 흥, 그 구더기다운 소리군. 그런 헛소리를 불어넣다니.<end>"
