## 2500010
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec250-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "申し訳ないが、予定がある。<line-break>今日はこれまでにしよう。<line-break>また来てくれ。<end>"
EN: "My apologies, but I have another engagement. Let us leave it there for today. Come and see me again.<end>"
KO: "미안하지만 일정이 있다. 오늘은 여기까지 하자. 또 와 줘.<end>"

## 2500011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec250-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>…。<line-break>できれば心に留めておいてほしい。<line-break>　<line-break>もしも君が、<line-break>人類自身の手で革新が成されることに<line-break>意味があるのだと思えるのならば…<line-break>そして、ネメア様に<line-break>その可能性を託してくれるのならば、<line-break>私はできる限り、君を支援しよう。<line-break>だが、もしも君が<line-break>超文明の力を頼みとするのであれば、<line-break>いずれ敵に回ることになるだろう。<line-break>できれば、そうならないよう<line-break>願っている。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>... I hope you will keep this in mind. If you believe it matters for humanity to achieve Humanity's Evolution through human effort alone, and if you are willing to entrust that possibility to Nemea, I will support you as far as I am able. But if you choose to rely on the power of a supercivilization, we will eventually find ourselves on opposing sides. I hope it does not come to that.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>…. 가능하면 마음에 새겨 두었으면 한다. 네가 인류 자신의 손으로 혁신을 이루는 데 의미가 있다고 생각한다면… 그리고 네메아 님께 그 가능성을 맡겨 줄 생각이라면 나는 할 수 있는 한 너를 지원하겠다. 하지만 네가 초문명의 힘에 의지한다면 언젠가는 적으로 돌아서게 되겠지. 가능하면 그렇게 되지 않기를 바란다.<end>"

## 2500012
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec250-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "申し訳ないが、予定がある。<line-break>今日はこれまでにしよう。<line-break>また来てくれ。<end>"
EN: "My apologies, but I have another engagement. Let us leave it there for today. Come and see me again.<end>"
KO: "미안하지만 일정이 있다. 오늘은 여기까지 하자. 또 와 줘.<end>"

## 2500013
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec250-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>…。<line-break>できれば心に留めておいてほしい。<line-break>　<line-break>もしも君が、人類自身の手で革新が<line-break>成されることに意味があるのだと<line-break>思えるのなら、<line-break>そして、ネメア様にその可能性を<line-break>託してくれるのならば、<line-break>私はできる限り、君を支援しよう。<line-break>だが、もしも君が超文明の力を<line-break>頼みとするのであれば、<line-break>いずれ敵に回ることになるだろう。<line-break>できれば、そうならないよう<line-break>願っている。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>... I would ask you to keep this in mind. If you believe it matters that humanity bring about Humanity's Evolution through its own efforts, and if you are willing to entrust that possibility to Lord Nemea, I will do all I can to support you. But if you choose to rely on the power of a supercivilization, we will eventually find ourselves on opposing sides. I hope it does not come to that.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>…. 가능하면 마음에 새겨 두었으면 한다. 만약 네가 인류 자신의 손으로 혁신을 이루는 데 의미가 있다고 생각하고, 네메아 님에게 그 가능성을 맡겨 줄 수 있다면 나는 가능한 한 널 지원하겠다. 하지만 네가 초문명의 힘에 의지한다면 언젠가는 적으로 돌아서게 될 것이다. 가능하면 그렇게 되지 않기를 바란다.<end>"

## 2510001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec251-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフフ。よく来た<value:$28>。<line-break>どうやら決心がついたようだな。<line-break>　<line-break>さあ、余と共に新しき人類の<line-break>時代を歩むのだ。<line-break>　<line-break>この世の苦しみも悲しみも<line-break>一切が消え去った世界を<line-break><value:$28>に見せてやろう。<line-break>すべての人々が超人類となった<line-break>理想の世界…。<line-break>　<line-break>そこではもはや、汝が造られた存在で<line-break>あることを問う者はいまい。<line-break>　<line-break>さあ、今こそ時は満ちた。<line-break>余と共に来るがよい。<end>"
EN: "Heh heh heh. Welcome, <value:$28>. It seems you have made up your mind. Come, walk with me into the age of a new humanity. I shall show <value:$28> an ideal world where all people have become superhuman, a world from which every trace of suffering and sorrow has vanished... There, no one will question your nature as a created being. The time has come at last. Come with me.<end>"
KO: "후후후. 잘 왔다, <value:$28>. 아무래도 결심이 선 모양이군. 자, 짐과 함께 새로운 인류의 시대를 걷는 것이다. 이 세상의 고통도 슬픔도 모두 사라진 세계를 <value:$28>에게 보여 주마. 모든 사람들이 초인류가 된 이상적인 세계…. 그곳에서는 더 이상 네가 만들어진 존재라는 사실을 따지는 자도 없을 것이다. 자, 이제 때가 무르익었다. 짐과 함께 오너라.<end>"

## 2510002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec251-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そんなことはできない<end>"
EN: "I can't do that.<end>"
KO: "그럴 수는 없어.<end>"

## 2510003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec251-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…決心はついた<end>"
EN: "…I've made up my mind.<end>"
KO: "…결심했어<end>"

## 2510004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec251-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それが汝の答えか…。<line-break>…賢明な選択とは言えぬぞ。<line-break><value:$28>よ。<end>"
EN: "So that is your answer... I cannot call it a wise choice, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "그것이 그대의 답인가…. 현명한 선택이라 할 수 없군, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 2510005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec251-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…まあよい。<line-break>１０００年あまりの時も待ったのだ。<line-break>これが最後の機会でもあるまい。<line-break>いましばらく時を待とう…。<end>"
EN: "No matter. I have waited more than a thousand years. This will not be my last opportunity. I shall wait a little longer...<end>"
KO: "…뭐, 좋다. 천 년이 넘는 세월도 기다렸다. 이것이 마지막 기회라는 법도 없지. 조금 더 때를 기다리자….<end>"

## 2510006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec251-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "決心がついたか…。<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>心地よいぞ。その響き…。<line-break>我が息子ベルゼーヴァに失望して以来、<line-break>余が久しく待ち望んだ言葉だ。<end>"
EN: "So you have made your choice... How pleasing those words are. I have longed to hear them ever since my son Beelzeva disappointed me.<end>"
KO: "결심했나…. 듣기 좋군, 그 울림…. 내 아들 벨제바에게 실망한 이후 짐이 오래 기다려 온 말이다.<end>"

## 2510007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec251-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフ。そう焦ることはない。<line-break>まずは汝に<line-break>やってもらわねばならぬことがある。<line-break>この塔の最上階…<line-break>その奧には、マノンの腕輪という<line-break>強大な力を秘めた魔道器がある。<line-break>マノンは、かつて<line-break>この塔にいた魔道士の女だ。<line-break>　<line-break>そのマノンの力を封じた腕輪が<line-break>余の復活を阻んでおるのだ。<line-break>　<line-break>汝がこれに打ち勝つことができれば、<line-break>余は解放される、<line-break>汝もまた新たな力を得られよう。<line-break>今はまだ、マノンの腕輪は<line-break>深い眠りについているようだが、<line-break>余が起こしておこう。<line-break>必ずやマノンの腕輪を滅ぼし、<line-break>余を解き放つのだ。よいな。<end>"
EN: "Heh. There is no need for such haste. First, I have a task for you. At the very top of this tower, deep within, lies a magical artifact of immense power called Manon's Bracelet. Manon was a sorceress who once dwelled in this tower. The bracelet, which seals away Manon's power, is preventing my resurrection. If you can overcome it, I shall be freed, and you too shall gain new power. Manon's Bracelet still appears to lie in a deep sleep, but I shall awaken it. You will destroy Manon's Bracelet and set me free. Is that understood?<end>"
KO: "후후. 그리 서두를 것 없다. 먼저 네가 해 줘야 할 일이 있다. 이 탑 최상층… 그 안쪽에는 마논의 팔찌라는 강대한 힘을 품은 마도기가 있다. 마논은 과거 이 탑에 있던 여마도사다. 그 마논의 힘을 봉인한 팔찌가 짐의 부활을 막고 있다. 네가 이것을 이길 수 있다면 짐은 해방되고 너도 새로운 힘을 얻겠지. 지금은 아직 마논의 팔찌가 깊은 잠에 빠진 듯하지만 짐이 깨워 두마. 반드시 마논의 팔찌를 멸하고 짐을 해방하라. 알겠느냐.<end>"

## 2520001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec252-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おや、<value:$28>ですか。<line-break>いらっしゃい。<line-break>　<line-break>どうです。<line-break>記憶は戻りましたか？<end>"
EN: "Oh, <value:$28>. Welcome. Well? Has your memory returned?<end>"
KO: "어라, <value:$28>인가요. 어서 와요. 어때요? 기억은 돌아왔나요?<end>"

## 2520002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec252-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうですか…。<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break><value:$28>が目覚めたという<line-break>魔道の塔に行ってみてはどうですか？<line-break>何か思い出せるかもしれませんよ。<end>"
EN: "I see... Why not visit the Arcane Tower, where you awakened, <value:$28>? It may bring something back.<end>"
KO: "그렇군요…. <value:$28>이 깨어났다는 마도의 탑에 가 보는 건 어떨까요? 뭔가 기억날지도 몰라요.<end>"

## 2520003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec252-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…それに最近、<line-break>気がかりなことがありましてね。<end>"
EN: "There is also something that has been troubling me lately.<end>"
KO: "…그리고 요즘 신경 쓰이는 일이 하나 있어서요.<end>"

## 2520004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec252-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ええ。<line-break>どうも魔道の塔から<line-break>不穏な空気を感じるのです。<line-break>まだ漠然としたものですし、<line-break><value:$28>に直接、<line-break>関わりがあるかはわかりません。<line-break>ただ、調べてきてもらえると<line-break>私としてはありがたいですね。<line-break><line-break>もし、何事もなければ、<line-break>私の思い過ごしだと安心できますから。<end>"
EN: "Yes. I sense something ominous coming from the Arcane Tower. It is still only a vague feeling, and I do not know whether it has any direct connection to you, <value:$28>. Still, I would appreciate it if you went and looked into it. If nothing is amiss, I can rest assured that I was only worrying over nothing.<end>"
KO: "예. 아무래도 마도의 탑에서 불온한 기운이 느껴집니다. 아직 막연한 느낌이고 <value:$28>와 직접 관계가 있는지는 모르겠습니다. 다만 한번 조사해 주신다면 저로서는 고맙겠군요. 아무 일도 없다면 제 기우였다고 안심할 수 있을 테니까요.<end>"

## 2530001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec253-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　聞こえるか、<value:$28>よ。<end>"
EN: "Can you hear me, <value:$28>?<end>"
KO: "들리느냐, <value:$28>여.<end>"

## 2530006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec253-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だが、その前にひとつだけ<line-break><value:$28>に確かめておきたい。<line-break>　<line-break>君はシャロームに従うつもりなのか？<line-break>もしそうであれば、君とはここまでだ。<line-break>答えてくれ、<value:$28>？<end>"
EN: "But first, there is one thing I must ask you, <value:$28>. Do you intend to follow Shalom? If so, this is where we part ways. What is your answer, <value:$28>?<end>"
KO: "하지만 그 전에 하나만 <value:$28>에게 확인하고 싶어. 너는 샬롬을 따를 생각인가? 그렇다면 너와는 여기까지다. 대답해 줘, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 2530007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec253-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…あのウジ虫とは決別したわけか。<line-break>ならば話そう。<line-break>わずかに残された最後の希望を…。<end>"
EN: "So you have severed ties with that maggot. Then I shall tell you of our last, faint hope...<end>"
KO: "…그 구더기 같은 놈과 결별한 모양이군. 그렇다면 말해 주지. 얼마 남지 않은 마지막 희망을….<end>"

## 2530008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec253-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>よ…。<line-break>多少、時間はかかったが、<line-break>すべては余の計画通りになった。<line-break>いよいよだぞ。<line-break>我らが人類の革新に<line-break>本格的に乗り出すのは…。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>... It took some time, but everything has unfolded according to my plan. At last, the moment has come for us to pursue Humanity's Evolution in earnest...<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>…. 조금 시간은 걸렸지만 모든 것이 내 계획대로 되었다. 이제 시작이다. 우리 인류의 혁신에 본격적으로 나서는 것이….<end>"

## 2530009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec253-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さて、とりあえず余はディンガル軍に<line-break>戻り、宰相ベルゼーヴァとして<line-break>采配を振るとするか。<line-break>まずは全軍を魔道兵に仕立て、<line-break>戦力増強を図らねばならんからな。<line-break>フフフ…。<end>"
EN: "For now, I shall return to the Dyneskal Army and take command as Chancellor Beelzeva. First, I must turn the entire army into sorcerous troops and bolster our forces. Heh heh heh...<end>"
KO: "자, 우선 짐은 딘갈군으로 돌아가 재상 벨제바로서 지휘를 맡도록 하지. 먼저 전군을 마도병으로 만들어 전력을 강화해야 하니까. 후후후….<end>"

## 2540001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec254-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "町を見てきてくれたか、<line-break><value:$28>？<line-break>この１年でだいぶ復興しただろう。<end>"
EN: "Have you been to see the town, <value:$28>? It's come a long way in the past year, hasn't it?<end>"
KO: "마을을 보고 왔나, <value:$28>? 이 1년 동안 꽤 복구됐지.<end>"

## 2540002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec254-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "すべては君のおかげだ。<line-break>竜王を倒し、<line-break>この世界を解放してくれたのだから…。<end>"
EN: "It's all thanks to you. You defeated the Dragon King and set this world free.<end>"
KO: "모든 게 네 덕분이다. 용왕을 쓰러뜨리고 이 세계를 해방해 줬으니까….<end>"

## 2540003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec254-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "愉快だとは思わないか？<line-break>いかに吟遊の名手、リルビーでも<line-break>思いつくまい。<line-break>人類の革新に命をかけ、運命と闘い、<line-break>世界の未来を勝ち取った者が<line-break>　<line-break>みずからの驕りと、魔道技術への<line-break>過信ゆえに滅んだ愚かな王によって<line-break>造られた人間であることを…。<end>"
EN: "Don't you find it amusing? Not even a minstrel as gifted as Lilubee could have dreamed it up. The one who put life itself on the line for Humanity's Evolution, fought against fate, and won the world its future was a human created by a foolish king who perished through his own arrogance and overconfidence in magical technology.<end>"
KO: "우습지 않나? 아무리 음유의 명수 릴비라도 생각해 내진 못했을 거다. 인류의 혁신에 목숨을 걸고 운명과 싸워 세계의 미래를 쟁취한 자가, 자기 오만과 마도기술에 대한 과신 때문에 멸망한 어리석은 왕이 만든 인간이라니….<end>"

## 2540004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec254-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "結局、いかなる運命の力でさえ<line-break>人の意志を支配することは<line-break>できないということか…。<line-break>君は証明してくれた、<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>すべては人の生まれによって<line-break>決められるのではなく、その後の<line-break>生き方によって、定まるのだと。<line-break><value:$28>は人々の希望だよ。<line-break>人類の可能性を見つめ、未来を<line-break>切り開いていくための道しるべ…。<end>"
EN: "Perhaps this means that, in the end, no power of fate can rule the human will. You proved that birth does not decide everything. How one lives afterward does. <value:$28> is the hope of the people, a beacon as we look to humanity's potential and forge a path into the future.<end>"
KO: "결국 어떤 운명의 힘도 인간의 의지를 지배할 수는 없다는 건가…. 네가 증명해 줬어. 모든 것은 태어날 때 정해지는 게 아니라 그 뒤 어떻게 살아가느냐에 따라 정해진다는 것을. <value:$28>는 사람들의 희망이야. 인류의 가능성을 바라보며 미래를 열어 가기 위한 이정표….<end>"

## 2540005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec254-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今にして思えば、君こそが<line-break>人類の革新を担うに<line-break>最もふさわしい存在だったのだろう。<line-break>人類の革新とは、<line-break>人の意志をもって、人自身の力で<line-break>進化していくことなのだから…。<line-break>君と共に成し遂げたことを<line-break>私は誇りに思う。<end>"
EN: "Looking back now, perhaps you were the one best suited to bring about Humanity's Evolution. For Humanity's Evolution means humankind evolving by its own will and its own power. I am proud of what we accomplished together.<end>"
KO: "지금 와 생각해 보면 바로 네가 인류의 혁신을 맡기에 가장 어울리는 존재였던 것 같다. 인류의 혁신이란 인간의 의지로, 인간 자신의 힘으로 진화해 가는 것이니까…. 너와 함께 이룬 일을 나는 자랑스럽게 생각한다.<end>"

## 2540006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec254-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ありがとう。<value:$28>…。<end>"
EN: "Thank you, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "고마워. <value:$28>….<end>"

## 2550001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec255-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なんなのよ、この部屋っ！<line-break>汚い本ばっかり！<line-break>カビ臭くて息がつまるわ！<end>"
EN: "What is this place?! Nothing but filthy books! It reeks of mold in here! I can barely breathe!<end>"
KO: "뭐야, 이 방은! 더러운 책뿐이잖아! 곰팡내가 나서 숨이 막혀!<end>"

## 2550002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec255-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ロジクーニン？　誰よ、それ？<line-break>どう見たって高貴なアタクシが<line-break>読む価値はなさそうね。<end>"
EN: "Logikunin? Who's that? It certainly doesn't look worth reading to someone as noble as I am.<end>"
KO: "로지쿠닌? 누구야, 그게? 아무리 봐도 고귀한 내가 읽을 가치는 없어 보이는데.<end>"

## 2550003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec255-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…え！？　これって、<line-break>もしかして、あのロジクーニンが<line-break>書き残した本じゃ…。<end>"
EN: "Wait, what?! Could this actually be a book Logikunin left behind...?<end>"
KO: "…어!? 이거 혹시 그 로지쿠닌이 남긴 책 아니야….<end>"

## 2550004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec255-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…間違いないわ！<line-break>すごーい！　世紀の大発見！<line-break>　<line-break>異世界について書かれた<line-break>超一級の資料よ！<end>"
EN: "There's no doubt about it! Amazing! This is the discovery of the century! It's a first-rate source on other worlds!<end>"
KO: "…틀림없어! 대단해! 세기의 대발견이야! 이세계에 대해 적힌 최고급 자료라고!<end>"

## 2550005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec255-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふーん…。この世界の他にも<line-break>別の世界があるなんて<line-break>なんだか素敵かも…！<line-break>ある日、もうひとつの世界から。<line-break>魅力的な王子様が現れて、私のこと<line-break>お迎えに来てくれたりして…。キャッ！<end>"
EN: "Hmm... Maybe it is rather romantic, knowing there are other worlds beyond this one. Perhaps one day a charming prince will appear from another world and come to sweep me away... Eek!<end>"
KO: "흐음…. 이 세계 말고도 다른 세계가 있다니 뭔가 멋질지도…! 어느 날 다른 세계에서 매력적인 왕자님이 나타나 나를 데리러 와 주고…. 꺅!<end>"

## 2550006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec255-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "バカね。バカすぎ…。<line-break>果てしなく下等だわ。<end>"
EN: "What an idiot. Too stupid for words... Utterly inferior.<end>"
KO: "바보네. 너무 바보야…. 끝도 없이 저질이야.<end>"

## 2550007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec255-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よーし、決めたわ！<line-break>ロジクーニンの研究を引き継いで、<line-break>この私が完成させちゃおっと！<line-break>そして、その研究成果を引っさげて<line-break>魔道アカデミーへ復学よ！<end>"
EN: "All right, I've decided! I'll carry on Logikunin's research and finish it myself! Then I'll take the results back to the Magic Academy and get reinstated!<end>"
KO: "좋아, 결정했어! 로지쿠닌의 연구를 이어받아 내가 완성해 버릴 거야! 그리고 그 연구 성과를 들고 마도 아카데미에 복학하는 거지!<end>"

## 2550008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec255-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アカデミーへ復学ですって？<line-break>下等生物のいかにも小市民的な願いね。<line-break>発想が貧困すぎて笑っちゃうわ！<end>"
EN: "Reinstated at the Academy? How typically petty-bourgeois of an inferior creature. Such poverty of imagination is laughable!<end>"
KO: "아카데미에 복학한다고? 하등생물답게 너무나 소시민적인 소원이네. 발상이 빈곤해서 웃음이 나와!<end>"

## 2550010
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec255-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "きゃっ！<line-break>これもロジクーニンの論文かも…！<line-break>　<line-break>…空間に穴を開けて、<line-break>異世界とつなげる方法！？<end>"
EN: "Eek! Could this be another of Logikunin's papers...? A method for opening a hole in space and linking it to another world?!<end>"
KO: "꺅! 이것도 로지쿠닌의 논문일지도 몰라…! …공간에 구멍을 열어 이세계와 연결하는 방법!?<end>"

## 2550011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec255-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "すばらしいわ！<line-break>この技術を完成させれば<line-break>　<line-break>魔道アカデミーに復学どころか<line-break>首席にだってなれるかも！<end>"
EN: "Wonderful! If I perfect this technique, I might not just get reinstated at the Magic Academy. I could even take the top spot!<end>"
KO: "멋지다! 이 기술을 완성하면 마도 아카데미 복학은커녕 수석도 될 수 있을지 몰라!<end>"

## 2550012
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec255-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ね、お願い！　<value:$28>。<line-break>本によると、この塔の最上階で<line-break>実験できるみたいなんだけど、<line-break>でも、空間に穴を開けるためには<line-break>千年石と、ハヤスの葉が必要なの。<line-break>もし、見つけたらよろしくね！<end>"
EN: "Please, <value:$28>! The book says the experiment can be done on the top floor of this tower. But opening a hole in space requires a Millennium Stone and a Hayasu Leaf. If you find either one, I'm counting on you!<end>"
KO: "있지, 부탁이야! <value:$28>. 책에 따르면 이 탑 최상층에서 실험할 수 있는 것 같은데, 공간에 구멍을 내려면 천년석과 하야스의 잎이 필요해. 혹시 찾으면 부탁할게!<end>"

## 2550013
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec255-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ああ、それと傲慢の首飾りって<line-break>闇の神器も必要だわ。<line-break>実験の時に使わせてちょうだいね！<end>"
EN: "Oh, and we'll also need the Dark Artifact called the Necklace of Pride. Let me use it for the experiment, all right?<end>"
KO: "아, 그리고 오만의 목걸이라는 어둠의 신기도 필요해. 실험할 때 쓰게 해 줘!<end>"

## 2550014
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec255-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ちょっと待ちなさいよぉ！<line-break>あんなバカバカしい戯言を信じて<line-break>本気で協力するんじゃないでしょうね！<end>"
EN: "Wait just a minute! You're not actually going to help with this because you believe all that ridiculous nonsense, are you?!<end>"
KO: "잠깐 기다려! 저런 터무니없는 헛소리를 믿고 진짜로 협력하려는 건 아니겠지!<end>"

## 2560001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec256-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さぁて、いよいよ世紀の実験の<line-break>始まりよ。<end>"
EN: "All right, the experiment of the century is about to begin!<end>"
KO: "자, 드디어 세기의 실험이 시작된다.<end>"

## 2560002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec256-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "じゃあ、<value:$28><line-break>さっそく手伝って。<line-break>ここに首飾りをかざしてほしいの。<end>"
EN: "All right, <value:$28>, give me a hand. Hold the necklace up right here for me.<end>"
KO: "그럼 <value:$28>, 바로 도와줘. 여기에 목걸이를 대 줬으면 해.<end>"

## 2560003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec256-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あらあら…。本当にやってるのね。<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>下等生物の下等な実験なんて<line-break>アタクシこれっぽっちも興味ないし。<end>"
EN: "Oh, my... You really are going through with it. Not that I have the slightest interest in some pathetic experiment conducted by inferior life-forms.<end>"
KO: "어머나…. 정말 하고 있네. 하등생물의 하찮은 실험 따위, 난 조금도 관심 없지만.<end>"

## 2560004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec256-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "つまらない実験で不様に失敗する<line-break>下等生物たちの姿を見物しにきて<line-break>あげたのよぉ！<line-break>この寛大なアタクシに<line-break>心から感謝することね。<end>"
EN: "I simply graced you with my presence to watch a pack of inferior life-forms fail miserably at their dreary little experiment! You should be thanking me from the bottom of your hearts for my generosity.<end>"
KO: "시시한 실험에서 꼴사납게 실패하는 하등생물들을 구경하러 와준 거야! 이 관대한 나에게 진심으로 감사하도록 해.<end>"

## 2560005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec256-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "その角度で大丈夫よ。<line-break><value:$28>。<line-break>えーと、それから次は…。<end>"
EN: "That angle is fine, <value:$28>. Now, let's see... What comes next?<end>"
KO: "그 각도면 괜찮아, <value:$28>. 음, 그리고 다음은….<end>"

## 2560006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec256-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…って、ちょっとぉ！<line-break>アタクシを無視して実験を<line-break>進めないでちょうだい！<end>"
EN: "H-Hey, wait! Don't just ignore me and carry on with the experiment!<end>"
KO: "…잠깐만요! 나를 무시하고 실험을 진행하지 말아 줘요!<end>"

## 2560007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec256-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…な、何なの！？<end>"
EN: "W-What is it?!<end>"
KO: "…뭐, 뭐야!?<end>"

## 2560008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec256-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "きゃっ！　やったわ！<line-break>実験大成功！<end>"
EN: "Eee! It worked! The experiment was a complete success!<end>"
KO: "꺄! 해냈다! 실험 대성공!<end>"

## 2560009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec256-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "こ、これのどこが成功なのよォ！？<line-break>下等生物が、さらに下等な生物を<line-break>呼び出しただけでしょうが！<end>"
EN: "H-How is this a success?! You inferior life-form just summoned one even lower than yourself!<end>"
KO: "이, 이게 어디가 성공이라는 거야!? 하등생물이 더 하등한 생물을 불러냈을 뿐이잖아!<end>"

## 2560010
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec256-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ううん！　実験は大成功！<line-break>ちゃんとこの世界と異世界を<line-break>次元の穴でつないだんだもの！<line-break>ふふっ。教授たちの驚く顔が<line-break>目に浮かんじゃう。<line-break>私ってやっぱ天才かも、きゃっ！<end>"
EN: "No, the experiment was a complete success! It connected this world to another through a dimensional rift! Hee hee. I can already picture the professors' faces. Maybe I really am a genius. Eee!<end>"
KO: "아니! 실험은 대성공이야! 제대로 이 세계와 이세계를 차원의 구멍으로 연결했잖아! 후후. 교수들이 놀랄 얼굴이 눈에 선해. 나 역시 천재인가 봐, 꺅!<end>"

## 2560011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec256-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…と、とにかく！<line-break>高貴なアタクシをこんな目に遭わせて<line-break>許されると思ってるのー！？<line-break>ほらっ、さっさと何とかしなさいよぉ！<end>"
EN: "A-Anyway! Do you think you can get away with subjecting someone as noble as me to this?! Come on, do something about it at once!<end>"
KO: "…어, 어쨌든! 고귀한 나를 이런 꼴로 만들고도 용서받을 줄 알아!? 자, 빨리 어떻게든 해 봐!<end>"

## 2560012
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec256-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うーん、どうしようかな…。<line-break>まだ成功の余韻にひたっていたいけど…。<line-break>でも、後片づけはしなくっちゃね。<end>"
EN: "Hmm, what should I do...? I'd like to bask in my success a little longer... but I suppose I have to clean up afterward.<end>"
KO: "으음, 어떡하지…. 아직 성공의 여운에 잠겨 있고 싶은데…. 그래도 뒷정리는 해야겠지.<end>"

## 2560013
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec256-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さあ、元の世界にお帰りなさい！<end>"
EN: "Now, off you go! Back to your own world!<end>"
KO: "자, 원래 세계로 돌아가세요!<end>"

## 2560014
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec256-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…あ、あれ？<line-break>おかしいな、戻らない…。<end>"
EN: "H-Huh? That's strange. It's not going back...<end>"
KO: "…어, 어라? 이상하네, 돌아오지 않아….<end>"

## 2560015
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec256-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ちょ、ちょっと…。<line-break>…ど、どうなってるのよぉ！？<line-break>早く何とかしなさいってば！！<end>"
EN: "W-Wait... What's going on?! Hurry up and do something!<end>"
KO: "자, 잠깐…. …어, 어떻게 된 거야!? 빨리 어떻게 좀 해!!<end>"

## 2560016
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec256-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まあ、魔道の発展には<line-break>多少の犠牲はつきものかも。<line-break>きゃっ！<end>"
EN: "Well, perhaps some sacrifice is inevitable in the advancement of the magical arts. Eek!<end>"
KO: "뭐, 마도의 발전에는 어느 정도 희생이 따르는 법일지도. 꺅!<end>"

## 2560017
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec256-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…このアタクシを下等生物の爆発に<line-break>巻き込むなんて……許せないわ！<end>"
EN: "How dare you get me caught in an inferior life-form's explosion... This is unforgivable!<end>"
KO: "…이 나를 하등생물의 폭발에 휘말리게 하다니…… 용서 못 해!<end>"

## 2560018
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec256-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ご、ごめんなさーい…。<end>"
EN: "I-I'm so sorry...<end>"
KO: "미, 미안해요….<end>"

## 2560019
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec256-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だいたい、あんたはいつも…。<line-break>………………。<end>"
EN: "Honestly, you're always...<end>"
KO: "대체 당신은 언제나…. ……………….<end>"

## 2560020
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec256-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>は気絶したフェティを<line-break>宿屋まで担いで帰った。<line-break><value:$28>のＳＴＲが１上がった。<line-break>ユーリスは高度な実験に成功した。<line-break>ユーリスのＩＮＴが１上がった。<line-break>ユーリスのＭＩＮが１上がった。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28> carried the unconscious Fety back to the inn. <value:$28>'s ＳＴＲ increased by 1. Julls succeeded in conducting an advanced experiment. Julls's ＩＮＴ increased by 1. Julls's ＭＩＮ increased by 1.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>는 기절한 페티를 업고 여관까지 돌아왔다. <value:$28>의 STR이 1 올랐다. 유리스는 고도의 실험에 성공했다. 유리스의 INT가 1 올랐다. 유리스의 MIN이 1 올랐다.<end>"

## 2560021
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec256-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あれだけ誘ったのに<line-break>誰も来ないなんて…。<line-break>　<line-break>一生に一度見られるかどうかの<line-break>歴史的な瞬間なのに…。<end>"
EN: "After I tried so hard to get people to come, not one person showed up... This may be the only chance in a lifetime to witness a historic moment like this...<end>"
KO: "그렇게 불렀는데 아무도 안 오다니…. 평생 한 번 볼까 말까 한 역사적인 순간인데….<end>"

## 2560022
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec256-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "来るわけないでしょ！<line-break>まったく、バカバカしいにも<line-break>ほどがあるわ…。<line-break>アタクシ仕方なくついて来ただけよ。<line-break>せいぜい感謝することね。<end>"
EN: "Of course no one came! The whole thing is too absurd for words... I only tagged along because I had no choice. You should be grateful.<end>"
KO: "올 리 없잖아! 정말 바보 같은 것도 정도가 있지…. 난 어쩔 수 없이 따라온 것뿐이야. 고맙게 생각하라고.<end>"
